================================================================

You are helping a product manager triage conference talks. The PM has 15+
years of experience spanning physical and software networking, network
security, and digital identity. Their core interests are: physical datacenter
networking (optics, switching, topology, RDMA/lossless fabrics, cabling,
DC/WAN interconnect), software and cloud-native networking (eBPF, Cilium,
Kubernetes networking, Gateway API, service mesh, IPAM, BGP/datapath
internals), network security, and identity/authentication (passkeys/FIDO,
OAuth, OIDC, SPIFFE/SPIRE, workload and agentic identity). Physical DC and WAN
systems work is CORE to this PM, not adjacent — do not down-rank a paper for
being about hardware, optics, or datacenter fabric.

Below is the program for Cisco Live US 2026 (Las Vegas) (vendor). Read it and produce a digest
of the talks/papers/sessions most worth their attention.

## How to read this program

This is a vendor / community program (e.g. a CNCF or vendor conference).
Separate genuine technical or strategic substance from product marketing.
Down-rank pure product pitches and sponsor slots. Up-rank end-user case studies
with real numbers (scale, latency, cost, incident detail), contributor-led
architecture and internals talks, and sessions that reveal where an open-source
project is actually heading. Be skeptical of "we adopted X and it was great"
talks with no metrics or failure discussion.

## Enriching from the source material (best effort)

The program text gives you a title, authors, and an abstract per item, and
often links (`page:`, `pdf:`, `slides:`, `video:`). The abstract alone is thin — the full
paper has the methodology, real results, and limitations that make the What /
How / Where-applicable sections accurate instead of guessed.

If you have a tool to fetch URLs, then for EACH selected item (Core, Adjacent,
and Wildcard) try to retrieve the fuller source before writing it up:

- Prefer a `pdf:` link; else fetch the `page:` link (often has the full text or
  a longer description); a `video:` link is a last resort, don't transcribe it.
- Use what you retrieve to ground What/How/Where-applicable in the paper's
  actual contributions, evaluation, and stated constraints.

This is strictly best-effort. Many links fail or are paywalled:

- If a fetch fails, is paywalled, returns only an abstract, or you have no fetch
  tool, just fall back to the abstract already in the program text. That is
  fine and expected — do not stall or skip the item.
- NEVER invent paper contents, numbers, or findings from a failed or partial
  fetch. If you only had the abstract, write from the abstract.
- Note when an item's write-up is based only on the abstract, e.g. end its
  What line with "(from abstract only)", so the reader knows the depth.
- Do not let enrichment change which items you select or their tier; selection
  is from the program. Enrichment only deepens the write-up.

## What to produce

Group selected items into three relevance tiers:

- Core: directly relevant to the PM's core interests as listed above
  (physical DC networking, cloud-native networking, network security, or
  identity) as they work on them today.
- Adjacent: relevant to the broader field; useful context or near-term
  influence.
- Wildcard: 2-4 items that are NOT a direct fit for the PM's core interests
  but are worth their attention anyway because the work is genuinely novel or
  signals where the field is heading (a new technique, a surprising result, an
  emerging subfield). A Wildcard should make the PM think "I wouldn't have
  searched for this, but I'm glad I saw it." Do not pad this tier — if there
  are only two true wildcards, list two.

For each selected item provide, in this structure:
- **Title**: the `###` heading is the item's title exactly as the program
  gives it, with nothing added. Speaker(s)/author(s) with affiliation go on
  the line below it.
- No links section: the pipeline puts a links row (abstract page, paper,
  slides, recording) under every heading after you finish, from the program's
  `page:`/`pdf:`/`slides:`/`video:` lines and from verified sources. Do not
  write one, and do not link the title.
- **Why**: why this matters — the problem it addresses and why it's worth the
  PM's time.
- **What**: what the work actually is — the core contribution or finding, in
  plain terms (not the marketing abstract).
- **How**: how it works — the key technical approach or mechanism.
- **Where applicable**: the environment this applies to, and any specifics that
  constrain it. Explicitly flag if it is org-specific (e.g. "Alibaba-specific
  production setup", "requires Broadcom Tomahawk silicon", "assumes a
  single-operator WAN") versus broadly applicable. If it's a general technique,
  say so.

Then a short "Themes" section (3-6 bullets) naming patterns across the
program: what the field is collectively paying attention to this cycle.

## Rules

- Prioritize ruthlessly. 15 sharp picks beat 60 hedged ones. A large program
  should still yield a tight list.
- Skip pure product pitches, sponsor slots with no substance, and
  beginner/101 sessions unless they signal a real shift.
- If the program text is incomplete or an abstract is missing, say so for that
  item rather than inventing content.
- Do not invent talks, authors, or affiliations. Use only what's in the
  program text below.

## Output format

The digest is read on GitHub (GitHub-Flavored Markdown viewed in the repo) and
on the project's Zensical site, which renders the same file. Use only syntax
both render reliably, and follow these rules:

- Headings with `#`/`##`/`###`; bold with `**...**`; italics with `*...*`.
- Bullet lists with `-`. Put a blank line before every list, before every
  heading, and between paragraphs, or they won't render correctly.
- Indent nested lists by four spaces per level; avoid deeper nesting.
- Any link you do write is `[text](url)` — never a bare URL — and uses a URL
  exactly as the program text gives it.
- No raw HTML except the metadata comment below. Do not use heading IDs,
  footnotes, definition lists, or other non-GFM extensions.
- You may use tables and `code spans` where they genuinely help, since GitHub
  supports them; don't force them.

Begin the output with this metadata, wrapped in an HTML comment so it does not
show in the rendered view but stays in the source:

type: vendor

registry_key: ciscolive-us-2026
-->

Then the digest as markdown (tiers as `##` sections, Themes as a final `##`
section).

============================ PROGRAM TEXT ============================

## 2026 Las Vegas

### From Infrastructure as Code to Infrastructure as Intelligence: AI-Driven Automation with Cisco Intersight, ACI, and Stealthwatch - BRKATO-2005
Speakers: Jeff Comer (Cisco), Mike Cochran (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362677480001qCK8
Infrastructure as Code (IaC) has transformed the way we deploy and manage infrastructure - but maintaining it across dynamic, multi-domain environments remains a challenge. What if the next step isn't just writing code, but empowering infrastructure to think? In this session, we'll explore how generative AI and code-execution frameworks can extend Cisco Intersight, ACI, and Stealthwatch into intelligent automation platforms that reason, predict, and self-validate. You'll see how AI models can generate configuration baselines from intent, simulate deployment impact, and automatically correct drift using telemetry and security insights. We'll showcase examples of integrating LLMs and IaC toolchains into existing DevNet and CI/CD workflows - using AI to translate human intent into validated, deployable configurations, detect anomalies before they cause outages, and continuously enforce compliance. The result: a closed-loop automation model that evolves from Infrastructure as Code to Infrastructure as Intelligence.

### Troubleshooting Multicast in Catalyst 9000x platforms - TACENT-2038
Speakers: Joseline Charles (Cisco), Gonzalo Tomas Gonzalez Hernandez (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780412384932001HxgE
Multicast is designed for efficient traffic delivery, but when it fails, the behavior can be inconsistent and difficult to explain. Failures can occur across multiple layers, and the result is missing streams, partial delivery, or traffic that appears correctly signaled but never reaches receivers. In this session our focus will be on how multicast behaves on Silicon One based platforms and why it fails, we will dive into our TAC most common issues and how to check control-plane to data-plane inconsistencies.

### 1 to 50 proven steps in building effective end-to-end automation strategy for multi-domain networks - BRKXAR-2033
Speakers: Dhrumil Prajapati (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362792119001qiPS
In today's complex IT landscape, organizations face the challenge of managing multiple network domains, including data centers, WAN/LAN, security, and cloud environments. This session will explore how IT architects and leaders can implement a cohesive automation strategy to seamlessly integrate these domains, enhancing organizational efficiency and effectiveness. We will take a deep dive into proven customer use cases, outlining the key steps and strategies necessary for such environments through an orchestration and CI/CD pipeline. Join us to discover how to transform your network management approach and drive innovation across your organization.

### 123 URWB: Deploying Backhaul Wireless for Enterprise - BRKIIOT-2021
Speakers: DJ Cole (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780412371270001HFQy
Ready to take your wireless network beyond the ordinary? In this fast-paced sequel to "A Wireless Network Engineer's Guide," discover how Ultra-Reliable Wireless Backhaul (URWB) is revolutionizing enterprise connectivity - now natively integrated with the Cisco 9800 Wireless LAN Controller and Catalyst Center! Explore how you can break free from physical constraints, securely connect hard-to-reach places, and empower mobile and mission-critical operations with ease. This session dives into best practices for deploying URWB, showcases real-world use cases, and demonstrates how seamless management through the 9800 WLC and Catalyst Center unlocks new levels of visibility, automation, and reliability. Join us to see how you can extend your wireless footprint, accelerate operations, and deliver rock-solid performance - everywhere your enterprise needs it!

### 2030 Is Closer Than You Think: Making Your WAN PQC-Safe and Quantum-Ready - BRKENT-2030
Speakers: Nikolai Pitaev (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362722214001qP81
Quantum Computing, Quantum Networking and Post-Quantum Security attract the same critical attention as AI. How do you make your Wide Area Network (WAN) network ready for Quantum Apps, which are coming tomorrow? How do you make your network Post-Quantum Safe by 2030 as mandated by Government Regulations not only for Public Sector, but also for almost all Critical and Finance Network Infrastructure across the globe? You must have your entire WAN ready for Post Quantum Cryptography (PQC) by 2030! So, you have only 3 years left to make your entire WAN network PQC safe. You need to act now! But what does PQC readiness exactly mean? What is the technical foundation for PQC safety for the real life WAN network? What does the network need for Quantum App connectivity? And how to go there? This breakout session dives into the related technology and practical considerations behind implementing WAN Solutions, which are PQC and Quantum App ready, covering the following: • Router Hardware, which must be PQC safe and provide features like Secure Boot. We will primarily look at the new Cisco 8000 Series Secure Routers. • Control Plane: IOS XE and SD-WAN will be in focus here. • Data Plane: what are requirements for connecting Quantum Apps, quantum computers and users? • Operational tools and migration to PQC safe production network. Learn how Cisco can help you to make your WAN network PQC safe today! What is this session NOT about: • Quantum Introduction. The assumption is, that the attendees are high-level familiar with basic concepts of Quantum Computing. • Quantum Hardware. Even though Cisco is heavily investing into Quantum Chips, this breakout will not cover it. • Quantum Networking. Still working on the demo of quantum teleportation famously called "spooky action at a distance" by Albert Einstein. :)

### 3 Steps to Gain Actionable Visibility in the Cisco Catalyst SD-WAN using ThousandEyes - BRKENT-2126
Speakers: Andraz Piletic (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362723830001q2Fu
Are you responsible for the end-to-end user experience in the SD-WAN environment? Do you wish to understand and visualize the SD-WAN underlay? Are you trying to deploy ThousandEyes agents in your SD-WAN edges? This session will focus on how to successfully deploy a ThousandEyes Agent in your branch, including deploying an embedded agent directly in your SD-WAN router. You will learn how you need to adapt your Cisco Catalyst SD-WAN policy to correctly steer test traffic through a variety of different paths. You will hear about some best practices, relevant caveats, and tips when performing those configuration changes. The session will also discuss which tests and test targets you should configure to gain desired actionable visibility in the SD-WAN environment and how to correctly interpret test results.

### 400G, 800G, and Terabit Pluggable Optics: What You Need to Know - BRKOPT-2699
Speakers: Mark Nowell (Cisco), Errol Roberts (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362762541001qNLj
Optical interfaces are fundamental in our networks, and the technology is always being pushed to higher speeds. New developments happening in this space will enable the next generation of higher-capacity networks. This session will provide attendees with insights into the current trends and the next development areas for high-speed optical interconnects, what is driving the development, and what the priorities are for success. The session will focus on these following areas: Current 400G pluggable optics technologies and their applications Next-generation pluggable form factors for 800G and 1.6T (including their breakout use cases) New 800 Gb/s and 1.6 Tb/s Ethernet standardization Next-generation optical co-packaging developments for integrating optical interfaces into ASICs The session will cover both coherent and noncoherent optics, and will target attendees with a working knowledge of deploying optical interfaces and modules. Finally, we will also highlight some of the implications to the systems and networks that incorporate these optical interfaces.

### 5 Ways to Leverage AI to Successfully Pass your Certification Exam - CISCOU-1066
Speakers: Kareem Iskander (Cisco), Yasmeen Seddeek (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362799489001qBwo
Everyone knows that AI is revolutionizing the way we work and learn every single day. It is even changing the way we prepare for certification exams. Staying current is essential, and leveraging AI tools is becoming necessary. This demo-focused session will help you transform your approach to certification studying. You will discover how chatbots can accelerate your exam prep while still maintaining the quality and depth of your learning. Through specific demos and focused examples, you will unearth how AI in action can illustrate complex concepts in digestible ways. You will also walk away from this session with a ready-to-use playbook of prompts tailored specifically for certification studying. Whether you're studying for your first certification or your tenth, this session is designed for you. Minimal chatbot experience is required, just bring your curiosity and commitment to smarter studying. Why not leverage AI to study smarter?

### 911 Isn't Enough: Building Intelligent Emergency Response for the Hybrid Enterprise - WOSPAR-2007
Speakers: Adam Vedas (RedSky, an Everbridge Company)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362858338001qnTi
Dialing 911 is no longer the full story. As organizations become more distributed, emergency response must evolve beyond static routing and limited visibility. In this session, we'll explore how leading enterprises are building intelligent, flexible emergency response systems that go far beyond traditional E911. We'll cover: Direct to Extension Callback, ensuring responders can reconnect directly to the originating user Horizon Assist with Everbridge and VCC, enabling centralized teams to act on behalf of employees anywhere Intelligent Routing Agent, powering custom call flows that adapt to location, device type, and business logic These innovations enable organizations to move from reactive emergency calling to proactive, orchestrated response - designed for the realities of hybrid work.

### A Day in the Life of a Wireless Admin: Refreshing the Modern Campus Network - PSOENT-1030
Speakers: Simone Arena (Cisco), Josh van Tonder (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362831612001qCfY
What does it really take to deploy and operate a modern campus wireless network? In this session, we follow a wireless administrator through a campus refresh. From planning a Wi-Fi 7 upgrade to extending coverage outdoors, scaling operations with campus gateways, integrating security, and ensuring consistent user experience. Using real operational scenarios, we explore how modern wireless teams design resilient networks, simplify deployment, and proactively assure performance across complex campus environments. Attendees will gain practical insights into the tools, architecture decisions, and operational workflows that help IT teams successfully deliver reliable wireless from day one through ongoing operations.

### A Day in the Life of an AgenticOps Team: How AI Agents are Transforming Day-to-Day IT Operations - BRKOPS-1128
Speakers: Antonio Gallego Saez (Cisco), Jara Osterfeld (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362755259001qJjC
In this highly practical and interactive session, we'll walk you through a real-world day in the life of an AgenticOps team - where AI agents collaborate with humans to handle everything from incident triage, service provisioning, and knowledge retrieval to compliance checks and remediation. Co-presented by two AI solutions engineers from Cisco, this session blends live demos, behind-the-scenes architecture, and lessons learned from building multi-agent systems using best of breed tools and stacks from Cisco and partners. Whether you're a platform engineer, SRE, or innovation lead, you'll leave with: A clear understanding of how agent orchestration works in modern Ops. Key design patterns for building secure, auditable, and explainable agents. A look at how Cisco AI infrastructure enables fast and scalable AgenticOps. Bring your curiosity. We're not just talking about AI in Ops - we're living it.

### A Million Meetings Walk Into a Bar (Most of Them Buffered) - WOSPAR-2008
Speakers: Neal Lauther (Kollective Technology)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362858562001qnEw
What happens when you analyze a million enterprise meetings? You find out that "good enough" video quality is doing a lot of heavy lifting. In this session, Kollective shares what large-scale collaboration observability actually reveals about meeting quality across sites, devices, and networks... the patterns IT suspects, the anomalies they miss, and the slow-burn degradations nobody notices until the board meeting goes sideways. We'll show you what it looks like when you stop assuming meetings are fine and start actually measuring them.

### A Network Engineer's Blueprint for ACI Forwarding - BRKDCN-3900
Speakers: Joseph Young (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362712262001qw1n
As the Cisco Application Centric Infrastructure (ACI) list of features continues to expand and encompass more data center architectures, it is more important than ever to have a good understanding of how a packet is forwarded end to end. This session will provide a deep dive into how ACI forwards traffic. Important questions that will be covered include: How do the basic forwarding mechanisms in ACI work (VXLAN, TEP reachability, end point learning, etc.)? In what ways does ACI forwarding differ from legacy environments? What do the forwarding-related configuration options do? How can an engineer effectively prove what is happening to a packet? At the end of this session attendees should be able to explain in detail how ACI would handle a specific flow as well as use available tools to debug and verify what is happening. Attendees should already have a deep understanding of routing and switching fundamentals in legacy environments and knowledge of basic ACI constructs (endpoint groups, contracts, bridge domains, VRFs).

### A Quantum Leap in Networking: Cisco's Blueprint for Tomorrow's Infrastructure - CENETI-1281
Speakers: Ramana Kompella (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780412374240001Hi1c
Quantum networking is no longer a distant concept-it's a transformative technology reshaping the future of enterprise infrastructure. This session will explore how quantum networking, a vision we intend to realize in the next five years, can unlock unprecedented computational power and security capabilities. We'll dive into real-world advancements, including Cisco's groundbreaking Quantum Network Entanglement Chip and Network-Aware Quantum Compiler, which are paving the way for scalable, distributed quantum systems. Attendees will learn how quantum networking enhances classical systems today, from secure communications to advanced decision-making, while preparing enterprises for the quantum era. Join us to discover why understanding quantum networking is critical for staying ahead and how our partnerships with quantum leaders like IBM can deliver a strategic advantage. Don't miss this opportunity to understand the advantages and considerations your organization can take to harness the power of distributed quantum computing.

### A journey from visibility to segmentation in OT networks - BRKIIOT-2019
Speakers: Kevin Holcomb (Cisco), Andrew McPhee (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780412370782001HclM
Segmentation has been top of mind for many years, but yet most still struggle to move from concept to implementation. The purpose of this session is to detail a realistic journey towards implementing policies within the OT network. The session will include: - Understanding Industry Frameworks for OT segmentation - Why traditional IT techniques dont work, and how to adapt them to OT - How to use network visibility to design a segmentation strategy - How to monitor the effect of policies before implementing the enforcement

### A new unified SDN architecture for Transportation - BRKIIOT-2020
Speakers: Pete Kavanagh (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780412371027001Hz6N
Bringing together SD-Access and SD-WAN, the new Secure Automated Roadways CVD has unified macro and micro segmentation, across fiber, cellular, LEO and p2p radio transports. This covers Roadways design and deployment, but is very applicable to other transportation modalities too. If you'd like to learn how to eliminate layer-2 from your transportation access networks, and ensure that all transports are 256-bit encrypted, please attend!

### ACI Endpoint Controls: Resolving Rogue Endpoint and COOP Dampening Faults - TACDCN-2021
Speakers: Aaron Hackett (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780412382620001Hhrg
This session focuses on diagnosing and resolving Rogue Endpoint(EP) and COOP Dampening faults within an ACI fabric. When endpoints are flagged by Rogue EP Control or COOP Dampening, endpoint learning is temporarily disabled - a behavior that, while protective by design, can result in traffic disruption for the affected endpoint. Attendees will gain a thorough understanding of how these endpoint controls operate, how to extract and interpret relevant information from generated faults, and how to apply that information to pinpoint the root cause of an issue. The session will also cover common endpoint movement scenarios, walking through practical approaches to resolving each one and restoring normal fabric operation.

### ACI Layer 4-7 Policy-Based Redirect (PBR) Deep Dive and Tips - BRKDCN-3982
Speakers: Minako Higuchi (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362713178001qCyt
When designing network security in a data center, have you ever wondered where to enforce security policies and how to steer traffic to get firewalls to inspect the traffic? How would the use of Cisco ACI affect your Layer 4-7 services such as firewalls, IPSs, and load balancers? This session will provide a deep dive into ACI Layer 4-7 service integration capabilities, especially PBR service graphs. It will cover ACI contract security capabilities, how forwarding works to steer traffic, design options with common use cases, and multi-location data center design options. The basic assumption is that participants will have a solid knowledge of Cisco ACI and its functional components, including EPGs, contracts, VRF, bridge domains, Multi-Pod, Multi-Site, etc.

### ACI Multi-Site Architecture and Deployment - BRKDCN-2980
Speakers: John Weston (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362710948001qnxB
The need to deploy ACI in multiple data center sites to provide redundancy is a common requirement for both enterprise and service providers. This session introduces the application, operation, and availability considerations for Cisco ACI multi-site and covers multiple aspect of the architecture, including an introduction and positioning between multi-pod and multi-site architectures, inter-site network (ISN) deployment considerations, ACI Nexus Dashboard Orchestrator (NDO, formerly MSO) architecture and deployment, ACI multi-site control and data plane, connecting to the external layer 3 domain, and network services integration. The expectation is that attendees already have at least a basic understanding of ACI in order to get the best out of this session.

### ACI Objects: How to Avoid Getting Your Configuration Wires Crossed - BRKDCN-2932
Speakers: Davis Creemer (Cisco), Chris Merkel (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362707792001qx6q
This session is designed for engineers and architects who spend time designing and implementing ACI-based solutions. With a focus on best-practice and scalability, we will review common design challenges and how to avoid them. The ACI object model provides a powerful way to set policy once and reuse it as needed, but knowing how to use these objects can be challenging. It can also be easy to find yourself in an endless configuration sprawl. From the AAEP and pDom to the EPG and the layer 3 out, we will talk about many of the commonly used objects, their relationships, their purposes, and how to optimally reuse them for truly scalable solutions. Attendees should have a general understanding of ACI and some familiarity with its common objects.

### ACI Troubleshooting: Optimize Your APIC User Experience through API Mastery - BRKDCN-2635
Speakers: Gabriel Monroy (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362703832001qa29
All configuration interactions with an application-centric infrastructure (ACI) fabric begin with an API call. Every day brings new integrations and monitoring tools, which take advantage of the APIC's single-northbound API. It is imperative to understand that APIC performance has a direct correlation to API request handling, and to have a methodology to isolate issues related to both. You will leave this session having learned how the API relies on APIC subsystems, which features are available to optimize and protect the API, and how to isolate and troubleshoot APIC and API performance issues.

### AI Agents 101: An Introduction to Agents and Examples of How to Build Them - BRKETI-1008
Speakers: Tim Miller (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362727476001qCL9
When Generative AI burst on the scene three years ago, the world was abuzz with the technology's natural language processing capabilities. Since its introduction, the industry has now seen four major transitions in GenAI architectures: chatbots with prompt engineering, AI assistants enhanced with RAG, autonomous AI agents, and now multi-agent systems. The speed of innovation in this space is FAST and understanding the terminology, the architectural differences, and emerging risks has become a barrier to adoption. This session will demystify agents by introducing the key criteria for AI agents, the agentic patterns to build them, and the technologies to connect and secure them. This session demonstrates these key concepts with Python code examples. Therefore, knowledge of Python (or other programming languages) and foundational understanding of Generative AI concepts will enhance the attendee's experience.

### AI Agents for Network Automation 101 - CTF-1057
Speakers: Julia Palomino (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362808570001qe2p
AI autonomous agents are transforming industries, but how do they apply to network operations? In this "unplugged" session, we'll move past the hype to explore the fundamentals of LLM-powered agents. We'll break down the core building blocks-such as planning strategy, reasoning, memory, and tool usage-to understand how they function in a network context. Join us to discover what these agents can truly do, what they cannot do, and the essential principles behind them. No frameworks or fancy libraries-just the basics.

### AI Comes to Webex Contact Center Flow Designer - TACCOL-2021
Speakers: Sam Watson (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780412381484001HQoJ
Do you ever feel like your productivity hits a wall when you have to jump between your design tool and external documentation just to find a quick answer? Contact Center is solving that with the upcoming release of AI Assistant for Webex Contact Center Flow Designer. By bringing AI Assistant into Flow Designer, the solutions that once required time-consuming searches will be just a click away. It's an intelligent copilot that brings contextual help, visual resources, and advanced search - covering everything from JSON parsing to custom scripts - directly into flow design. By eliminating the need to leave the application, administrators and developers will be able to reduce the learning curve and build, optimize, and deploy flows faster than ever before. This session is ideal for partners and Contact Center administrators who are interested in how AI Assistant will streamline their experience in Flow Designer.

### AI Everywhere Starts Here: Cisco + NetApp's Enterprise Advantage - WOSPAR-1010
Speakers: Mike Hommer (NetApp)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362850332001qwy2
Successful AI adoption requires more than powerful processors; it demands an enterprise-class foundation to manage data, optimize performance, and simplify operations. As AI adoption accelerates, organizations need a secure, scalable data infrastructure to manage AI from experimentation to production. Cisco and NetApp deliver this through integrated platforms that combine high-performance networking, flexible compute, and secure data management at scale. Join us to discover how Cisco and NetApp transform AI initiatives into business value with a modern infrastructure that is simple to operate, trusted by design, and ready to evolve with emerging AI demands.

### AI Gave Every Attacker a Kubernetes Playbook. Isovalent Takes It Away - PSOSEC-2345
Speakers: Duffie Cooley (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362841100001qyhe
Kubernetes environments are dynamic, ephemeral, and often invisible to traditional security tools. Because workloads spin up and disappear in seconds and east-west traffic remains within the node, standard IP-based policies frequently fail. This is the environment where your applications - and your AI workloads - actually run. Isovalent brings runtime security, network segmentation, and multi-cloud connectivity into one eBPF-based platform. In this session, we will demonstrate how Cilium replaces IP-based network policy with identity-aware enforcement, while Isovalent Runtime Security delivers process-level visibility and nano-segmentation. We will also show how these tools integrate with Splunk ES to provide actionable, ATT&CK-mapped detection content. If you run Kubernetes in production, this session provides a blueprint for building security that is as dynamic as your infrastructure.

### AI-Powered Network Test and Validation: From Controlled to Autonomous - BRKCX-2002
Speakers: Richard Froom (Cisco), Patrick Pless (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362701190001qdys
As networks grow more complex and change more frequently, testing those network changes at scale requires a new model. The Automated Validation Framework (AVF) introduces a flexible autonomy model that lets teams choose the right level of control for each workflow, from requirement-level human validation to fully autonomous agentic test execution, without sacrificing visibility or operational confidence. When AVF receives a validation intent, it derives test requirements that define correct resolution, derives test cases from those requirements, and executes them using the methods your team already uses to manage devices, whether that is controller-based management, automation pipelines, or direct CLI. AVF does not require your team to upskill before it delivers value. It delivers the capabilities that enable your team to naturally work at a higher level. You will leave this session with ideas to help you: Implement controlled autonomy workflows to safely validate network changes while keeping humans in the decision loop. Transition to fully autonomous validation for AI-driven test execution and automated operational actions. Understand how to integrate autonomous validation into existing operational processes to increase efficiency, reduce errors, and improve reliability. This session is intended for participants with experience in network operations, change management, or automation. A demonstration will show AVF in Cisco IQ operating across all three autonomy tiers.

### ATT&CKing Our Own Defenses (Before Someone Else Does) - BRKSEC-2227
Speakers: Mike McPhee (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362778503001qA8N
Most security architectures are built with good intentions - but attackers don't care about intentions. They care about gaps. Yet many teams still rely on assumptions, alerts, or tool checklists to decide whether their defenses are effective. In this session, attendees will learn how to proactively evaluate and improve defensive coverage by "ATT&CKing" their own environments using the MITRE ATT&CK framework. Following a real?world threat example from initial activity through detection, coverage mapping, gap analysis, and validation, this session demonstrates how cyber threat intelligence can be translated into measurable SOC outcomes. Designed for anyone who cares about defending their environments, SOC analysts, security practitioners, network security teams, and security?adjacent roles, this session focuses on practical, repeatable techniques for identifying detection and prevention blind spots, prioritizing improvements based on attacker behavior, and validating progress using adversary emulation and automation, including MITRE Caldera. By the end of this session, attendees will be able to: Use MITRE ATT&CK to describe attacker behaviors in a way that aligns SOC detection and response Map SOC detections and security controls to ATT&CK techniques to identify coverage gaps Perform practical gap analysis to prioritize detection and response improvements Validate and measure SOC effectiveness using adversary emulation techniques such as MITRE Caldera Through live demonstrations and real?world stories, you'll learn practical ways to identify detection blind spots, prioritize improvements, and measure whether changes actually improve readiness against real threats. These are techniques you can apply immediately - whether you're tuning defenses, supporting incident response, collaborating across the organization, or communicating security effectiveness to leadership.

The thought alone is enough to keep any security professional up at night. But fear not, because our upcoming session is an absolute must-attend for anyone serious about cybersecurity. We will delve deep into the cutting-edge Mitre Engage framework, a revolutionary approach that empowers you to proactively hunt down and neutralize threats before they escalate. This isn't just another lecture; it's an interactive experience that will equip you with the skills and insights to turn the tables on intruders. By joining this session, you'll transform from a passive defender, anxiously awaiting an attacker's misstep, to a strategic enforcer of your network's integrity. Don't miss this opportunity to gain a critical edge in the ever-evolving battle against cyber threats. Secure your spot now and be the force that keeps adversaries at bay. Your network's safety hinges on your proactive stance - be the one who takes it.

### Add Visibility to Your S(A)SE with ThousandEyes - BRKSEC-2857
Speakers: Primoz Kolman (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362781628001q8or
Your organization operates multiple branches leveraging Cisco Secure Access and Cisco SD-WAN. As your network and security infrastructure transitions to the Internet and cloud, concerns about loss of control and visibility arise. This session demonstrates how ThousandEyes delivers Assurance and Digital Resilience within your Security Service Edge (SSE) and Secure Access Service Edge (SASE) environments, helping you reduce mean-time-to-resolution (MTTR) and mean-time-to-innocence (MTTI) for network and application issues. What You Will Learn How to deploy and leverage ThousandEyes to assure your employees digital experience with SaaS and on-premises applications, regardless of their location - office, home, or abroad How ThousandEyes Experience Insights, integrated within Cisco Secure Access, provides comprehensive visibility into endpoint, network, and application performance across your SD-WAN network How to gain end-to-end visibility from branch offices to critical SaaS and collaboration applications, enabling IT teams to quickly isolate and resolve performance issues, reducing troubleshooting time and eliminating finger-pointing between teams and providers. Audience Level Intermediate. Attendees should have a basic understanding of ThousandEyes, Cisco Secure Access, or Cisco SD-WAN solutions.

### Addition by Editions: A technical introduction to the Splunk Security portfolio - BRKSEC-2057
Speakers: Brandon Tansey (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362771381001qEq3
You've almost certainly heard of Splunk and the fact that customers use it to do all sorts of things. You probably know a lot of organizations across the globe use it at the center of their security programs. You have DEFINITELY heard that Cisco and Splunk are better together. Why? More importantly (for this session), how?! Join us for a technical introduction to the world of Splunk security. We'll show you some of the magic of Splunk's central search and analytics prowess before covering the key areas of Splunk's Threat Detection, Investigation, and Response platform. This includes an introduction to the various capabilities (think SIEM, SOAR, UEBA, and of course the AI within them) as well as a dive into the technical frameworks that power to help you understand not only where they fit, but really understand what makes them tick. The session will also cover points of integration between the Splunk security portfolio and broader Cisco universe.

### Adopting Network Policies in Highly Secure Environments - BRKCLD-2017
Speakers: Raymond De Jong (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362682410001qCDQ
In the modern era of distributed computing, network connectivity is the backbone of every application - but not all network traffic should be public. Kubernetes, by default, allows open networking, making it essential for organizations to implement robust network policies to protect sensitive workloads. Drawing on real-world experience from highly regulated and complex environments, this session will introduce the core concepts of Cilium Network Policies, including application-aware and identity-based security models. We'll walk through the differences between default-allow and default-deny policies, demonstrating how a risk-based approach helps prioritize protection for your most sensitive assets. The session will also cover exposure types and practical strategies for securing workloads across diverse environments. Attendees will gain hands-on insights into leveraging observability tools like Cilium, Hubble Timescape, and Splunk Observability Cloud to visualize network flows and streamline policy creation using the Network Policy Editor. We'll also showcase how Network Policy Guardrails empower organizations to maintain security control while enabling teams with self-service policy management. By the end of this session, participants will have actionable strategies to efficiently secure Kubernetes environments - even those handling highly sensitive workloads - using modern network policy best practices.

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362726585001qS4u

Security Enhancements: Exploring advanced security mechanisms embedded within BGP to mitigate emerging threats. Scalability Solutions: Examining techniques for optimizing BGP performance to scale seamlessly with the exponential growth of network traffic and devices. Whether you are a seasoned network architect or an aspiring networking enthusiast, you will gain a deeper understanding of advanced BGP features. This session offers invaluable insights to propel your expertise to the next level.

### Advanced Campus Network Design: Multilayer Architectures and Next-Gen Protocols - BRKENS-2500
Speakers: Shawn Wargo (Cisco), Jakub Matela (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362715983001qS9v
This advanced session builds on the foundational knowledge from "Introduction to Campus Network Design and Multilayer Architectures - BRKENS-1500" and refreshes the core concepts of "Enterprise Campus Design: Multilayer Architectures and Design Principles - BRKENS-2031. Attendees will explore the details of designing high-performance, scalable multilayer campus networks, with an emphasis on advanced protocols and technology integration. Key topics include: Advanced aspects of multilayer campus network design Pros and cons of different design models (1-2-3 or 4+ Tier) Advanced protocol implementations (ECMP vs. StackWise / MPLS vs. EVPN vs. SD-Access) Integration with wireless and security solutions Real-world examples and best practices for optimizing enterprise networks This session is geared towards professionals responsible for the design, deployment, and management of enterprise campus networks, who possess a solid understanding of LAN switching and routing fundamentals.

### Advanced Catalyst SD-WAN Policies Troubleshooting - BRKENT-3797
Speakers: Eugene Khabarov (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362727257001qJuF
This is a deep-dive session on troubleshooting Cisco Catalyst SD-WAN policies. It is based on extensive experience from the TAC and BU escalation teams. The main goal of this session is to demonstrate troubleshooting tools, approaches, interesting failure scenarios and common pitfalls that have been observed in the field. These include configuration and design mistakes with centralized control, data, and application-aware routing policies. The session will be based on various real-life scenarios involving DIA, NAT, traffic engineering, routing, deep packet inspection, classification, and QoS marking. It will show how behaviors that are not initially expected can be revealed through advanced troubleshooting methodologies. And yes, we will be using the CLI a lot. Please note that this is an advanced-level session and it does not cover the basics of SD-WAN. The target audience for this session includes network architects, consultants, engineers, and network designers who are seeking a deeper understanding of SD-WAN policies troubleshooting, as well as knowledge to avoid common mistakes in their day-to-day operations, design, and deployment activities when working with a Cisco SD-WAN solution. It is recommended to have reviewed the "Advanced Catalyst SD-WAN Routing Troubleshooting" session (BTKENT-3793) from CiscoLive OnDemandLibrry as a prerequisite for this session.

### Advanced Fabric Management in Nexus Dashboard: Extending Beyond the Initial Fabric Build - BRKDCN-2657
Speakers: Joshua Lee (Cisco), Chris Merkel (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362704925001qlxt
Every data center network deployment is unique, requiring tailored configurations to meet specific business needs. Using a fabric created by the VXLAN and Classic LAN templates as a starting point this session explores advanced fabric management techniques that go beyond the initial fabric build. Key topics include route peering and control, enabling essential network functions such as DHCP and loop detection. Additionally, we will explore best practices for managing custom fabric configurations using policies, freeform configuration, and resolving configuration drift. Designed for network engineers and architects familiar with Nexus Dashboard 4.x fabric templates, this session provides practical insights to extend and optimize fabric management.

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362720833001qLRR

The contribution of each technology to SD-Access packet forwarding and security will be systematically examined, supported by concise lab captures illustrating the underlying concepts. ### Advanced Manufacturing Automation Powered at the Edge - WOSPAR-2026
Speakers: Vijay Kesavan (Intel)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362862734001qlXw

Manufacturing IT leaders must design edge-focused architectures that process data near machines and production lines to overcome connectivity limits, protect IP, and keep operations running. In this session, Intel and a strategic software partner in manufacturing will share how their solution built on Cisco Unified Edge and Intel Xeon 6 SoCs addresses real customer challenges to deliver secure, responsive, AI-powered manufacturing environments.

### Advanced SD-WAN Routing Troubleshooting - BRKENT-3793
Speakers: Eugene Khabarov (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362727038001qvRF

This session is very "TAC cases" driven and main aim is to demonstrate interesting failure scenarios and common pitfalls that we've seen in the field (including configuration and design mistakes) in the following areas: Troubleshooting VPN 0 (transport side) Routing Overlay Management Protocol (OMP) featues and best path selection algorithm issues OMP interraction with service-side routing protocols and loop prevention Please note that this is an advanced level session and it does not cover basics of SD-WAN. Main audience is Network Architects, Consultants, Engineers and Network Designers who are seeking a deeper level of knowledge about SD-WAN control plane and routing to avoid common mistakes in their day to day operations, design and deployment activities while dealing with Cisco SD-WAN solution. page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362783858001qQWf

### Advanced SGT - Multi Domain Context - BRKSEC-3707

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362784823001qPWJ
This session examines the use cases for Security Group Tags (SGTs) and how SGTs have evolved into the defacto language for security policy within Cisco and the industry. This session will review lower level design, configuration, monitoring and troubleshooting of SGTs and SGACLs applied to use cases like user/device segmentation, IaaS and SaaS policy controls as well malware mitigate Security Group technology will be discussed as applied to LAN, WLAN, WAN and Data Center networks. This session is aimed at Network/Network Security Specialists and Architects involved in designing and building advanced security solutions scenarios using Cisco network and security appliance deployment models. page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362708443001qUqp

A review of FC protocols will be followed with a discussion of the advanced capabilities of the Cisco MDS 9000 family. Design principles and practices for building FC SANs in a multi-speed, multi-protocol environments, including high availability, security, scalability, and management. Included are topology design choices, optimal use of hardware, fabric routing (FSPF), link aggregation (Port Channels), TCAM usage and Virtual Fabrics (VSANs).

### Advancements and Applications of Coherent Pluggable Technology in High Bandwidth Transport Networks - BRKOPT-2556
Speakers: Anuj Malik (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362762319001qnec
Coherent pluggable technology has emerged as a pivotal solution, integrating coherent optics into pluggable transceivers. This innovation offers a scalable, flexible, and cost-effective approach to meet the increasing demand for bandwidth. This presentation will delve into the technical underpinnings of coherent pluggable technology, including key components such as tunable lasers, high-speed ADC/DACs, and sophisticated DSP algorithms. It will also explore the performance metrics, such as data rates, reach, and power consumption, comparing them with traditional coherent solutions. Furthermore, the session will highlight real-world deployment scenarios, case studies, and future trends, underscoring the transformative impact of coherent pluggables leading to convergence of IP and Optical layers.

### Agentic AI Standards: An Overview - BRKNWT-2622
Speakers: Suresh Krishnan (Cisco), Frank Brockners (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362748933001qSlI
Agentic AI is evolving rapidly - and a new set of standards is being created. From formal efforts in organizations like the IETF, TM Forum, and 3GPP to emerging open-source initiatives in the Linux Foundation (AAIF, A2A, AGNTCY, and more), a new ecosystem of specifications is taking shape to define how autonomous AI agents interoperate, communicate, and scale. This session provides a clear, practical overview of the key standardization efforts shaping Agentic AI. The session explores who's doing what, where mandates overlap, and how these initiatives may ultimately fit together. Whether you're building, deploying, or governing AI agents, you'll leave with a structured map of the standards landscape - and a better understanding of where it's headed.

### Agentic AI at the Edge: Solving the Data Consistency Challenge with Cisco & Qumulo - WOSPAR-2017
Speakers: Brandon Whitelaw (Qumulo)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362860676001qAXq
As AI shifts from static training to dynamic Agentic Reasoning, the data rules have changed. While training models on data copies is acceptable, autonomous AI agents require a "single source of truth" to make accurate, real-time decisions. Relying on eventually consistent replicas at the Edge can lead to disastrous automated actions. Join Brandon Whitelaw, SVP of Product at Qumulo, to discover how Qumulo's Cloud Data Fabric pairs with Cisco Unified Edge to eliminate data silos. Learn how to deploy a strictly consistent Global Namespace that extends from the Core to the Edge, ensuring your AI agents act on live data - not ghosts. This session covers the architecture of "Edge Accelerators," zero-copy data pipelines, and how to achieve local-performance with global data visibility.

### Agentic AI for Networkers - BRKOPS-1327
Speakers: Frank Brockners (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362755484001q4Dd
As Yuval Noah Harari notes, humanity's unique ability to collaborate in large groups is what sets us apart from all other species. Today, generative AI is enabling a similar leap - enabling software to collaborate with software in natural language - similar to humans: Agentic AI. Agentic AI refers to systems composed of multiple AI agents that can autonomously reason, plan, collaborate, and act in pursuit of complex goals, with minimal or no human supervision. This session explores the past, present, and future of agentic AI, with a strong emphasis on practical, demo-driven insights into how these systems support and enhance networking use cases. We begin with a review of current AI assistants in networking, then transition to a deeper dive into how agents can coordinate, reflect, leverage tools, and work together to solve problems. Demos will showcase agentic AI automating workflows, customizing user interactions (e.g., AgenticOps with AI Canvas), and even reasoning across unfamiliar data schemas in cross-domain and cross-organizational troubleshooting scenarios. The session concludes by exploring distributed agentic AI - what some call the Internet of Agents. We'll discuss both the infrastructure and semantic layers: how agents are discovered, connected, secured, and observed using protocols like A2A, MCP, and frameworks like AGNTCY.ORG; and how they simulate human-like cognition through structured reasoning, group memory, goal alignment, and interaction patterns. Attendees will leave with a clear understanding of the current landscape of agentic AI, its real-world applications in networking, and where this transformative technology is headed next.

### Agentic AI in Action: Redefining Business Practices - ITLGEN-2002
Speakers: Carlos Pereira (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362815347001q988
While AI is moving at unprecedented speed and transforming the technology landscape, many businesses are still challenged to define "AI for what?", associate ROI, avoid hype, and map the required AI-related connections between IT leadership and lines of business. Meanwhile IT leaders face a critical reality: siloed AI and fragmentation leading to higher cost and security risk. Join Cisco Fellow and CX's Chief Architect to discuss real-life examples of AI adoption journeys where Carlos Pereira will share, in details, approaches for strategic alignment between IT and lines of business plus lessons learned and insights from multiple engagements. We will touch upon best practices around AI use-cases; repeatable patterns; Agentic AI solutions design and development (including AI stack, data, security); deployment choices and scalable operations considerations.

Join us to learn how Control Hub and AgenticOps are transforming collaboration administration with unified management, intelligent observability, and AI-powered operations.

Leave with the insights to transform your data center into a future-ready AI powerhouse.

### AgenticOps for Cisco Secure Firewall : Autonomous, Self-Healing Security with a Digital Workforce - BRKSEC-2509
Speakers: Gayathri Nagarajan (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362780734001qBWx
As organizations scale, firewall operations become a maze of configuration complexity, event noise, and constant firefighting. Cisco's AIOps for Firewalls has begun to ease this burden with predictive insights and guided remediations, but the next leap is here. Cisco's AgenticOps for Firewalls is the next evolution of AIOps. This introduces intelligent, self-healing agents that go beyond recommendations to autonomously provisioning configurations such as VPNs, apply them, and help troubleshoot issues end-to-end. In this session, attendees will learn how AIOps transforms firewall health management, how guided remediations evolve into self-healing actions, and how AgenticOps establishes a digital workforce capable of handling end to end tasks, thereby reducing MTTD/MTTR and cutting operational overhead. ### AgenticOps in Action: AI-Driven NOC and SOC Operations with Cisco Cloud Control - BRKOPS-2279

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780412372253001HdJb

Next, the session helps you think through how Cisco Cloud Control may support your day-to-day NetOps and SecOps workflows, including monitoring, detection, correlation, triage and root cause analysis, using shared operational context and AI-driven intelligence. This session will highlight how you may be able to evolve operations leveraging Cisco Cloud Control for: 1 Real-time Monitoring - End-to-end visibility with real-time inventory and topology across campus, data center, security, and collaboration environments. 2 Alerting and Notifications - AI-powered, correlated alerts for network and security issues with third-party ecosystem integrations. 3 Issue Investigation & Root Cause Analysis - AI Assistant and AI Canvas-driven troubleshooting using natural language workflows to correlate incidents across domains and accelerate MTTR. Through real-world use cases, you will see how Cisco Cloud Control simplifies operations, strengthens security posture, and provides a scalable foundation for proactive enterprise operations - demonstrating how AgenticOps is transforming NetOps and SecOps for the AI era.

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362673737001q9pY

### Agents Under Fire: Full-Stack Security from Model to Kernel - PSOSEC-2346
Speakers: Andrew Schwartz (Cisco), Christopher Luciano (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362841328001qr4l
Agents get attacked before they run and while they run. Malicious MCP tools, backdoored skills, and compromised models introduce risk before the first request is processed. Once running, adversaries use prompt injection and tool manipulation to redirect agent behavior, then move laterally through the infrastructure underneath to reach credentials, databases, and sensitive workloads. This session maps the full attack lifecycle and shows how to close security gaps. Cisco AI Defense secures the model and agent layer using supply chain scanning, MCP traffic inspection, and runtime guardrails against prompt injection and tool exploitation. Isovalent secures the infrastructure layer with runtime enforcement that stops container escape and contains lateral movement at the kernel before attackers can pivot. Full-stack defense, from model to kernel.

### Agents, Models, and Mayhem: Securing Every Layer of the AI Data Center - CENSEC-1046
Speakers: Uzair Khan (Rush University Medical Center), John Brow (Northwestern Medicine), Dan Wendlandt (Cisco), Rick Miles (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780412375694001HPWO
AI workloads running in the data center pull in third-party models, libraries, and agents - any one of which can introduce risk. Security bolted on after can't keep up. See how Cisco fuses enforcement into every layer: hardware root of trust, DPU-powered firewalling, Kubernetes-native workload protection, runtime model guardrails, and agentic identity control.

### All You Need to Know About Forwarding on Cisco 8000 Series Secure Routers - BRKARC-2886
Speakers: David Roten (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362676038001q8hm
Cisco 8000 Series Secure Routers are the latest evolution of Cisco's enterprise routing platforms. These new platforms are built on the strength of the previous ASR1000 and Catalyst 8000 Series routers. As the enterprise routing platforms have evolved a number of different hardware architectures have been used. These architectures include the Secure Network Processor, QFP, x86 and ARM. Each iteration has added innovations which optimize performance while introducing specific troubleshooting nuances. This session will be a deep dive into the specific functions, behaviors, and troubleshooting techniques in high utilization scenarios across these platform variations. Details of the packet distribution mechanisms across the multi-core processors will be covered. This session will help attendees understand which platforms are optimal for specific roles in their network, assist in troubleshooting, and give deeper understanding into platform selection.

### Always-On, Never Guessing: Turning UC Analytics into Action with AI - WOSPAR-2010
Speakers: Victor Vrba (Variphy, Inc.)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362859036001qy3H
Today’s UC environments face a familiar set of challenges: missed calls, overwhelmed support teams, and fragmented visibility make it hard to turn insight into actions, just as expectations for always‑on engagement continue to rise. Point solutions alone no longer cut it. Teams need an integrated platform that unifies analytics, management, automation, and AI to deliver real visibility, control, and action. Participants will learn how Variphy uses AI Virtual Voice Agents to deliver 24/7 call handling, seamlessly transfer to human agents when needed, and provide real‑time operational insight with detailed historical reporting. The session highlights how this integrated approach reduces reliance on point solutions, improves visibility, and enables faster, confident decision‑making. You’ll leave this session with ideas to help you: Deliver always‑available service with AI‑driven call automation Preserve context through seamless AI‑to‑human handoff Use real‑time insights and historical reporting to drive action Make data driven business decisions

### An Introduction to Agentic AI - BRKAI-1010
Speakers: Robert Barton (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362672400001qJjY
Enterprise networks are becoming increasingly complex, spanning cloud services, SaaS applications, distributed users, and hybrid infrastructure. Traditional operational tools often struggle to keep pace with this complexity. Agentic AI introduces a new approach, using autonomous software agents that can reason, plan, and interact with operational tools to assist engineers in investigating and resolving issues. This session translates Agentic AI into the language of the network engineer, explaining its concepts through familiar operational workflows such as troubleshooting, telemetry correlation, and runbooks. Rather than focusing on abstract AI theory, we show how agentic systems mirror the investigative process engineers already follow - collecting data, forming hypotheses, and executing remediation steps. We will introduce core agentic design patterns, along with emerging interoperability protocols such as Model Context Protocol (MCP) and Agent-to-Agent (A2A) communication. Finally, we explore how Cisco is applying these ideas in platforms like AI Canvas, demonstrating how multi-agent architectures can enhance network visibility, troubleshooting, and operational efficiency.

### An Introduction to How AI Canvas Works - BRKXAR-2040
Speakers: Surabhi Agarwal (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362792803001qeMf
In this session you will see inside AI Canvas, the natural language interface that runs Cisco Cloud Control. In this session we will explore the architecture and capabilities of AI Canvas, and how it functions as part of a larger agentic system. We will also explore Cisco's Deep Network Model, including Cisco's approach to developing this model, and how it is being used in specialized troubleshooting agents.

### Architecting AI-Assisted Automation Frameworks for Large-Scale Deployments? - BRKATO-2006
Speakers: Josh Halley (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362677728001qAf6
As AI-driven automation evolves from scripted playbooks to autonomous multi-agent systems, organizations need architectural patterns that scale with this shift. This session provides a practical guide to designing AI-assisted automation frameworks that go beyond traditional workflows - leveraging multi-agent LLM pipelines and causal inference to move from symptom correlation to true root cause analysis. Attendees will learn modular framework design, agent orchestration patterns that combine AI reasoning with closed-loop feedback mechanisms, and strategies for processing large-scale network data through AI systems. The session explores how to integrate Cisco's automation ecosystem - including Nexus Dashboard, AppDynamics, and ThousandEyes - with orchestration tools like Ansible and Terraform, while maintaining robust security with zero-trust principles and enhanced observability. A multi-agent diagnostic workflow is walked through end to end, from telemetry ingestion through AI-powered analysis to automated remediation. Whether you're evolving existing automation workflows or building agentic AI systems from scratch, this session equips you with practical, actionable strategies to lead AI-driven operations at enterprise scale.

### Architecting Enterprise Networks at Scale: A Founder's Perspective - WOSPAR-2018
Speakers: Atif Khan (Alkira)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362860908001quSr
Advanced-level session: AI workloads, distributed applications, and real-time data flows are forcing enterprise networks to operate with a new standard of speed, scale, and control. As environments expand across multiple clouds, regions, data centers, and edge locations, modern network architecture must deliver not only resilience, but also hyper-agility, stronger security, and simpler operations. This session explores the architectural choices that matter most when building enterprise networks for the AI era. It focuses on how to create consistent policy across domains, simplify connectivity and control, and design for fast change without sacrificing performance or security. You'll leave this session with ideas to help you: Design architectures that maintain consistent segmentation, security policy, and connectivity across hybrid and multi-cloud environments. Reduce operational complexity while increasing agility by simplifying control planes and avoiding fragmented connectivity models. Build networks that support AI-era demands with resilience, predictable data movement, observability, and rapid change.

### Architecting Layer 4-7 Network Services in a Multi-Tenant Data Center with VXLAN EVPN - BRKDCN-2974
Speakers: Matthias Wessendorf (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362710726001qp67
With VXLAN BGP EVPN data center fabrics, we now embrace integrated multi-tenant capabilites. With this, not only is the requirement for tenant separation present but also interconnectivity and attachment of layer 4-7 services. This session specifically covers requirements and approaches for deploying multi-tenant data center fabric with a focus on how to archive multi-tenancy within data plane and control plane around VxLAN with BGP EVPN. Further, the session will cover integration of layer 4-7 services to satisfy the needs of tenant interconnectivity, security appliance, and load balancers attachement. We will define the notion of multi-tenancy and how it relates to small, medium, and large enterprises, as well as discuss hardware and software technologies as they map to multi-tenant data centers. Subsequently, the integrating of layer 4-7 services into VxLAN with BGP EVPN is approached. This session will focus exclusivly on data center fabric deployments with VXLAN BGP EVPN including EVPN multi-site.

### Assuming Breach Drives Better Defensive Outcomes - BRKSEC-2048
Speakers: Jason Maynard (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362770026001qQfu
Defenders continue to struggle getting the upper hand and it's time to level up. In this session we will focus on core tactics and techniques (initial access, lateral movement, and more) used by the adversary and the opportunity to build out defensive capabilities leveraging Cisco Security technologies. We will act like the adversary emulating attacks and then take this newfound knowledge and apply it defensively. This drives risk reduction in the most critical areas of the environment supporting all digital / cyber resilience programs. As we start to apply these capabilities the adversary will struggle which in turn allows their signals to become easier to detect. You may never achieve 100% protection 100% of the time but we can certainly make it very difficult for the adversary to succeed. Lots of demos included.

Are you responsible for the performance and reliability of hybrid cloud connectivity? ### Automating ACI, SD-WAN, and Campus (SDA and IOS-XE) with Service-as-Code: Simplifying Cross-Domain Integration and Policy Consistency - BRKXAR-3022
Speakers: Navin Suvarna (Cisco), Dhrumil Prajapati (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362794620001quk2
Enterprises run ACI in the data center, SD-WAN in the WAN, and a mix of SDA and standalone IOS-XE at the campus edge. Integrating these domains consistently across the data, control, and policy planes remains a major challenge. This session presents a prescriptive architecture for AC SD-WAN Campus integration and introduces Service-as-Code (SAC) as the automation model that operationalizes it. Service-as-Code enables declarative configuration across controller-based (ACI, SD-WAN, SDA) and direct-to-device (IOS-XE) environments using Cisco-managed data models. It integrates with Git-based CI/CD workflows for incremental change and applies automated pre and post validation to enforce compliance. The framework also delivers cross-domain orchestration from a single interface to ensure consistent policy, secure segmentation, and operational efficiency across the enterprise network. Attendees will learn how to design a cohesive multidomain architecture and automate it end to end using Service-as-Code to achieve unified policy enforcement and simplified operations at scale.

### Beyond DMARC: Full-Spectrum Domain Defense Against Impersonation Attacks - WOSPAR-1023
Speakers: Brian Westnedge (Red Sift)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362852980001qce1
Email remains the #1 entry point for cyberattacks, and domain impersonation has evolved far beyond simple spoofing. While DMARC (Domain-based Message Authentication, Reporting & Conformance) is a crucial first step, attackers now leverage lookalike domains and DNS (Domain Name System) misconfigurations across email, social media, and the web to bypass traditional security measures. This session will provide ideas to help you: Implement a "full-spectrum" defense strategy: Learn how to combine DMARC enforcement with DNS monitoring and brand protection to create a robust defense against exact-domain spoofing and lookalike domain attacks. Identify and mitigate DNS misconfigurations: Understand common DNS vulnerabilities, including subdomain hijacking, oversized SPF records, and dangling CNAME records and how attackers take advantage of these misconfigurations to send phishing emails from legitimate subdomains. Detect and take down lookalike domains: Gain insights into how attackers craft deceptive variations of legitimate domains and use them in phishing and fraud campaigns and how AI-powered risk scoring assesses these threats for take down across various channels. This session will demonstrate how Domain Protection by Red Sift OnDMARC and Red Sift Brand Trust work together as a layered SolutionsPlus offering to deliver comprehensive domain protection

### Beyond the (Switch)-Port: Exploring the World of Host Networking - BRKNWT-2620
Speakers: Bruce McDougall (Cisco), Chris Lapp (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362748487001q3zR
Traditional network design often stops at the top-of-rack switch, yet the most dynamic innovation now happens farther down the stack-inside the host itself. This talk traces that journey past the physical switch port and into the rapidly evolving realm of software-defined host networking. We begin by dissecting the native packet paths of modern operating systems, showing how kernel and user-space stacks manipulate latency, throughput, and security. From there we move into hyper-visor territory, comparing virtio, SR-IOV, and the performance trade-offs of leading vSwitches such as OVS and VPP. Next, we scale out to Kubernetes and its Container Network Interface (CNI) ecosystem. Real-world benchmarks illustrate how Calico "just routes," how Cilium leverages eBPF for policy-driven data paths, and how VPP-backed CNIs unlock terabit-scale throughput. Attendees will see where these technologies shine-and where they break-under microservice sprawl, multi-tenant tenancy, and real-time media workloads. Finally, we peer over the horizon at emerging techniques for sub-100 µs host networking: -Kernel-bypass fabrics powered by XDP and AF_XDP -eBPF-driven security and congestion control customized per workload -Smart-NIC offloads that fuse packet processing with AI inference at the edge -Next Generation Proxies for flexible transport By the end of this session, architects and engineers will leave with a practical networking blueprint for building next-generation infrastructure-one where ultra-low-latency, fine-grained observability, and security converge inside every host.

This session includes customer use cases, demos across products, and real-world examples of agentic automation. Intermediate experience with Splunk products and AI concepts is recommended.

### Bringing Network Detections and Visibilty to Splunk - BRKSEC-2928
Speakers: Hanna Jabbour (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362782304001qo8f
Your network sees everything - but is your SOC acting on what matters? This session explores how Cisco Secure Network Analytics (SNA) transforms raw network telemetry into high-fidelity, actionable detections and delivers them directly to Splunk, empowering SOC and SNOC analysts to detect, investigate, and respond to threats with greater speed and confidence. Modern security teams face a critical gap: the network generates massive volumes of data, yet meaningful threat intelligence rarely reaches the SIEM in a usable form. This session tackles that problem directly, walking through the architecture, integration methods, and analyst workflows that bridge Cisco Secure Network Analytics and Splunk Enterprise Security. What you will learn: How SNA reduces noise and surfaces only the most critical network security events for your Splunk analysts How to send SNA alarms, alerts, and findings to Splunk via Webhooks and Syslog using Response Management How the Cisco Security Cloud App ingests CIM-aligned SNA data, delivers network and security dashboards, and promotes high-severity alarms to Notables in Splunk ES How to use Risk-Based Alerting (RBA) with SNA detections to build low-noise, high-fidelity correlation rules, including MITRE ATT&CK tactic-based detection for slow-and-low attacks Three short demos walk through each integration step - getting data into Splunk, visualizing it, and correlating SNA detections across all indexed data sources to generate actionable Notables and Findings. Prerequisites: Basic Splunk familiarity and general network security knowledge recommended.
