### Building AI/ML Fabrics at Hyperscale: Architecture Evolutions, Challenges, Open Standards, Performance Characterization and Validation on Cisco SiliconOne-based Platforms. - BRKMSI-2014
Speakers: Masiuddin Mohammed (Cisco), Evan Knipp (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362746189001qPdj
Due to the explosion of AI/ML infrastructure, scaling from thousands to hundreds of thousands of accelerators, the need for a comprehensive overview of the evolution, challenges, and deployments of these technologies at scale is pertinent in understanding Cisco's AI networking product roadmap. We will show how the latest features are validated in production-like environments. Architecturally, we trace the evolution from 4K and 16K GPU leaf-spine clusters, to 64K GPU Disaggregated Scheduled Fabric (DSF) two-tier designs with Super Spines, to >200K GPU three-tier scale-out fabrics - plus multi-DC DCI interconnects. We address both scale-out (fat flows, ECMP polarization, asymmetric failures, fault resilience) and scale-up (low-latency tensor-parallel at ~8x per-accelerator bandwidth, e.g., NVL72) requirements, and the massive bandwidth and buffering demands of DCI (including encryption and high-speed buffers over ~100 km/1 ms RTT). In this session, we present the benchmarking methodologies used in the field - including lessons from hyperscale deployments - to align tests with fast-evolving AI training and inference requirements, minimizing test drift, and bringing reproducibility closer to reality through open, standards-aligned practices

### Building Decision-Driven Wireless Networks: Applying AI Where It Actually Matters - BRKEWN-3007
Speakers: Vishal Desai (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362734155001qRMv
Enterprise Wi-Fi networks generate massive telemetry from RF metrics, client behavior, and environmental signals. Yet even when the network appears "healthy," user experience often tells a different story. The reason: traditional wireless systems rely on static rules and thresholds that don't adapt to context, timing, or scale. In this session, we move beyond the hype to show how wireless networks must evolve from signal-processing systems into decision-driven systems. We introduce Cisco's Wireless Decision Layer; an architectural approach that separates insight from action to enable context-aware, time-aware, and intent-aware decisions. You'll learn how Cisco applies advanced analytics and AI to answer four key questions behind every wireless action: When to act (timing and context) What to change and why (root-cause-driven decisions) How far to go (confidence and blast radius control) Who should act (recommendation vs automation) Through real-world case studies and production architectures, we demonstrate how this approach: Reduces unnecessary RF churn and instability Improves root cause analysis with confidence-driven attribution Enables safe, bounded automation at scale Delivers measurable improvements in client experience and predictability We'll also cover how these systems are built by combining wireless expertise with AI models, feedback loops, and decision guardrails to ensure intelligence enhances stability rather than compromising it. The session concludes with a live demo showing how decision-centric design translates into real operational outcomes. If you design, operate, or troubleshoot large-scale wireless networks, this session will provide a practical framework - and a new mental model - for evolving wireless systems in the AI era.

### Building Secure Multi-Tenant AI Clouds for Enterprise: Infrastructure Patterns from NeoCloud Builders Using Cisco - CSSCOM-2008
Speakers: Tarun Raisoni (Gruve), Kamal Garg (Cisco), Dave Driggers (Cirrascale Cloud Services), Ryan DiRocco (Qumulus)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362804477001q3xo
As enterprises move from AI pilots to production deployments, many are building or consuming shared GPU infrastructure that must meet enterprise and sovereign standards for security, performance, and operational governance. Unlike typical bare-metal GPU clouds designed for single-tenant workloads, these environments must support strong tenant isolation, deterministic network performance, and deep operational visibility across distributed AI infrastructure. In this panel, operators from leading NeoCloud platforms like Cirrascale will share how they design enterprise-grade AI data centers capable of supporting secure, multi-tenant workloads at scale. The discussion will explore architectural patterns emerging across the industry, including regulated multi-tenant AI clouds, distributed inference fabrics deployed close to users through regional interconnection points, and modular sovereign AI infrastructure designed for edge and regulated environments. Attendees will gain practical insight into how networking, security, and observability shape the design of modern AI clouds and what infrastructure decisions enterprises must consider when building or consuming multi-tenant AI platforms.

### Building Secure, Agile Campuses: Cloud-Managed Campus Fabric with End-to-End Segmentation & Dynamic threat response - BRKENS-2654
Speakers: Raj Kumar Goli (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780412368450001H2Z0
In today's distributed enterprise environments, organizations require secure, agile networks that span campus, branch, and hybrid sites - without the operational complexity, management silos, or rigidity of traditional infrastructure. Legacy approaches frequently result in fragmented visibility, slow troubleshooting, and increased exposure to lateral movement during security incidents. This session demonstrates how the Cisco Meraki Dashboard, a fully SaaS-based, cloud-native platform, enables intent-based campus fabrics using standards-based EVPN-VXLAN orchestration. Attendees will learn how to implement end-to-end segmentation through macro-segmentation (Virtual Networks for broad trust-zone isolation) combined with micro-segmentation (Adaptive Policy with Security Group Tags/SGTs for identity-driven, granular policy enforcement within the same VLAN or subnet, all enforced network-wide in a topology-agnostic manner. The session will also go into Wireless consideration when deploying Fabric using Meraki Dashboard. The session will go in detail on how to implement Adaptive Policy and extend this info the Fabric and carry external to the Fabric. We will also discuss the AI Assistant capabilities on how it helps simplify day-to-day operations . Demo scenarios will illustrate rapid deployment, granular policy enforcement in modern enterprise environments.

And how does everything learned in a lab environment translate to enterprise workflows that actually need to work at 2 AM without a human watching? You'll leave with a clear-eyed view of where the technology stands today versus where it's heading, and practical insight into how WWT thinks about the design choices that make the difference between an agent that's impressive in a demo and one that's trustworthy in production.

### C2C and Zero Trust Reporting from the Sources of Truth - BRKXAR-2026
Speakers: Chad Mitchell (Cisco), Christopher Hess (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362791219001qrCW
Compliance comes in many forms and reaches across multiple endpoint or device types. The status and enforcement of compliance adds additional complexities that require full situational awareness and network wide information that could dictate action now or action later. The Department of Defense Comply to Connect (C2C) program introduces these problems in an enterprise wide implementation mandate that can be a daunting and complex process. Thankfully, Splunk is poised perfectly to handle this problem. In this session, participants will examine the multitude of inputs and attributes that must be collected and reported on for the C2C program compliance. Using multiple data inputs from solutions like Cisco ISE, Secure Network Analytics, Secure Firewall, and multiple third party solutions attendees will see how data can be correlated and acted upon to achieve the outcomes of the C2C program while also setting the stage for Zero Trust. By the end of the session, you'll be equipped with practical ideas to identify the attributes and their sources that apply to your C2C journey and how they can be presented with Splunk to give you actionable intelligence, in real time, on the compliance of your network.

On Monday, your colleague Max tried to upgrade the network, but now Rohan can't access data on a server, Emma can't connect to the internet, and Joe can't SSH to a core switch. What do you do? ### CPS Security 101: Why IT Tools Don't Cut It - WOSPAR-2000
Speakers: Hans Noordhuis (SELF)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362856745001qBrj
For decades, Operational Technology (OT) lived in isolation. But as IT and OT networks converge to drive revenue and productivity, that "air-gap" has evaporated, introducing connected cyber-physical systems (CPS) that leave industrial environments exposed. The danger? Many organizations are trying to protect turbines, PLCs, and SCADA servers using the same tools they use for office laptops. Standard IT scanning can break fragile OT devices, and a single "reboot to patch" can cost thousands of dollars per second in lost production. Join us as we break down why OT requires a total shift in mindset - from the "CIA" triad of IT to the "AIC" priority of industrial operations. What You'll Learn: Why OT assets can't handle modern IT security scans.How to protect system Availability first without compromising the Integrity of operational processes.How to give plant engineers and CISOs a shared language to maintain uptime and regulatory compliance.

### Campus Design with Secure Network Reference Architecture - BRKENS-2614
Speakers: Shawn Wargo (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780412368223001HMlP
This session introduces you to the new Secure Network Reference Architecture (SRNA) design methodology. Building a future-proofed secure campus network for the next decade requires a fundamental shift in how networks are designed and operated. Today's campus networks must support unprecedented demands for speed and scale, ultra-high availability, protection from security threats, quality for entirely new traffic patterns driven by AI and HD video, and increased power and connectivity requirements - all while delivering deep visibility and analytics at the device and application level. Secure Campus Solution Brief This session will cover the following areas & topics: What is the Secure Network Reference Architecture (SRNA)? What is the SRNA design methodology & key building blocks? 1. Secure Network Infrastructure - Wired & Wireless deviecs, Unified Hardware, Software & Licensing 2. Secure Fabric & Secure WAN - Overlay Fabric for L2/L3 & Multicast, with SGT & VRF segmentation 3. Common Policy & Firewalling - On-premise & Cloud-based Identity & Policy, including Common Policy 4. Unified Management & Assurance - On-premise & Cloud Automation & Analytics, including AgenticOps 5. Extended Ecosystem - Sharing device & flow analytics with external management tools Applying SRNA to Secure Campus network design, Real (ACME) Campus design examples, using SRNA By completing this session, you'll gain familiarity with the SRNA design methodology and learn how to confidently apply its principles when designing your own future-proof Secure Campus architecture.

### Circuit-Style Segment Routing and Service Emulation - BRKMPL-2133
Speakers: Thomas Peiyao Wang (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362741497001qQAp
This session will demonstrate how to enable time-division multiplexing (TDM) and private-line emulation services over bandwidth-guaranteed circuit-style segment routing. Attendees will also see service emulation use cases over the resilient circuit-style segment routing transport. Traditional circuits over SONET/SDH transport are becoming outdated, even at the end of life, and costs are skyrocketing. Segment routing has been well adopted by the market. Circuit-style segment routing provides an alternative solution to offering reliable transport, but much cheaper than SONET/SDH. The centralized SR-PCE helps to compute the network resources and ensure the bandwidth-guaranteed CS-SR data path with protection.

### Cisco Secure Client. Bringing VPN, VPNaaS, SWG, EDR, EPP, DLP and the whole alphabet soup together! - BRKSEC-2834

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362781405001qBZN

Come learn from Bill Yazji, while he shows you that Cisco has listened to the complaints and continues to improve our unified security agent called Cisco Secure Client. Cisco Secure Client (CSC) provides a modular framework allowing for VPN (both on-premise and cloud), Cisco Secure Endpoint (the artist formerly AMP for Endpoints), Network Visibility Module, Secure Access (ZTA/ZTNA/SASE/SSE) & Umbrella Cloud Security, ISE Posture, Secure Firewall Posture (formerly Hostscan) and the Network Access Module (NAM) to all exist together; with a modern cloud-based management solution. Oh yea - there are new modules we will be reviewing as well! Did someone say DLP? In this session, we will dive into the technology behind the Secure Client, how things really work and the nuances where they do not. We will cover best practice deployment models from the cloud and using your own software deployment mechanisms. We will learn all about the upgrade flows from existing AnyConnect and Secure Endpoint (AMP) agents, and how to migrate from non-cloud managed installs to cloud managed. Come spend some time with Bill & be entertained while learning all about this exciting development from Cisco Security.

### Cisco Secure Firewall & Splunk: Uncovering Behavioral Anomalies with Advanced Logging - CISCOU-2073
Speakers: Marc Mastrangelo (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362802201001qzJh

Seamlessly integrated with curated Splunk dashboards, this empowers security teams to proactively uncover behavioral anomalies like data exfiltration and command and control. You'll gain deep, actionable intelligence for Big Data analysis, moving beyond basic event monitoring. Basic understanding of network security concepts and logging principles is recommended.

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362774698001q6Sr
In today's complex, multi-vendor, and multi-vector threat landscape, XDR is essential for unifying telemetry and security detections across network, cloud, endpoint, email, identity, and applications. Leveraging advanced AI and machine learning, XDR allows to detect sophisticated threats earlier, prioritize alerts by business impact, and guide security teams through efficient investigation and response workflows. Join us for an in-depth session exploring how Cisco XDR transforms security operations by integrating broad telemetry sources and providing enriched context and automation. This session offers a comprehensive walkthrough of Cisco XDR's feature set, latest innovations, and real-world use cases with demos designed to empower your SOC to detect, investigate, and respond faster and more confidently.

### Cisco's Quantum Networking Vision: Architecting the Infrastructure of the Future - BRKETI-2007
Speakers: Reza Nejabati (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362728581001qna0
Quantum networking is no longer a distant research concept, it is rapidly emerging as a foundational technology that will reshape enterprise infrastructure. In this session, we outline a clear path toward realizing a practical quantum networking, unlocking new levels of computational scale, resilience, synchornisation and security. We will showcase real-world breakthroughs in building a full-stack quantum network, including Cisco's quantum networking hardware and our quantum software stack. These innovations demonstrate that quantum networking is not merely a physics milestone - it is a systems engineering challenge requiring tight integration of hardware, synchronization, control, and orchestration to operate at scale. Participants will gain insight into how quantum networking can enhance classical infrastructure today - strengthening secure communications, enabling advanced optimization and decision workflows, and preparing data centers to effectively integrate and leverage quantum computing resources. Join us to understand why quantum networking is becoming a strategic imperative, and how collaborations with industry leaders such as IBM are accelerating the path from lab to large-scale deployment. This session will equip you with the perspective needed to position your organization for leadership in the quantum era.

### Closing the AI Skills Gap: Strategies for Building a Future-Ready Workforce - PP-1204
Speakers: Marci Paino (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780412378455001HLxo
AI is reshaping the world of work at breakneck speed, with the majority of ICT jobs set to transform and nearly 40% of today's skills becoming obsolete by 2030. As organizations race to keep up, the technical talent gap is reaching critical levels across G7 economies - threatening the ability to scale AI responsibly, securely and effectively. In today's fast-changing environment, mastering advanced technologies isn't enough; professionals must also build essential human skills to remain competitive and drive innovation. This session draws on the Cisco-led AI Workforce Consortium's latest research to help organizations prepare employees for the AI era and build a resilient, future-ready workforce. In this session, you will: • Gain insights from the AI Workforce Consortium's latest global research into the most urgent skills needs across G7 economies. • Explore actionable frameworks to strategically align workforce development with evolving AI demands. • Learn real-world examples of Cisco's scalable AI upskilling initiatives driving workforce agility.

### Closing the Distance with Connected Intelligence: How AI and Security are Reshaping the Collaboration Platform - KDDCOL-1000
Speakers: Espen Loberg (Cisco), Snorre Kjesbu (Cisco), Ilya Bukshteyn (Microsoft Corp.), Vinod Muthukrishnan (Cisco), Amit Barave (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362820859001qliW
Every enterprise runs on decisions. The distance between a decision made and a decision in motion is where productivity stalls and risks rise. Join this session to learn how collaboration, powered by AI and grounded in security, is the infrastructure that closes that gap for better employee and customer experiences.

### Closing the Gap: Turning Neocloud GPUaaS into Secure, Multi-Cloud AI Platforms with Cisco - PSODCN-1013
Speakers: Samir Parikh (Cisco), Sree Chadalavada (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362830249001qDuZ
As enterprises move from AI experimentation to production, the requirements for AI cloud services are rapidly converging with traditional enterprise expectations: security, reliability, performance, and operational control. At the same time, AI workloads introduce new challenges - massive east-west traffic within GPU clusters, distributed inference across hybrid environments, and the need for deep visibility into workload behavior. Delivering enterprise-grade AI cloud services therefore depends on a secure, high-performance networking foundation that is purpose-built for AI. The session will examine how Cisco's portfolio (AI Networking, SD-WAN, Secure Access, Isovalent and Thousand Eyes) supports hybrid and multi-cloud AI deployments, ensuring consistent security and operational models across on-prem, edge, and cloud environments. By aligning high-performance networking with zero-trust principles and automation, Cisco enables AI cloud providers and enterprises to deliver scalable, compliant, and resilient AI services.

### Closing the Identity Trust Gap - PSOSEC-2342
Speakers: Prabhu Barathi (Cisco), Katherine Yang (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362840431001qKkB
In 2026, attack patterns are overwhelmingly targeting infrastructure components that broker trust: compromised VPN credentials, IAM weak points, and persistent lateral movement via RDP, PsExec, and PowerShell. These threats exploit critical blind spots like non-human identities and unmanaged devices in your privileged environment. In this session, learn how to close the gap in identity security and visibility by securing these hard-to-cover "edge cases" of IAM. We will demonstrate new identity intelligence tools and how to operationalize identity context within your firewall policies to build a dynamic, adaptive defense that stops attackers at the network edge. See how to get started with practical, scalable strategies that strengthen authentication posture and defend against the most detrimental identity-based threats.

### Codifying Business Intent: Driving Consistency with a Unified Architecture Framework and Architecture as Code - BRKXAR-2042

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362793251001qvdg
Architecture as Code (AaC) represents the strategic evolution of architecture, transforming traditional, static architectural artifacts into dynamic, machine-readable code. This session introduces AaC as the pinnacle of architectural automation, demonstrating how business intent and strategic objectives can be directly translated, codified, and validated within an architectural framework. The session will introduce a unified architecture framework that seamlessly integrates Generative AI and automation, enabling the translation of high-level business priorities into actionable architectural specifications. The session will feature a comprehensive, live demonstration illustrating the complete lifecycle: from defining business intent, through automating the codification process with AI tools, to generating a robust architecture plan. ### Collective Cyber Defense: The Why, What, and How of Cybersecurity Threat Intelligence Ecosystems - BRKSEC-2179
Speakers: Jeremy Stanley (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362777179001qc35
U.S. critical infrastructure is increasingly targeted by sophisticated cyber adversaries, exposing organizations to risks that threaten the stability of essential services and national security. In this evolving threat landscape, organizations cannot effectively defend themselves in isolation. Timely and collaborative cybersecurity threat intelligence is essential. Cybersecurity Threat Intelligence Ecosystems are collaborative frameworks where organizations, agencies, and stakeholders exchange actionable information to detect, prevent, and respond to cyber threats. By actively participating in these ecosystems, organizations not only strengthen their own defenses but also contribute to the safety and resilience of their broader community and the world at large. This session will explore the evolution of threat intelligence sharing, explain how these ecosystems are built and operated, and demonstrate the vital role that widespread participation plays in protecting organizations locally and globally. Real-world examples will illustrate how sharing threat intelligence elevates collective cybersecurity for all.

### Common Policy to rule them all: Seamlessly Integrate SDA, SD-WAN, ACI and Cloud - BRKXAR-2001
Speakers: Arvind Durai (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362790762001q4Qb
Infrastructure segmentation is achieved through packet-level controls applied from source to destination, often requiring traffic to traverse multiple network domains. A common policy framework is essential to maintaining consistent enforcement across these domains. This session explores the design elements needed to extend common policy across SD-Access (SDA), SD-WAN, ACI, and VXLAN EVPN - using Cisco Identity Services Engine (ISE) as the central policy broker to propagate Scalable Group Tags (SGTs) end to end. How far can you extend microsegmentation across network domains - and what are the tangible benefits for your organization? This session demonstrates how ISE brokers policies across multiple domains - Campus, WAN, and Data Center. It also includes a live demo showcasing how the ISE policy model can reduce reliance on firewalls in the campus environment and simplify enforcement consistently across all domains. Attendees will learn how to construct end-to-end policy spanning from the campus to the data center, with design guidance and best practices covering SDA, SD-WAN, and ACI. The session concludes with a live demonstration, giving attendees a hands-on view of building a software-defined enterprise with secure segmentation in action.

Low Earth Orbit (LEO) satellite-based Non-Terrestrial Networks (NTNs) offer a high-throughput, low-latency, and scalable solution for extending wireless connectivity to remote and underserved regions. As NTNs become a strategic complement to terrestrial infrastructure, they are enabling key use cases such as 5G backhaul using SRv6 and EVPN overlays, SD-WAN for enterprise connectivity, secure communications for defence and national infrastructure, and future-ready support for quantum-resilient transport. Cisco is developing a validated, cloud-native architecture to enable seamless deployment of 5G Xhaul, SD-WAN, and IoT services over integrated terrestrial and non-terrestrial domains. The architecture is designed with built-in resiliency, leveraging segment routing and advanced telemetry to deliver dynamic path optimization, redundancy, and high availability, even in the face of link instability or constrained satellite bandwidth. The session will also showcase Cisco's Provider Connectivity Assurance (PCA) solution, which plays a critical role in delivering end-to-end service assurance. PCA provides real-time visibility, deep analytics, and SLA monitoring across both terrestrial and satellite transport, ensuring consistent performance, rapid fault isolation, and operational simplicity in complex hybrid network environments.

### Cross-Domain Agentic Troubleshooting with AI Canvas - BRKAI-2000
Speakers: Yathi Udupi (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362673071001qjDR
As enterprise networks span increasingly complex domains - networking, security, and observability - traditional troubleshooting approaches struggle to keep pace with operational demands. AI Canvas is Cisco's AI-driven platform that brings agentic operations to network troubleshooting through a collaborative, visual experience designed for cross-domain investigations. At its core, AI Canvas uses a central supervisor agent that orchestrates multiple specialized domain agents, each equipped with domain-specific knowledge and tools. This multi-agent architecture transforms troubleshooting from reactive guesswork into structured investigative workflows that surface root-cause analysis, evidence-backed reasoning, and actionable remediation steps. AI Canvas pairs this orchestration with a generative visual interface that renders interactive diagnostics, workflow views, and investigation dashboards directly from natural language queries. Operators can clearly see how agents reason and collaborate, while Human-in-the-Loop checkpoints ensure that critical remediation actions remain under human control. Through real-world examples and a live demo, this session shows how agentic AI can accelerate troubleshooting, reduce MTTR, and bring transparency to complex network operations.

### Deep Dive into Cisco's Use of QUIC, MASQUE and OS Native Capabilities to Deliver Frictionless Zero Trust Access - BRKSEC-3027
Speakers: Vinny Parla (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362783415001q0QA
Zero Trust has not yet reached its full adoption potential in the market, largely due to the degree of friction for both end users and IT administrators. Cisco's design goal is to develop Zero Trust solutions that frustrate attackers, not users. The experience should be as seamless and easy-to-use as turning on a Wifi connection. In this Tech Talk, we explore technologies that can help us deliver on this frictionless vision in collaboration with ecosystem partners such as Apple, Google, and Samsung. This session will examine industry standard protocols such as MASQUE as well as other technologies like WebAuthN, passkeys, and private 5G access that will eventually make Frictionless Secure Access as ubiquitous as Wifi in the enterprise and home. The session will also provide for an overview of native OS clients from Apple & Samsung as well as the Cisco Zero Trust Access module built into Cisco Secure Client, and how they leverage industry standards such as MASQUE and QUIC to deliver a seamless Zero Trust Access user experience.

### Defend the Flags - Access, Firewalls and Quantum - CTF-2009

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362812385001q1Gi

Because the future of security isn't built one product at a time, it's architected for what's coming next.

### Demystifying the Impact of AI on Security: Opportunities and Risks - BRKAI-1941
Speakers: Jay Tiwari (Cisco), Shaul Hellmann (Cisco), Munib Shah (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362672843001qjZU
Artificial Intelligence is reshaping how networks are built, secured, and managed. For security and network engineers, AI is not just a new tool it's a fundamental shift in how threats are detected, analyzed, and contained. AI-driven technologies can enrich threat intelligence through deep learning and accelerate the contentment of malicious activity. But the same capabilities that empower defenders also equip adversaries, creating an urgent need for more advanced security controls. This session explores both sides of the equation: how AI can enhance your security posture and how to defend against AI-powered threats. Join us to see how Cisco security solutions - including Secure Firewall, Identity Services Engine (ISE), and our AI-powered security framework, AI Defense - can be leveraged to: Enhance threat detection and response with AI and deep learning Automate security operations and reduce false positives Protect environments where AI is used to perform business-critical tasks Build proactive defenses against AI-enabled attacks Attendees will walk away with a clear understanding of how to integrate AI-driven security capabilities into their infrastructure, and how to safeguard against the next wave of AI-powered threats

### Deploy Network Changes with Confidence: Test and Validation with Network as Code - BRKOPS-1603
Speakers: Mac Carr (Cisco), Nandakumar Arunachalam (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362756139001q6sj
As organizations accelerate the pace of change in their networks, robust and reliable processes for deploying and updating network services are more important than ever. This session shows how Cisco's Network as Code (NaC) and Services as Code (SaC) enable teams to embed automated, comprehensive test and validation strategies into NetDevOps pipelines, reducing operational risk and increasing confidence with predictable change outcomes. Participants will discover how NaC's intent-based, declarative data models not only simplify configuration, but also enable automated verification of configuration accuracy and operational state. SaC, delivered by Cisco Customer Experience (CX), extends these capabilities by orchestrating service provisioning and ongoing change management through automated, version-controlled workflows with built-in validation at every step. The session features practical demos of integrated testing, using open-source tools like nac-test, and illustrates how additional applications and third-party tools can enhance validation and assurance. You'll leave this session with ideas to help you: 1) accelerate changes with greater speed and confidence, 2) minimize risk and manual effort through automated validation, and 3) leverage SaC for scalable, auditable, repeatable service delivery. ### Deploying IPv6 Routing Protocols: Specifics and Considerations - BRKIPV-2418
Speakers: Peter Paluch (Cisco), Fernando Goncalves (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362739499001qAD5
Even though all major IPv6 routing protocols are rooted in their well-known IPv4 counterparts, they all come with their own unique set of idiosyncrasies. And when faced with the task of deploying an IPv6 routing protocol along with its IPv4 variant, there are often multiple ways of doing it - but the best choice is not always obvious. The goal of this session is to discuss the specifics of IPv6 support in OSPFv3, IS-IS, EIGRP, BGP and OMP in IOS XE, and also spend a few words about IPv6 specifics in first hop redundancy protocols such as HSRP, GLBP, and VRRP. For each of these protocols, we will do a quick IPv6-focused refresher, and then focus on the protocol's unique IPv6 traits (and quirks). We will also look at how the protocol supports the co-existence of IPv4 and IPv6, and if there are multiple options to choose from, we will discuss the pros and cons of each of them. To benefit the most from this session, it is highly recommended to have a working knowledge of routing protocols in general.

### Deploying MPLS/SD-WAN Gateways for a Secure and Agile Access Network - BRKMPL-2257
Speakers: Chad Hill (Cisco), Patrick Evangelista (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362742374001q35a
SD-WAN is an essential technology that drives the success of modern networks. Leading enterprises increasingly turn to SD-WAN for its unparalleled cost-effectiveness, robust security, agility, flexibility, and superior network performance. This session will highlight how service providers can maximize these benefits by seamlessly interconnecting SD-WAN with MPLS networks using Inter-AS options. Participants will delve into compelling real-world use cases and discover how SD-WAN effectively meets urgent connectivity needs while also providing flexible solutions for rural, mobile, and transient locations. Additionally, we will explore automation of these configurations and the exciting potential of integrating SD-WAN with SRv6 networks for a native IPv6 transport solution.

### Deploying Nexus Dashboard in Your Organization - BRKDCN-2914
Speakers: Matthias Wessendorf (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362706714001q2jB
This technical breakout will provide an end-to-end view of deploying and operating Cisco Nexus Dashboard, which provides unprecedented simplicity by integrating operational services to manage customers on-premises and in cloud and hybrid fabrics through a single dashboard. You'll learn that by using the Cisco Nexus Dashboard, IT operations teams can seamlessly navigate all aspects of infrastructure lifecycle tasks from initial configuration and capacity planning to running and troubleshooting the entire IT infrastructure. Understanding how an infrastructure is deployed for usage of new tools and methodologies in your organizations is one side, but effectively operating the infrastructure is the other side. This session will review some common tasks and activities for deploying and operating Cisco Nexus Dashboard for day-to-day operations.

Cisco IQ is a standardized, scalable, and resource-optimized platform designed to collect telemetry data from customer networks and host Cisco applications for actionable insights and automation. This session addresses the business problem of ensuring a smooth and efficient deployment of the virtual appliance in your network. Participants will learn best practices for proper deployment and configuration as well as get introduced to the three deployment modes

### Deploying the Webex App to your Organization - BRKCOL-2198
Speakers: Shane Long (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362689764001qDCV
The Webex App brings together various collaboration workloads into one sleek, modern interface. In this session, we'll dive into how to deploy the Webex App effectively for your organization's users. We'll explore ways to streamline the user lifecycle of the app, covering best practices for installation, autostart, as well as login and logout processes. Plus, we'll share real-world examples of how different organizations are successfully implementing the app in specific industries. We'll also focus on two crucial aspects of app deployment: (i) the Cisco AI Assistant for the Webex App and its admin controls, and (ii) best practices for Unified CM calling with the Webex App.

### Deployment and operation of an Agile Services Network - BRKMSI-2012
Speakers: Phil Bedard (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362745716001qkO1
This session will guide participants through the multiple phases of deploying and operating a resilient multi-service provider network based on Cisco's Agile Services Networking architecture and validated design. Participants will learn technologies such as Segment Routing, EVPN, Routed Optical Networking, Crosswork Automation and others through a real-world deployment scenario. Deployment best practices and configuration will be used to present building a resilient underlay, VPN overlay services, and traffic engineering paths.

### Deployment of VXLAN EVPN Gateways with Cisco ACI for the Interconnection of Heterogeneous Data Center Fabrics - BRKDCN-2634
Speakers: Takuya Kishida (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362703617001q8sn
In recent years, the use of the VXLAN EVPN technology has become the de-facto standard for the deployment of data center fabrics. Cisco leverages this technology for its two flavors of fabric (and multi-fabric) architectures: Cisco ACI and Cisco NX-OS VXLAN EVPN. As a consequence, customers are very often challenged with the requirement of interconnecting those heterogeneous fabrics, both for co-existence or migration purposes. This session introduces the use of a VXLAN EVPN gateway functionality in a Cisco ACI fabric to establish native Layer 2 and Layer 3 connectivity with standard-based VXLAN BGP EVPN fabrics. The session covers the functional components and the control and data plane mechanisms, required to implement the VXLAN EVPN gateway functionality in an ACI fabric. It will also discuss how to interconnect pre-existing (brownfield) heterogeneous fabrics, a functionality usually referred to as "namespace normalization" and how to provide end-to-end micro-segmentation support between ACI and VXLAN EVPN fabrics through the integration of VXLAN EVPN gateways with the VXLAN Group Policy Option (GPO) technology. This session is a companion of breakout BRKDCN-2933, and the expectation is that attendees already have at least a basic understanding of Cisco NX-OS VXLAN EVPN technology in order to get the best out of this session. Also, it is highly recommended to attend BRKDCN-2602 before coming to this session.

### Design and Deploy Cisco Network Detection and Response Solutions - BRKSEC-2248
Speakers: Hanna Jabbour (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362779176001qVSd
Network Detection and Response (NDR) tools are critical components for any SOC or NOC and provide a great value for both users. Designing and deploying an NDR to provide complete visibility across hybrid network is key for successful and smooth day to day operation. This session will provide details on how to design and deploy Secure Network Analytics (SNA) in a hybrid environment. You will learn all the components (new and old) and how they fit in your network to provide visibility into workloads on-prem and in the cloud as well as users and devices locally or remotely. The attendees will learn how to integrate the different telemetry sources and their contribution into the analytics including firewall, network, endpoint and cloud data. This session includes details on SNA architecture as well as configuration and troubleshooting guidelines based on requirements and uses cases.

### Design your Cisco Cloud Managed Enterprise Wireless Networks with Campus Gateway - BRKEWN-2046
Speakers: Justin Loo (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362730596001qQu0
Are you thinking of going to a Cloud Native Management Solution for your campus? Are you worried that the current distributed data plane solutions will not meet your needs of a large roaming domain? This session is for you. Come and learn how to design for scale and performances leveraging Cisco Campus Gateway and the upcoming Network Groups release, while enjoying the simplicity and ease of use of the Meraki Dashboard for Cloud Management.

### Design, Automate and Visualize your AI Clusters with Nexus Dashboard - BRKDCN-1663
Speakers: Meghan Kachhi (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362702287001q5QS
Join this session to learn how Cisco Nexus Dashboard serves as the central command center for high-performance AI fabrics. We will explore how you can leverage this powerful platform to build a resilient, automated, and fully observable network optimized for demanding AI workloads. Simplify operations with centralized AI fabric automation (L3 Routed or EVPN VXLAN), enabling network deployment and management across multiple switches and data centers in just a few clicks. Optimize your AI fabric with new Dynamic Load Balancing settings, including flowlet switching, per-packet load balancing, and policy-based routing. Leverage deep integration with key ecosystem partners, including support for NVIDIA Spectrum-X, to build a robust and validated AI networking solution. Visualize network with advanced features like anomaly detection, event correlation, and suggested remediation to minimize downtime and accelerate issue resolution. Proactively identify and resolve bottlenecks using key capabilities like congestion scoring, PFC/ECN statistics, and microburst detection. Observe AI Jobs, with end-to-end visibility into AI workloads across the entire stack - from the network, NICs, and GPUs to distributed compute nodes - for comprehensive insight into infrastructure and AI job health. No previous AI networking background required. Walk away ready to design and deploy production-grade AI fabrics.

### Design, Automate, and Simplifying VXLAN EVPN Multi-Site Architectures with Nexus Dashboard Federation - BRKDCN-2988
Speakers: Parth Patel (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362711615001qO0g
With the exponential growth of Applications, Enterprises and Service Providers must create Scalable Data Centers. This has led to the deployment of Data Centers, large and small, spread across geographies, with IT requirements to interconnect them for reasons such as High availability, Disaster recovery, and Scale. VXLAN BGP EVPN Multi-Site allows inter-connecting loosely coupled VXLAN sites to build hierarchal overlays to provide secure Multi-Tenancy and Mobility at Scale. In this session, attendees will first learn different Design options and Automation workflows of VXLAN EVPN Single-Site and Multi-Site using Nexus Dashboard. Next, we will introduce Nexus Dashboard Federation, powered by One-Manage, which provides a unified platform to join, automate, and manage multiple Nexus Dashboard clusters. This centralized control enables seamless VXLAN EVPN Multi-Site deployments across Multi-Cluster fabrics, offering a scalable and automated solution to extend Layer-2 and Layer-3 services. Note: Attendees should have basic knowledge of Nexus Dashboard and Day-0 fabrics.

### Design, Validate and Certify your Wireless Streaming Telemetry Deployment - BRKEWN-2108
Speakers: Deepak Kumar (Cisco), Rafael Ceara Batlle (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362733036001qxkQ
This session will focus on the design, monitoring, and alarming of the wireless infrastructure with streaming telemetry and the process that the Cisco Wireless engineering team follows to validate and certify real use cases to scale as expected on production. We will cover different aspects that wireless engineers must take into account to ensure the network can grow as the business demands more traffic and compute to observe in real time what is happening in the network. Topics that will be discussed: Scalable streaming telemetry deployment Examples of use cases leveraging Cisco solutions such as Splunk, select open-source tools, and Cisco Catalyst Center. How to scale your telemetry infrastructure in/out based on network demand

### Designing Access and Core Switching Architectures with Meraki - BRKENS-1402
Speakers: Larry Biegel (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362714300001qgzK
Catalyst and Meraki are turning up the cloud power with Cloud-Native IOS-XE, bringing new platforms and new features to your enterprise networking tool belt. This session introduces the new access and, for the first time, core switching platforms available to address your network challenges, as well as powerful Catalyst features coming to the cloud for the first time. Participants will get to see how all of the new features and platforms can be used to solve problems, while improving performance and manageability at scale.

### Designing Agile Service Networks: A Practical Approach to AI Transformation - BRKMSI-2013
Speakers: Rob Piasecki (Cisco), Matt Birkner (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362745951001qawv
Service Providers and Enterprise Architects are challenged to transform their networks into AI-Ready platforms, driven by growing demands for compute, bandwidth, and scale. In reality, many customers find it difficult to know the best way to begin this process. This session shares our real-world expertise in building agile, simplified, scalable, and automated architectures that reduce costs and seamlessly integrate AI workloads. We will explore the key business drivers and industry trends behind AI-Ready Agile Services Networking. The session will highlight essential architectural components like Segment Routing, EVPN, and Routed Optical Networks, which are crucial for a resilient Service Fabric. The session offers practical advice for strategic and technical decisions in infrastructure design, while covering network topologies, best practices, and automation frameworks to help you implement AI-driven networking effectively and how to avoid common pitfalls. Attendees will gain real-world guidance on planning, design, migration, and operational strategies, providing actionable steps no matter where they are in their own architectural journey.

### Designing Always-On Campus Networks with Cisco Catalyst 9000 High-Availability - BRKARC-2102
Speakers: Aaron Cespedes Vega (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362675545001qhQk
Downtime is no longer an option in today's hyper-connected enterprises. Join us for an in-depth exploration of High Availability (HA) on Cisco Catalyst 9000 switches - built to keep your campus access, distribution, and core layers resilient, responsive, and upgrade-ready. In this session, we will: • Trace the evolution of HA - from traditional supervisor redundancy to the latest Silicon One-powered innovations. • Demystify HA architecture fundamentals, including fault domains, control-plane resiliency, and data-plane continuity. • Compare access-layer options, highlighting Stateful Switchover (SSO), Non-Stop Forwarding (NSF), and In-Service Software Upgrade (ISSU) for zero-impact maintenance. • Examine Stackable HA designs with StackWise/StackWise Virtual that consolidate multiple switches into a single, resilient control plane while delivering multi-hundred-Gbps stack bandwidth. • Dive into modular HA strategies for Catalyst 9400 and 9600 platforms - leveraging dual supervisors, fabric redundancy, and N+1/N+N power architectures. • Map best-practice HA models to campus distribution and core networks, ensuring sub-second convergence across routing protocols like OSPF, EIGRP, and BGP . • Talk about common problems and troubleshooting

### Designing Enterprise Wireless Networks for Challenging Environments - Tales from the Wi-Fi Crypts - BRKEWN-2054
Speakers: Al Dumdei (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362731260001q0jG
Challenging environments drive innovative solutions. In this session we will break down a number of challenging design requirements, discuss options for solutions, how we solved them, what best practices apply to these solutions, and look forward to incorporating additional spectrum by going to 6GHz and leverage Wi-Fi 7 features. Example challenging environments include: Voice in Healthcare - roaming and multicast. Dealing with very large and dynamic networks such as Higher Education (aaa, mdns, optimized timers, dealing with large crowds moving with schedule). What makes Wi-Fi work well in 'large public venues'? Dealing with challenging RF Environments (manufacturing, warehouses, industrial) In this session, you will improve your skills with best-practice wireless architectures, solutions to difficult installations, and optimized design process and configurations for on-premises, cloud, and hybrid solutions. Wireless design process and design options are continually evolving, and the number of options has been greatly expanded. We will dig into multiple complex design environments from real-world deployments and see how we solve these challenges of scale, unique environmental conditions, network requirements, and management using various architectures. Remember, your wireless design sets the stage for how well your network will perform, how well you will be able to manage your wireless network, and ultimately what your client's perception is of your wireless network.

### Designing Highly Available Networks using Catalyst 9000 Series Switches - BRKENS-2095
Speakers: Minhaj Uddin (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362715766001q4Ma
This session will cover new and existing high-availability features introduced in IOS-XE on Catalyst 9000 Series switching platforms. We will discuss how you can achieve high availability in your network through various stages in the design lifecycle. During the initial design, we will discuss the benefits of features like StackPower, power resiliency modes, StackWise-480, and StackWise Virtual. After the design phase, we will discuss features like GIR, extended fast software upgrade (xFSU), patchability, and ISSU that will help maintain resiliency during the operational phase. We will then conclude by covering how you can efficiently leverage all the high-availability features in different network designs and achieve maximum uptime and resiliency.

### Designing Next-Generation DC and WAN Networks for the Neo-Cloud Era with Cisco 8000, SONiC and XR - BRKMSI-1471
Speakers: Jiri Chaloupka (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362744386001qqCU
Modern data-center (DC) and WAN networks must handle unprecedented scale, complexity, and performance demands, particularly in hybrid and multi-cloud environments. This session explores the Neo-Cloud reference architecture - a proven design blueprint that uses IOS XR for backbone roles and SONiC for data center functions, delivering unified operations across both DC and WAN domains. Attendees will learn how to design high-performance interconnects, enable multi-vendor interoperability, and apply best practices for capacity planning, redundancy, and traffic optimization. Real-world use cases will illustrate how this architecture supports rapid service deployment, operational consistency, and the agility needed for emerging workloads. You'll leave this session with insight into: Proven architectural patterns for scalable and resilient Neo-Cloud DC and WAN deployments Understand strategies to monitor, manage, and optimize distributed networks effectively Practical examples showing improved agility, efficiency, and adaptability in Neo-cloud networks

### Designing Resilient Data Center Fabrics: HA Features at Every Layer - BRKDCN-2947
Speakers: Samer Theodossy (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362709124001qq44
This session will examine data center network design fundamentals and considerations for improving high availability, impacting an organization's daily activities by minimizing network disruption. It will cover key concepts, methodologies, and deployment recommendations, including network topology analysis and best practices. Starting with overall high-availability topics, the session will delve into device-level recommendations and network design concepts. It will address system-level high availability, service-level high availability, and network-level availability with scale and redundancy.

### Designing RoCEv2 Networks for Distributed AI Training with Cisco Nexus 9000 - BRKDCN-2688
Speakers: Praveen Poojary (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362706030001qxos
Distributed AI training jobs that coordinate thousands - even tens of thousands - of GPUs running for days or weeks place extreme demands on the data center network. Fabrics must deliver ultra-low latency, near-lossless behavior, and high, predictable throughput for collective operations such as AllReduce and AllGather. In this session, we walk through how to design and operate RoCE-based data center fabrics purpose-built for large-scale AI training. We'll examine why many hyperscalers separate frontend and backend networks for GPU clusters, and what that means for leaf-spine topology choices, routing designs, and failure domains. We'll then dive into how to tune congestion control, PFC/ECN, and QoS for bursty, low-entropy AI flows.

### Designing Routed Optical Networks: IP/MPLS Considerations - BRKSPG-2029
Speakers: Emerson Moura (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362785779001qS2b
Routed optical networking (RON) is a new IP/DWDM network architecture vision that greatly improves the economics and efficiency of networking as well as enables a new application paradigm. This session will specifically focus on the IP routing layer of RON and how this simpler, multi-service IP-centric architecture reduces operator costs, while at the same time improves network asset utilization and resiliency, as well as enables optimization of the network for distributed applications, content, and internet peering. A comparison of a traditional IP/DWDM architecture vs. RON architecture will also be presented.

### Designing Secure Networks for Imperfect Conditions in the Age of AI and Quantum - PSOSEC-2341
Speakers: Jonathan Felten (Cisco), Larry Lidz (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362840198001qr9H
Today's enterprise networks were designed for connectivity, not for today's AI-driven threat landscape or the looming reality of quantum-enabled attacks. As adversaries exploit identity, management-plane blind spots, and legacy cryptographic standards, traditional security assumptions have become critical operational liabilities. These gaps create visibility blind spots and slow incident response, increasing an attack's blast radius. Join us to explore a shift from "layering" security to embedding it directly into the network fabric. We will demonstrate how to future-proof your environment with hardware-level trust, PQC readiness, and identity-based policy enforcement that protects critical assets while enabling rapid innovation. Drawing on our expertise across the network and security stack, we will provide the strategies necessary to modernize your operating model, bridge the gap between NetOps and Security, and secure your infrastructure against both current threats and the quantum disruptions of tomorrow.

### Designing Smarter Journeys: Building AI-Ready Data Models with Journey Data Services - BRKCCT-3000
Speakers: Tarek Ayadi (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362681282001q9yv
Customer experience platforms are evolving fast, but AI cannot act intelligently without context. Journey Data Services (JDS) in Webex Contact Center bridges this gap by unifying customer data from CRM, business applications, and interaction history into a single, dynamic workspace. This session explores how to design and structure JDS for real-time decision-making and context-aware automation. Participants will see how JDS enables AI agents to look up customer records, follow ongoing support cases, and trigger intelligent routing decisions across systems. You'll also learn how to separate workspaces for AI-driven actions and human-assisted experiences, ensuring both sides share a consistent view of the customer journey. You'll leave this session with ideas to help you: • Build a scalable JDS schema aligned with your enterprise data. • Design decision-routing and proactive engagement powered by AI. • Create intuitive agent interfaces that surface contextual insights in real time. Live demos and design examples illustrate how to make JDS your foundation for AI-ready customer journeys.

### Designing Wi-Fi for a Wireless First World - BRKEWN-2057
Speakers: Jerome Henry (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362731702001q0xI
Congratulations! You are tasked to design and configure a Cisco Wireless network. Building on RF Fundamentals, this session will show you how to design and configure Cisco WLANs in a wireless-first world. Learn how to set up Wi-Fi networks for smooth roaming and consistent performance for latency-sensitive applications, like AR/VR, voice and video. We'll cover how to use Cisco's advanced features for client visibility and network tuning, ensuring your wireless environment supports a wide range of mobile devices and real-time use cases. Walk away with clear, actionable guidance for optimizing your Cisco wireless network for efficient and reliable connectivity

### Designing Zero Trust Data Center Segmentation Architecture - BRKDCN-2930
Speakers: Cesar Obediente (Cisco), Brenden Buresh (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362707575001qAww
Data center security has historically been dominated by leveraging firewall appliances to provide L4-L7 policy based stateful traffic controls. Introducing data center segmentation to sub-divide the network into smaller, more controlled domains offers additional protections by reducing attack surface diameter to defend against vulnerabilities. This session will investigate how data center segmentation can be systematically deployed in a layered fashion to provide the best aggregate security defense posture and create zero trust architecture. Utilizing a Nexus 9K VXLAN EVPN fabric baseline, first we'll explore macro-segmentation with VRFs and micro-segmentation with VXLAN GPO leveraging the network to provide L4 stateless policy controls. Next, we'll examine adding the DPU SmartSwitch platform and Isovalent host-based agents to strengthen security with L4 stateful controls. Finally, we'll expand to the hybrid mesh firewall concept combining network, host, and appliance enforcement points with a single central security controller to author and distribute policy.

### Designing for Distributed AI: A New Infrastructure Imperative - ITLGEN-2019
Speakers: Daniel McGinniss (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362818008001q1nc
The rise of real-time, data-driven applications is pushing AI beyond centralized environments and into every corner of the enterprise. From edge devices to data centers to the cloud, AI is now distributed by necessity - driven by the need for speed, efficiency, and contextual decision-making. This shift requires a new approach to infrastructure design. In this session, we'll explore how IT leaders can rethink where AI workloads live, how to manage the tradeoffs of a distributed model, and what it takes to build a flexible, secure, and scalable foundation for the next wave of AI innovation.

### Designing the Physical Foundation for AI Scale Networks: Power, Cooling, and High-Speed Connectivity - BRKPAR-2009
Speakers: Corey Lacaire (Legrand), Lucas Beran (Accelsius), Ribal Btaddini (Legrand), Garrett Furlong (Legrand)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362766800001qBXH
As AI workloads drive higher network speeds and denser switching architectures, network performance is increasingly influenced by the physical infrastructure that supports it. Power delivery, thermal design, and rack?level architecture are no longer only background considerations - they also directly affect network stability, latency, and the ability to scale as high?speed optics and AI fabrics become more common in modern data centers. This session takes a network?centric look at how power and cooling decisions impact real?world network performance. Industry experts will explore the relationship between switching density, power distribution, airflow, and thermal management, and discuss what network engineers should consider earlier in the design process to ensure reliable, efficient, and AI?ready network infrastructure.

### Detect, Correlate, Defend: The Power of Cisco XDR Against Adversarial Threats - BRKSEC-2053
Speakers: Ahmadreza Edalat (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362770722001q0ex
Adversarial attacks are becoming increasingly sophisticated, leveraging multiple vectors and evasion techniques to bypass traditional defenses. In this session, we'll explore how Cisco XDR detects, correlates, and responds to these complex attacks across the entire organization. You'll learn what defines an adversarial attack, how attackers move laterally through systems, and how Cisco XDR integrates telemetry from multiple security tools to provide unified visibility and faster response. Through live demonstrations, we'll walk through real-world adversarial attack scenarios showing how Cisco XDR surfaces hidden relationships, prioritizes critical alerts, and empowers analysts to quickly contain and remediate threats. Whether you're a SOC analyst, incident responder, or security leader, this session will give you a practical view of how Cisco XDR helps defenders stay one step ahead of adversaries.

### Deterministic Migration Automation Using GenAI- lessons from building production-grade solution - BRKATO-2004
Speakers: Hamid Sheikhghanbari (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362677234001qumH
GenAI offers transformative potential for Migration Automation , yet the probabilistic nature creates justified skepticism among engineers requiring deterministic outcomes. This session shares battle-tested methodologies for AI-Assissted Automation Solution that achieve 90%+ accuracy when proper discipline is applied. You'll witness our complete engineering journey: from 30% accuracy with naive prompts, through systematic A/B testing across multiple GenAI models, to our final architecture using phased refinement loops with convergence metrics. We'll candidly expose techniques and demonstrate the automated post-processing layer that corrects these errors without manual intervention. Critically, our architecture includes SME-in-the-loop validation: human experts review and approve/reject AI-generated outputs before production use. This hybrid approach addresses the core concern: "Can we trust LLM outputs for critical network operations?" This isn't AI hype; it's a rigorous engineering deep-dive into architectural decisions, validation frameworks, and quality gates required to make LLMs trustworthy. Learn reusable patterns for building reliable AI systems with appropriate human oversight, reducing adoption risk while maintaining quality control.

### Diagnosing Data Flow Anomalies in Splunk Ingestion Pipelines - TACOBS-2001
Speakers: Maria Heloisa Santos Flores (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780412386340001H1Gl
Data ingestion issues often surface as a simple but frustrating question "Where did my data go?". When data that was previously flowing stops or becomes delayed, identifying the root cause can be challenging as it can involve different features of the data ingestion pipeline. This session explores practical approaches to investigate these anomalies by examining patterns and key system signals. We will discuss common ingestion issues and how to interpret indicators such as queue behavior and data delays, and considerations to help guide troubleshooting efforts. Participants will learn how to interpret the data flow behavior, identify common ingestion issues and apply practical steps to investigate delays, gaps and unexpected data in Splunk ingestion pipeline.

### Digital Orchestration with Cisco's AI-powered CX platform - BRKCCT-2024
Speakers: Preetham Kademada (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362679879001qBJ6
AI is transforming contact centers, but many deployments remain voice-centric. This technical breakout shows how Webex Connect adds a digital orchestration layer - SMS/Branded Texting/RCS/WhatsApp, proactive notifications, and post-interaction follow-ups - to extend contact center moments into ongoing digital journeys. We'll cover the technical building blocks and reference architectures you need to implement Digital Front Door patterns: channel entry points, event triggers from contact center interactions, message orchestration, routing to AI or agents, and post-call continuity (secure links, FAQs, and persistent messaging threads). Using real-world scenarios (e.g., account access support followed by guided digital steps and an ongoing AI-assisted channel), you'll learn how to design for measurable outcomes like reduced repeat contacts, improved containment, and stronger customer confidence - without turning this into a step-by-step flow-building lab.

### Digital Resilience in the AI-era: Building a Secure and Trusted Agentic Enterprise - KDDOBS-1000
Speakers: Vikram Chatterji (Galileo), Cory Minton (Cisco), Chris Doebert (Cisco), Nimesh Bernard (Fannie Mae)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362821525001qBgm
Resilience in the AI era demands a new playbook for faster, smarter operations with autonomous agents, while ensuring trustworthy outcomes. Learn how Cisco and Splunk are helping customers build the foundation to move from reactive to predict-and-prevent operations across security, observability, and networking to drive autonomous resolution at machine speed.

### Digital twins are redefining how we interact with infrastructure and networking - WOSPAR-2022
Speakers: Kevin Egan (Equinix), Roger Duclos (Equinix)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362861834001qDJd
As AI systems become more distributed, the infrastructure supporting them becomes harder to reason about - and harder to control. Digital twins give us a way to model that complexity, simulate outcomes, and move from static networks to adaptive systems that respond in real time.

### Disaggregated Data Centers with Cisco 8000 & SONiC: Open, Scalable, AI-Optimized Front- and Back-End Networks - PSOMSI-2003
Speakers: Krithika Moorthy (Cisco), Tim Hu (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362838390001qmUH

### Don't Let Customers Run AI on Blind Trust: Learn how Webex and GetixHealth Deliver Exceptional Experiences with Agentic CX strategy - PSOCOL-2015

Everyone wants to use Agentic AI to deliver better customer experiences in the contact center - but AI can go wrong. Poorly designed and managed systems may hallucinate, leak information, skip authentication, or make promises they can't keep. These failures create issues that cascade to human agents - and ultimately erode customer trust that is difficult to rebuild. This strategic session will bring those risks to life with a rogue-agent example that will be uncomfortable, possibly humorous, and painfully real. We break down each step not only what the AI got wrong, but also how our newest Webex Contact Center innovations help solve these challenges. Finally, GetixHealth will join us to show how Cisco's strategy for Agentic CX will empower organizations to build and deliver trusted AI driven experiences the right way.

### Driving Superior Customer Outcomes: How Cisco and Splunk Leverage OpenTelemetry to Deliver Business Impact - BRKOBS-1026
Speakers: Antoine Toulme (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362752079001qMxb
This session explores why Cisco and Splunk have embraced OpenTelemetry as the foundational standard for telemetry data collection across all Cisco business units. Attendees will learn how this unified approach enables comprehensive observability, empowering customers to gain real-time insights into their infrastructure, applications, and networks. By leveraging OpenTelemetry across our greater product strategy, Cisco and Splunk deliver faster issue detection, improved operational efficiency - directly translating into measurable business outcomes for customers. The talk will also highlight how Splunk is investing in OpenTelemetry across our roadmap and ongoing innovations designed to meet evolving customer needs and ensure flexible, vendor-neutral observability solutions that drive customer success.

### Driving the Future: SRv6 as the Backbone of Mission-Critical Utility and Transportation Networks - BRKMSI-2386
Speakers: Zubair Khan (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362747349001qSFm
Utilities, transportation, and industrial IoT networks have long depended on siloed transport architectures, relying on legacy OTN and MPLS-TP technologies to carry mission-critical operational traffic such as tele-protection, GOOSE messaging, and WAMS in utility networks, and SCADA, level crossing control, and signaling in transportation. While these technologies have historically delivered the deterministic performance these environments require, they are aging, increasingly costly to operate, and ill-suited to the pace of modernization that critical infrastructure now demands. Segment Routing bridges this gap by enabling next-generation WAN transport while preserving the reliability, symmetry, and deterministic behavior that mission-critical applications depend on. This session explores how Cisco helps customers build modern transport solutions supporting both Layer 2 and Layer 3 VPN services across their most demanding environments. Attendees will see how Segment Routing combined with EVPN simplifies operations, with capabilities including Traffic Engineering and Traffic Protection enhancing network agility. The session will also cover specialized use cases leveraging circuit-style Segment Routing paths for deterministic, bidirectional, and latency-optimized connectivity for the most time-sensitive critical applications. Complementing these software innovations, the session will also cover Cisco's Routed Optical Networking architecture, which converges the IP and optical layers into a single unified infrastructure. By delivering coherent wavelengths directly from router ports using pluggable optics, it eliminates traditional multilayer complexity, reduces capital and operational expenditure, and future-proofs critical networks from end to end.

### Duo Identity Security: Protect your Users and Applications with SO MUCH more than MFA - BRKSEC-2879

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362781859001qsxZ

We will start with a new Duo account and build an Identity Security solution from the ground up. We'll configure exciting new features such as true passwordless, multiple IdP support in Duo SSO, user attribute syncing, passwordless windows logon, and other new Duo advancements utilizing Duo's new directory or IdP functionality.

### EVPN Campus: Design and Implementation - BRKENS-2041
Speakers: Sergey Nasonov (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362715332001qs9T
Ethernet VPN (EVPN) has become the de facto standard in the Service Provider and Data Center domains and has recently begun making inroads into campus networks, replacing MPLS VPN and traditional networking approaches. This session provides an in-depth exploration of the architecture and design choices involved in deploying an EVPN fabric in campus environments, along with step-by-step implementation guidance. The session focuses on delivering a fully functional EVPN campus configuration in IOS-XE, incorporating lessons learned from real-world EVPN deployments. The session will cover: BGP EVPN protocol operation Campus design choices Implementation guidance using IOS-XE CLI DHCP, Group-Policy, ESI Multihoming and Multicast operation in the EVPN overlays A baseline knowledge of networking (at the CCNP level) is expected from the audience.

### Empower Your Network with Segment Routing and MPLS Network Migration - BRKMPL-2177
Speakers: Thomas Peiyao Wang (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362741712001qsf8
This session will present how segment routing helps network simplification, SR and MPLS coexistence, SR migration, and SRv6 evolution. Session participants will also learn segment routing IPv6 fundamentals, path protection, on-demand SR policy, and the benefits of SRv6 evolution. Most importantly, participants will learn the steps on how to migrate from heavy signaling MPLS to a scalable and stateless SR/SRv6 network.

### Enabling SP Transformation Through Autonomous Networking - BRKSP-2133
Speakers: Daniele Ceccarelli (Cisco), Emerson Moura (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362785307001qHIc
Our market is undergoing the biggest transformation in the history of telecommunications. Increased competition and heightened expectation from their customers mean it is now time for some radical changes. This transformation gives rise to a more simplified, automated, programmable and intelligent network layer whose characteristics are encompassed in the industry drive around Autonomous Networking. A more detailed description of the objectives and desired outcomes of Autonomous Networks and how these contribute towards Cisco's three pillar vision around Network Simplification, Autonomics with Data Intelligence and Service and AI- Ready Connectivity. These three pillars will be explored, and customer examples and use-cases will be described, showing the Cisco differentiation in each area. The first two pillars will describe Cisco developments and innovations that enable the network simplification, and also delves into the evolution from automation to autonomics with increased importance of data intelligence. The third pillar will identify some of evolving "service factory" solutions which are being developed to address the direct needs of Service Providers' customers. The session will describe the Cisco SP Network as a Service (Naas) framework and some use-cases specifically around the B2B segment which includes aspects of Enterprise connectivity, trusted services and the integration of aaS-based services such as Cisco Mobile Services Platform (MSP). The final section will describe industry initiatives with a description of the two catalyst projects use-cases which are currently being driven with our customers and partners through TM Forum. The first is Game X : Elevating the customer experience via self-governed converged networks with operators including STC, Colt and Verizon. The second one is "Accelerating Dynamic Network Marketplaces" which includes our Network-as-a-Service (NaaS) framework.

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362690420001qzci
The last few years has seen major changes in the way we use our workplaces, resulting in workplace users struggling to find and effectively use available workspaces and workplace admins needing to provide enhanced workplace experiences, while optimised the use of these workspaces. This is leading to changes in the capabilities and requirements we now have of our modern workplaces.
