Alleviating workspace inefficiencies through adhoc spaces usage methods and intelligent room booking enhancements and automation. Solving for the common room challenges with new RoomOS26 AI directed experiences. While capturing extensive workspace analytics and insights including comprehensive room occupancy data and environmental metrics for continual workspace optimization.

### End to End Segmentation with Cisco Catalyst SD-WAN and ISE - BRKENT-2166
Speakers: Steven Penland (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362724058001qYFa
With the increased sophistication and number of network attacks, controlling the ability for threats to move laterally throughout the network is more important than ever. In this session you will learn how: The combination of SD-WAN, ISE, and Secure Access provides a secure networking architecture that allows for flexible end-to-end macro and microsegmentation of users, devices, and applications. ISE can assign secure group tags (SGTs) based on user/device identification, SD-WAN propagates those SGTs across the WAN, and policy can be created to control access to resources. Integration with ISE allows security policy in SD-WAN to match on user identity instead of traditional attributes like IP addresses. This session will be targeted for an audience with a baseline understanding of SD-WAN and segmentation concepts. The session will consist of a combination of presentation and demonstration. The presentation will focus on the details of the different integrations and segmentation options. The demo will focus on how to enable the integration, enable SGT propagation, and build policy.

### End-to-End Micro-Segmentation and L4-7 Service Redirection in Cisco NX-OS VXLAN EVPN Fabrics with GPO and Nexus Dashboard - BRKDCN-2909
Speakers: Alessandro De Prato (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362706500001qh77
Macro- and micro-segmentation are foundational design strategies for securing modern data centers in response to the proliferation of attacks across both east-west and north-south traffic flows. This session provides a comprehensive overview of Cisco NX-OS VXLAN EVPN fabric segmentation capabilities using the VXLAN Group Policy Option (GPO). Attendees will learn how endpoints and networks are classified into security groups, and how policies can be used to enforce controlled communication between them, independent of their location. The session examines both the architectural foundations and the practical implementation of GPO within the VXLAN EVPN fabric. You will gain a clear understanding of how the control and data planes operate, how policies are programmed and enforced across the fabric, and how these mechanisms enable a consistent zero-trust communication model. In addition to the theory, we will talk about real deployment scenarios and design considerations to help you confidently implement micro-segmentation in production environments, including the integration of Layer 4-7 services. Finally, attendees will discover how Nexus Dashboard streamlines the automation of these segmentation capabilities across the VXLAN EVPN fabric.

### End-to-End Segmentation Made Simple with Cisco ISE Integrations - CTF-2008

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362812162001q3wS

This session will provide a technical walkthrough of how Cisco Identity Services Engine enables end-to-end segmentation through the implementation of TrustSec. Attendees will learn how to integrate ISE with various technologies, including CMDBs, firewalls, and CSPs, to build a unified policy framework. The session will include a detailed demonstration of TrustSec in action, showcasing practical configurations and real-world use cases. Whether managing traditional IT networks or hybrid cloud deployments, participants will leave with a clear understanding of how to design and deploy a segmented infrastructure using Cisco ISE and its ecosystem integrations.

### Energy Management and Sustainability with Cisco Campus Smart Switches and Catalyst 9000 - BRKENS-2617

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362717517001qo6c

Cisco's new C9000 smart switches are built for this shift - engineered from the ground up to be sustainable, efficient, and power?aware. With integrated smart?power technology, they function as intelligent energy?management platforms that monitor, control, and optimize power consumption across the enterprise while maintaining secure, high?performance connectivity. Join this session to dive deep into the new smart power feature on smart switches. learn how the smart power protocol/framework embedded in IOS lets network infrastructure measure, monitor, and control power consumption of connected devices (PoE endpoints, switches, routers, even servers) through the network fabric itself and how its APIs can be levered by agentic AI

Speakers: Omkar Nimbalkar (IBM), Bala Rajaraman (IBM), Bina Hallman (IBM)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362854336001qHNZ

This session shows how participants can shift from reactive support to a proactive, AI-driven support strategy that transforms traditional maintenance into measurable operational outcomes. ### Engineering the Future of Work with AI - PP-1205
Speakers: St.John Johnson (Cisco), Scott Herpolsheimer (Cisco), Greg Tesimale (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780412378679001Hnl3
According to the World Economic Forum, AI will disrupt 22% of today's jobs by 2030, yet most companies lack a clear roadmap for what's ahead. In this session, you'll get a behind-the-scenes look at how Cisco is transforming work and redesigning roles in the age of AI to drive growth and innovation and how your organization can do the same. Whether you're leading a business, a team, or a project, this session will help you turn AI insight into action and give you practical guidance to redesign roles, quantify productivity gains, and lead your workforce confidently into the AI era.

### Engineering the Secure, Connected Roadway: The Playbook for Building Smarter, Safer Cities - BRKIIOT-2005

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780412369615001HEgb

Discover how Cisco partnered with Nashville DOT to build a secure, custom-designed network foundation. Cisco Edge Intelligence was deployed to acquire data from both modern and legacy ITS devices such as traffic controllers, RSUs, and LiDAR sensors.

### Enhancing Cloud-Native Security: Isovalent's CNI Innovations in Kubernetes - BRKSEC-2891
Speakers: Dirk Stoeckmann (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362782079001qWLw
In this presentation, we introduce Isovalent's products Cilium, Tetragon, and the underlying eBPF technology. We explain how these tools address the network and security challenges of modern cloud-native applications. Cilium revolutionizes networking in Kubernetes clusters, while Tetragon provides deep insights into the security and behavior of applications. Both are based on eBPF technology, which allows dynamic code insertion into the Linux kernel without the need for recompilation. We will also provide an outlook on how Cisco Hypershield will utilize these products as part of a decentralized security architecture in the future.

### Enhancing Network Operations with Observability: Strategies for Real-Time Insight - BRKOPS-2418
Speakers: Quinn Snyder (Cisco), Kareem Iskander (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362759893001qpL7
In the rapidly evolving landscape of network management, achieving operational excellence requires advanced tools and strategies. This session delves into how Splunk's observability solutions empower Network Operations (NetOps) teams with the technical capabilities to maintain high-performing, reliable networks. Attendees will explore how Splunk collects and analyzes multiple streams of data, including syslog, Model-Driven Telemetry (MDT), NetFlow/IPFIX, and SNMP from network devices. This comprehensive data ingestion allows for real-time visualization of network issues, enabling NetOps teams to identify traffic patterns, latency, and performance bottlenecks swiftly. Additionally, leveraging Splunk's platform for automated remediation via automation webhooks offers a transformative approach to proactive network management. Discover how to utilize Splunk's machine learning algorithms for predictive analytics, accelerating incident resolution and driving continuous improvements in network operations.

Speakers: Aaron Cespedes Vega (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362788965001qdtp
With the rapid growth of bring-your-own-device and IoT adoption, real-time network visibility and security have become essential for today's enterprise environments. As organizations strive to protect their infrastructure and ensure compliance, Switch Integrated Security Features (SISF) like IP Device Tracking (IPDT) on Cisco Catalyst 9000 Series switches play a pivotal role in identifying and monitoring network endpoints. You'll leave this session with ideas to help you: Understand how IP Device Tracking integrates with Switch Integrated Security Features to enhance endpoint visibility and security Deploy and configure IP Device Tracking effectively on Cisco Catalyst 9000 Series switches in diverse enterprise scenarios Troubleshoot and optimize IP Device Tracking using real-world examples and live demonstrations This session is designed for network engineers and security professionals with an intermediate understanding of Layer 2 and Layer 3 switching concepts. The session includes live demos and customer case studies to provide practical insights and actionable techniques for maximizing security and operational efficiency.

### Enhancing Observability with the Power of AI - WOSPAR-2009
Speakers: Valentina Dunoski (IR)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362858797001qZSA
In today's fast-paced business environment, reliable communication is essential. IR Collaborate enables organizations to proactively manage and monitor their unified communications and contact center infrastructure, ensuring seamless connectivity across teams and customer touchpoints. Real-time monitoring and advanced troubleshooting give IT teams the visibility and tools they need to resolve issues quickly, reducing downtime and keeping operations running smoothly. By optimizing communication systems, organizations can enhance user experience, strengthen performance, and improve overall efficiency. Join this session to explore IR's latest innovations, including Iris - our natural-language AI assistant - and Prognosis Elevate, a fully hosted platform option that simplifies management while delivering peak performance.

### Enhancing Trust and Security in Service Provider Networks - BRKSPG-2868
Speakers: Rakesh Kandula (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362786248001q0GV
In today's rapidly evolving threat landscape, networks face increasingly sophisticated attacks, making it essential to establish trust from the ground up and maintain it throughout a device's lifecycle. This session explores the foundational trust mechanisms underpinning Cisco routers and outlines security best practices designed to defend against advanced threats. Considering recent high-profile incidents such as Salt Typhoon, operational security hardening has become more critical than ever. While the session primarily focuses on features available in IOS-XR routers, these principles are broadly applicable across Cisco's product portfolio. Additionally, the session will address emerging risks to network security posed by quantum computing, as well as the post-quantum cryptography (PQC) transition options available today.

### Enterprise Encryption: Good, Bad, and Absent - CISCOU-2015
Speakers: David McGrew (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362801078001qUrB
Networked information systems rely on encryption to protect communication, but that encryption is not always as secure as required, is not always used where needed, and is sometimes used by attackers as a place to hide. The upcoming threat of quantum computers is driving the adoption of quantum-safe cryptographic algorithms, while legacy systems may still be using cryptography that is vulnerable today. Worse, they may be failing to use cryptography at all, exposing sensitive data such as credentials, as was exploited in some recent attack campaigns. This session explains what encryption algorithms and protocols are good, which ones are bad, and how the latter can be identified in network traffic. It also covers how the Cisco Secure Firewall Encrypted Visibility Engine (EVE) detects malware communication in encrypted traffic, and summarizes ongoing work in the industry to discover and report weak encryption.

### Enterprise Multicast Technologies for the 21st Century - BRKENT-2455
Speakers: Peter Paluch (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362725466001qAa7
Multicast is a key functionality of today's IP networks, providing the foundation for applications such as IPTV, video/audio streaming, reimaging multiple hosts at once, or other one/many-to-many communication services. Aside from these, multicast is also an important component in contemporary overlay solutions such as SDA or BGP EVPN where it optimizes the delivery of multi-destination traffic in the overlay, avoiding headend replication. Having a solid understanding of multicast protocols and operations is therefore highly advantageous. This session offers an insight into key multicast technologies relevant to present-day enterprises, including Internet Group Membership Protocol (IGMP), Multicast Listener Discovery (MLD), Protocol Independent Multicast - Sparse Mode in Any Source Multicast and Source Specific Multicast modes (PIM-SM, ASM and SSM), Rendezvous Point discovery and redundancy, as well as optimizing and securing the multicast operation. IPv6 and IPv4 multicast will be covered equally.

### Enterprise Network Automation Strategy, Framework, Best Practices and case studies - BRKATO-2107
Speakers: Anis Edavalath (Cisco), Azharuddin Mohammed (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362677965001qjTJ
This session will focus on building an Enterprise automation Journey Map for customers . All the component elements required in building a secure automation environment covering different technology domains like Datacenter, Campus , WAN , Security and Cloud will be evaluated. A recommendation based on the real time customer success stories will be shared allowing the attendees to gain insights to design a unique automation strategy. We will cover a reference architecture framework, which can be consumed by customers to bring on Infrastructure as code approach in to the enterprise Intent Based network

### Enterprise Wi?Fi for Real?Time Collaboration: Design, Deployment, and Optimization - CTF-2012
Speakers: Wellington Assis (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362813051001qczS
As organizations bring more employees back to the office and hybrid work becomes the norm, reliable Wi?Fi is essential for voice and video collaboration. Applications such as Webex, Microsoft Teams, and Zoom place strict requirements on latency, roaming performance, and airtime efficiency, and poorly designed wireless networks can quickly impact user experience. This session explores practical strategies for designing, deploying, and optimizing enterprise Wi?Fi networks to support real?time collaboration. Attendees will learn key RF design considerations, roaming behavior, QoS and traffic prioritization techniques, and best practices for modern Wi?Fi environments. The session will also highlight common causes of voice and video quality issues over Wi?Fi and provide practical approaches to troubleshooting and improving collaboration performance.

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362683988001qmT6
Join us for a practitioner-focused session, drawing on a real-world Cisco-on-Cisco case study, to explore best practices for designing and deploying a non-blocking Ethernet fabric tailored to AI workloads. This session will unpack the design and technology stack of Cisco Data Center Nexus fabrics, highlighting the use of the Nexus 9000 with ND solution and the cloud-managed Nexus HyperFabric AI solution. We'll discuss critical configuration considerations, share insights on overcoming challenges encountered during infrastructure build-out, and demonstrate how to deliver robust GPU compute environments optimized for demanding AI workloads. Attendees will gain practical guidance and actionable strategies for architecting high-performance, scalable data center networks to support next-generation AI applications.

### Evolution of AI in Telecom Service Provider Networks - The Emergence of Embedded Network Inference - BRKMSI-1004
Speakers: Mukul Golash (Cisco), Shahid Ajmeri (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362743493001q78P
Telecom networks are entering a new phase of AI-driven transformation, progressing beyond traditional analytics toward deeply integrated, distributed intelligence. Early AI deployments centered on automated network operations, where ML models enhanced fault prediction, capacity planning, and policy-driven closed-loop control. This foundation is now being expanded through Agentic AI, enabling intent interpretation, multi-step reasoning, and autonomous workflow execution across orchestration and assurance systems. At the same time, operators are adopting distributed AI architectures, pushing inference to edge locations, into RAN subsystems, transport policy decisions, and cloud-native network functions. This distribution reduces decision latency and improves real-time visibility across dynamic, high-throughput environments. These advancements are converging in the emergence of embedded AI inference directly inside routing platforms. With the integration of specialised acceleration blocks, high-frequency telemetry pipelines, and onboard model runtimes, routers can execute microsecond-level inference for anomaly detection, path optimisation, and traffic engineering. This capability moves intelligence from external controllers into the fabric itself, enabling AI-augmented routing and more autonomous, responsive networks. Together, automation, agentic frameworks, and distributed inference are driving the shift toward AI-native telecom infrastructures capable of continuous self-optimization and real-time adaptive control.

### Evolution of Agentic AI for Enterprise - BRKPAR-1004
Speakers: Chris Marriott (NVIDIA)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362763891001q0Ea
Agentic AI has moved from research curiosity to operational reality, but what does that actually look like inside an enterprise? This session follows an enterprise's journey integrating AI agents into everyday IT and business workflows, covering the architectural decisions, integration challenges, and governance considerations that made it possible. Expect real examples, honest lessons, and a practical look at what it takes to move from proof-of-concept to production at scale.

### Exploring the Inner Workings of OSPF - BRKENT-2088
Speakers: Elvin Emilio Arias Soto (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362722674001qRUu
Open Shortest Path First (OSPF) is a link-state routing protocol commonly deployed in enterprise, data center, and service provider environments. Thanks to its exceptional flexibility and feature richness, OSPF underpins routed IPv4 and IPv6 networks all around the world. This session aims at dispelling the perceived complexity of OSPF and demonstrating its simple-to-understand foundations. We will look at selected in-depth protocol details, including the OSPF packet types, adjacency formation, link-state database synchronization, link-state advertisements, use of areas and their types, and path selection, making it easy to understand OSPF comprehensively.

### Exposing Hidden Weaknesses: How Identity Attack Paths Circumvent Your Best Defenses - WOSPAR-2057
Speakers: Jared Atkinson (SpecterOps), Aaron Woland (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362869835001qD18
Even with top-rated security tools in place, attackers still succeed because they exploit unseen attack paths that link identities, systems, and permissions across your environment. In this session, learn how BloodHound Enterprise, paired with Cisco Duo, shines lights on these hidden routes, how AI is accelerating attacker capabilities, and how you can use BloodHound analytics with Splunk to shift from reactive detection to continuous observability and proactive prevention. BloodHound Enterprise is a product of SpecterOps and is a SolutionsPlus partner.

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362688431001qCm3
This session provides a deep dive into the key architectural considerations for Cisco Expressway. ### Failure Patterns in Modern Distributed Systems: Lessons from Major Internet Outages - BRKOPS-2000
Speakers: Mike Hicks (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362756585001qYP0
As applications evolve into increasingly distributed architectures with specialised services and complex dependency chains, traditional approaches to outage detection and root cause analysis are failing. Through analysis of major incidents across cloud providers, collaboration platforms, and content delivery networks, this session examines three critical failure patterns that explain why outage symptoms increasingly appear disconnected from their root causes. We'll explore how service features designed for coordination accidentally become failure distribution mechanisms, why systems can appear completely healthy while core functions silently break, and how well-intentioned configuration changes cascade through interconnected systems with devastating effects. Crucially, we'll show how the ThousandEyes MCP server - and the analytical framework it enables - was used to surface, interrogate, and codify these patterns from real incident data, turning raw telemetry into repeatable forensic methodology. Through detailed case study walkthroughs, attendees will see both the patterns themselves and the investigative process behind them, learning practical strategies to detect and prevent similar failures in their own environments. This 90-minute session is led by the Principal Analyst from ThousandEyes' Internet Intelligence team, who leads outage analysis and publishes findings through The Internet Report. Attendees will leave with actionable frameworks for identifying hidden dependencies, detecting functional failures before users do, and preventing configuration changes from cascading into major incidents.

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362828194001qWHJ

Cisco and Nutanix provide a powerful path forward, combining enterprise-class infrastructure with the Nutanix Hybrid Cloud Platform to deliver scale, simplicity, flexibility, and choice. In this session, we explore how Cisco infrastructure integrated with Nutanix enables modern platforms across the data center, AI environments, and distributed edge locations. We will highlight architectures including Cisco HCI with Nutanix, FlashStack with Nutanix, Cisco AI POD with Nutanix, and Unified Edge with Nutanix. Attendees will gain insight into deployment models and how Cisco and Nutanix together provide a flexible foundation for modern applications, AI workloads, and hybrid cloud operations from core to edge.

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362812831001qa1B

Building on this foundation, we'll cover advanced architectures beyond the wizard's scope, including BGP default route and multi-path, HTTP-based Path Monitoring, PBR and how traffic flows from traditional and VRF-enabled spokes to datacenter and to internet resources.

### First VPC to Global Enterprise Network: Cisco Networking on AWS - BRKPAR-2008

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362766580001qCln
As enterprises move critical workloads to cloud, network teams face a gap between cloud-native constructs and the control they depend on. Participants leave with: (1) a clear model of AWS networking from a single VPC to multi-region enterprise design, (2) deployment patterns for Cisco Secure SD-WAN that can replace MPLS at up to 66% cost savings, and (3) a validated reference architecture connecting branch, data center, and cloud workloads. Includes walkthroughs proven across retail, healthcare, and financial services. Foundational networking knowledge recommended.

### Fortify Your Network: Proactive Security Through Advanced Configuration Analytics - CISCOU-1017
Speakers: Julia Palomino (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362798298001q9Kx
Network outages and security breaches frequently originate from unnoticed configuration changes, whether introduced by well-meaning field technicians or malicious actors. In just 45 minutes, discover how advanced analytical methods revolutionize network integrity and security. This session will explore the power of Machine Learning-driven pattern recognition to automatically learn your network's 'normal' configuration states, moving beyond complex golden templates. Through practical examples, we'll illustrate how these techniques precisely identify deviations, such as those made by a sophisticated "Salt Typhoon"-like attack, pinpointing exactly where they occurred. The demonstration will also highlight how AI agents can interact with these analytical insights to streamline operational responses. Learn to proactively detect stealthy threats and accidental misconfigurations, drastically reduce your Mean Time To Resolution (MTTR), and fortify your network against both internal and external risks. Invest 45 minutes to gain a critical, cutting-edge advantage in network defense.

### Fortify the Grid Edge: Reinventing Substation Security with Cisco SD-WAN and Industrial Routing - BRKIIOT-2017
Speakers: Rodney Smith (Cisco), Nipun Jain (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780412370319001HYKS
As power grids evolve with digital transformation, substations - the heart of modern utilities - are becoming prime targets for cyber threats. Operational Technology (OT) systems once isolated are now converging with IT networks, exposing critical protection relays, IEDs, and SCADA systems to sophisticated attacks that can disrupt energy distribution and safety operations. Join this session to explore how Cisco's Next-Generation Firewall (NGFW) architecture is redefining substation security with deep OT protocol visibility, zero-trust segmentation, and AI-powered threat detection. Learn how Cisco's ruggedized platforms with integrated NGFW capabilities - engineered for extreme environments - secure Modbus, DNP3, and IEC-61850 communications while enabling compliance with NERC CIP and evolving grid security mandates. Through real-world use cases, we'll demonstrate how utilities are deploying NGFWs alongside Cisco's AI Canvas, ThousandEyes agents, and quantum-ready security to build resilient, self-defending substations. You'll walk away with best practices to protect mission-critical assets, simplify network operations, and future-proof your substation security posture for the next decade.

### Foundation Models for NetOps: From Time Series and Log Reasoning to Closed-Loop Operations - BRKOBS-1001
Speakers: Nick Ma (Cisco), Joseph Ross (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362750279001qMgx
Modern networks generate massive streams of logs and telemetry, yet most NetOps teams rely on manual sifting and reactive troubleshooting. This session shows how Cisco Time Series and Log Reasoning Foundation Models transform raw syslogs, events, and metrics into accurate, streamlined, and easy-to-use closed-loop network operations. You'll learn how these foundation models power predictive forecasting and anomaly detection, automate root cause analysis across network telemetry and application performance monitoring (APM), and enable AI-driven workflows that detect issues earlier, reduce noise, and recommend next actions in clear natural language. You'll leave understanding how foundation models can move your team from reactive firefighting to proactive NetOps. Recommended for intermediate to advanced users familiar with network operations and interested in applying AI and machine learning to operational workflows.

### From 10 to 10,000 : Hyper-scaling Meraki networks without templates - BRKOPS-2269
Speakers: Kenneth Pinto (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362757897001qqoo
With Meraki Networks, template-based approaches shine in organizations that are highly distributed with similar needs and applications. The cookie cutter scales quickly to operationalize your design. However, if you combine the proliferation of applications, disparate network needs and a security architecture that begs for customization, templates will quickly limit you. Looking to break away from Meraki templates? Do you want a highly customized network architecture that can scale in seconds? Do you despise the rigidity of auto IP addressing or local overrides? Here you will dive deep into concepts that help orchestrate Meraki networks at scale - Scaling Deployment : Uncover techniques using API, Cisco Workflows, Unified Branch and Branch-as-Code to deploy hundreds of Meraki full-stack sites in minutes - Scaling Policy : Distribute policy to sites using Cisco Access Manager, Organization-wide group policy and automated switching Smartports - Configuration Customization and Reporting : Learn to develop and run custom workflows for network reporting using agenticAI - Case studies and sample API scripts to get you started Join us in this template-free transition away from uniformity toward autonomy!

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362854793001qtxA
As data center networks scale to support AI workloads and ever?increasing fiber counts, operational complexity at the physical layer has quietly become a source of risk. But a new solution offers a colorful alternative to tedious and time-consuming installations. The result is a physical layer that actively supports reliability, scalability, and speed as networks continue to grow.

### From Feeder to Field Device: Automating the Distribution Grid with Cisco Industrial Networking - BRKIIOT-2018

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780412370551001Hfk4
Utilities are under pressure to improve reliability, integrate distributed energy resources, and operate with greater visibility at the grid edge - all while keeping costs and cyber risk under control. This session explores how Cisco industrial networking platforms enable end-to-end distribution automation, from feeder protection and FLISR schemes to capacitor bank control, recloser automation, and advanced sensors. Whether you're an OT engineer, network architect, or operations leader, you'll come away with practical best practices for building a scalable, secure, and automation-ready distribution grid.

### From Global Scale to Zero Downtime: SD-Access Lessons from Siemens and a renowned Healthcare Provider - BRKENS-1803
Speakers: Guru Apparassou (Cisco), Manfred Brabec (Cisco), Radu Niculita (Siemens AG), Mrunal Trivedi (UNIVERSITY OF CALIFORNIA IRVINE MEDICAL CENTER)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362714926001q8Ts
In this session, Siemens shares how it is transforming a fragmented global network into a unified, policy-driven architecture using Cisco SD-Access-spanning 350+ sites, 118 countries, and 2M+ endpoints. Learn how SD-Access enables consistent segmentation, Zero Trust access, and simplified operations across IT and OT, providing the foundation for scalable, secure growth in one of the world's largest enterprise networks. In contrast, a renowned Healthcare Provider brings a high-stakes perspective- executing an SD-Access deployment across four live hospitals under a fixed deadline, with no rollback and zero tolerance for disruption. Facing extreme constraints, including no pre-discovery and ~24-hour cutover windows, they successfully delivered a complete transformation with zero patient impact . Together, these stories showcase how SD-Access delivers both global scale and mission-critical reliability, offering a proven blueprint for modernizing campus networks

### From Incident to Answer: Rethinking Network Observability with AI-Driven Workflows - WOSPAR-2032
Speakers: Erik Rudin (cPacket)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362864132001qilb
Every network team faces the same paradox: the data they need already exists, but is buried in trillions of packets moving across their network. Yet when applications degrade, latency spikes, or audits demand evidence, the gap between incident and answers can take hours (or days), require specialized expertise, and involve multiple tools. The challenge isn't collecting data. It's turning available data into clear, defensible answers. In complex environments, packet-level telemetry provides the most complete record of what actually happened, but it's often difficult to operationalize at scale. This session explores how AI-driven observability workflows - combining large language models (LLMs) with deep networking expertise and direct access to packet data - are changing how teams detect and investigate issues. These approaches help teams move faster while ensuring every conclusion is grounded in verifiable evidence and remains under operator control. Through real-world examples from financial services and healthcare, we'll show how organizations are reducing escalations, standardizing investigations, and extending expert-level troubleshooting across their teams. For organizations with strict data sovereignty, compliance, or air-gapped requirements, we'll also examine architectures that keep sensitive telemetry entirely within your environment. Attendees will leave with a framework for evaluating AI observability approaches and a clear model for delivering faster, explainable answers - without sacrificing control, compliance, or trust. Target Audience: Network Engineering Architects, Network Operations Leaders, Infrastructure Directors and VPs, and Application Performance teams supporting mission-critical workloads at high speed and scale.

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362810377001qHyb

Let Python have your back: implement automated testing into your workflows. ### From NetDevOps to AI-Assisted Operations: Bridging Code and Intelligence - BRKOPS-2357
Speakers: Patrick Mosimann (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362758761001qlQL
As AI reshapes network operations, the challenge is no longer automation - it is control. Many environments already rely on NetDevOps practices such as Source of Truth, CI/CD pipelines, and validation frameworks. But introducing AI adds a new dimension: non-deterministic behavior interacting with deterministic systems. This session explores how to safely evolve from code-centric NetDevOps to AI-assisted, agentic operations - without losing control. We start with the foundation: building a deterministic system using NetBox as a Source of Truth, automated execution with Ansible and GitLab CI/CD, and validation with pyATS to ensure predictable outcomes. From there, we introduce the AI bridge: using the Model Context Protocol (MCP) to connect AI agents to real network systems in a controlled and structured way. You will also learn how to guide AI behavior using structured workflows, turning runbooks into repeatable, context-aware processes, and how to scale this approach without overwhelming systems or models. Finally, we compare build vs. consume approaches, including low-code workflow options, to help you decide how to adopt AI in your own environment. What you will learn: Why AI requires a deterministic foundation in network operations How to connect AI to systems safely using MCP How to structure AI behavior using workflows instead of free-form prompts How to maintain control, validation, and repeatability in AI-assisted operations Practical patterns to scale AI integration across multiple systems Target audience: Intermediate-level engineers and architects working with NetDevOps who want to understand how to introduce AI into real-world network operations in a controlled and practical way.

This session focuses on building hybrid mesh firewalls using Cisco Secure Workload, Firepower Threat Defense (FTD), Secure Cloud Controls, and Multicloud Defense. We'll present a reference architecture that demonstrates how these technologies interoperate to deliver unified security policies, automated threat response, and seamless segmentation across hybrid deployments. Through technical deep dives and practical demos, you'll learn how to: Architect hybrid mesh firewalls that connect Cisco Secure Workload and FTD for end-to-end east-west and north-south protection Extend consistent firewall and microsegmentation policies into AWS, Azure, and other clouds with Secure Cloud Controls and Multicloud Defense Achieve unified visibility, centralized policy management, and automated incident response Ideal for architects and engineers, this session will equip you with the design patterns and integration strategies needed to secure distributed workloads using Cisco's latest security innovations.

You'll see how this pattern reduces hallucinated configs, improves code review, and fits cleanly with Cisco automation and emerging AI certification topics - so you can design safer AI workflows, not just faster scripts.

### Future-Proofing Cisco Collaboration: Navigating the EKU Certificate Sunset - Ensuring Resilient Security and Uninterrupted Service - TACCOL-2023
Speakers: Victor Rolandson (Cisco), Randy Valverde (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780412381938001HO2y
"Future-Proofing Cisco Collaboration: Navigating the EKU Certificate Sunset - Ensuring Resilient Security and Uninterrupted Service : As Cisco continues to evolve its security posture across the entire Collaboration portfolio, the upcoming sunsetting of the Client Authentication (Client Auth) Extended Key Usage (EKU) in certificates, has become a critical focal point for administrators. This transition is not limited to Expressway; it impacts the broader ecosystem, including Cisco Unified Communications Manager (CUCM), IM and Presence, Unity Connection and Cisco Unified Border Element (CUBE). Understanding the technical implications of this change is essential to maintaining secure, uninterrupted communication services. In this session, you will gain the technical expertise required to future-proof your Collaboration environment. You'll leave this session with actionable insights to help you: -Analyze the impact of EKU changes across your entire Collaboration infrastructure. -Identify potential points of failure in TLS handshakes and service-to-service authentication. -Implement proactive certificate lifecycle management strategies to avoid service outages. This session provides a look at the ""why"" behind the EKU sunset, the technical mechanics of how EKU functions within a certificate, and the specific risks to your mTLS connections without the Client EKU. We will walk through the problem definition, evaluate temporary workarounds, and provide a clear roadmap for the permanent solution, broken down by software version requirements. Whether you are managing a single Expressway node or a multi-cluster enterprise deployment, this session will ensure your infrastructure remains resilient against evolving security standards."

### Gain Scalable, Deep Visibility into Network Traffic with Cisco Nexus Dashboard Data Broker - BRKDCN-1614
Speakers: Mallikarjuna Rao Mudigonda (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362701413001qh6f
Visibility into network and application traffic has become increasingly important in our modern data centers. Network visibility is integral to network security, compliance, troubleshooting, and capacity planning. Custom monitoring solutions cannot cope with the explosive growth of data center networks. In order to match the port density and port capabilities of the data center, monitoring networks must also leverage those same switches used in the production network. Cisco Nexus Data Broker (NDB) is an automated, intelligent packet brokering solution that can scale with your data center. NDB provides a software-defined, programmable solution that can aggregate, filter, and replicate network traffic using SPAN or optical TAPs for network monitoring and visibility. This solution offers a simple, scalable, and cost-effective solution that is well suited to customers who have a need for monitoring large amounts of traffic or business-critical traffic. With NDB being avaiable in the Nexus Dashboard(ND) is icing on the cake, this enable customers to adopt the product quick and get started with DC visibility with speed of a thaught. This session will cover the following: Introduction to Nexus Dashboard Data Broker TAP and SPAN aggregation use case. Integration with other Cisco products Demonstration of the NDDB interface Product Demo- Demonstrate Nexus Data Broker using ND( Nexus Dashboard)

We will explore how GenAI can reduce the time spent collecting and analyzing network configurations, operational state, and logs, helping you uncover issues faster and gain better insights from large volumes of data. You will see how real-time, MCP-based agents can use tools and live data to support guided troubleshooting workflows while incorporating your own best practices. Prior experience with GenAI and LLMs is beneficial, but not required. Demos and complete open-source sample code will be included. Don't miss this opportunity to reimagine network troubleshooting with GenAI Your Way.

Yet your monitoring is stuck in 1999, with a classic mix of SNMP interface graphs, a pile of logs, and emails nobody reads. Is anybody even catching SNMP traps anymore? Become the hero of your network's story. In this session, you will learn how to utilize the APIs already available in your network and security infrastructure to access troubleshooting data and telemetry from multiple sources, combine them into meaningful Splunk dashboards, and leverage AI to accelerate your learning journey. This session will take you on a cross-domain adventure full of demos, covering how Cisco solutions such as Catalyst Center, Meraki, ISE, Secure Firewall, and Splunk can be brought together for easier monitoring and troubleshooting.

### Getting Started in Open-Source: A Practical On-Ramp to Making Meaningful Contributions to Your Favorite Projects - CISCOU-1063

We'll walk through how open-source contributions actually happen: choosing approachable issues, communicating with maintainers, navigating community norms, and submitting your first pull request. Whether you're aiming to improve the software you use in production or grow as an engineer, you'll leave with actionable steps to make your first (or next) contribution.

### Ghost in the Machine: Strategies for Identifying and Managing Silent Hosts in SDA - TACENT-2036

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780412384468001HzIC
Modern enterprise environments face a growing challenge: the increasing presence of ""Silent hosts"", endpoints that communicate only on-demand. These devices often struggle with SD-Access fabric authentication and connectivity, leading to network disruptions and control-plane registration issues. To solve this, within SD-Access fabric we offer a streamlined approach to network management. You'll leave this session with ideas to help you simplify the planning of Silent-Host detection features such as IP Directed Broadcast, Layer 2 Flooding or EEM Scripts on Cisco Catalyst Center and optimize authentication templates to enable Wake-on-LAN functionality for secured ports. ### Goodbye Legacy, the Move to an IPv6-Only Enterprise - BRKENT-2008
Speakers: David Prall (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362721988001qXWd
This session is for those who currently have IPv6 deployed in a dual-stack environment and are looking to transition from dual stack, to IPv6-only. With perhaps the middle step of Pv6-mostly. IPv6 was chosen as the recommendation for the IP next-generation protocol in RFC 1752 in January 1995. Many RFCs since have delivered updates and enhancements. The specification RFC 1883 was published in December 1995, becoming RFC 8200 (STD 86) in July 2017, making IPv6 an Internet Standard. Just as we've said goodbye to legacy protocols IPX/SPX, DECnet, AppleTalk, and others, it is now time to say goodbye to IPv4. Many governments have mandated the transition to IPv6-Only. The US Government issued Memorandum M-21-07 in November 2020, titled "Completing the Transition to Internet Protocol Version 6 (IPv6)." Germany 2030, China 2030, Botswana 2030, Czech Republic 2032 In this session, we will focus on the move from dual-stacked IPv4 and IPv6 enterprise infrastructure to an IPv6-only infrastructure. What do we need to know before we make the move? What about IPv4-only enclaves? We will look at NetFlow as the picture of truth and NAT64/DNS64 as our transition mechanism.

This self-maintaining knowledge system allows agents to adapt as networks and operational practices evolve. Together, disciplined orchestration and continuous knowledge evolution make autonomous AIOps reliable - not just at launch, but at enterprise scale over time.

### IPv6 Security in the Local Area with First Hop Security - BRKENT-3002
Speakers: Eric Vyncke (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362726126001quL4
A big difference in the security between IPv4 and IPv6 is at the layer-2/layer-3 interaction, as DHCP is optional in IPv6 and ARP is replaced by Neighbor Discovery Protocol (NDP). Legacy IPv4 attacks such as ARP or DHCP spoofing have their equivalent in IPv6. Cisco has developed techniques for many years to secure this interaction in the local area (WLAN, LAN, SD-Access, Meraki, ACI, etc). This session explains the attacks and how Cisco can help protect your networks with short demonstrations. A good knowledge of IPv6 is required, especially NDP.

### IPv6 Transition Technologies and IPv4aaS - BRKIPV-2282
Speakers: Raul Ricano (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362739274001qq05
As the world rapidly exhausts IPv4 addresses, service providers are increasingly deploying IPv6-only networks. This shift presents a significant challenge for IT teams needing to maintain connectivity for legacy IPv4 applications and services, while embracing the future of networking. This session will explore the critical IPv6 transition mechanisms on IOS XE and those now available in the Cisco Meraki dashboard. Attendees will gain a comprehensive understanding of how technologies such as Network Address Translation 64 (NAT64), 464XLAT, IP in IP, Dual-Stack Lite (DS-Lite), and Mapping of Address and Port with Encapsulation/Translation (MAP-E/MAP-T) function to provide connectivity. You will also learn how to select and configure the appropriate transition strategy for your specific network environment, simplifying what has traditionally been a complex undertaking and ensuring you are IPv6 ready. This is an intermediate-level session. A solid understanding of fundamental networking concepts, including IP families, addressing and routing is recommended.

### IPv6 uSID Implementation in Action: From CLI to Closed-Loop Automation - BRKMSI-2010
Speakers: Pradip Mule (Cisco), Nikhil Ariscrisna (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362745488001q0IN
This session dives into IPv6 uSID concepts and demonstrates three practical ways to implement them. Manual Implementation with CLI NetDevOps Implementation with Cisco Network Services Orchestrator (NSO) SDN Implementation with Cisco Crosswork Network Controller (CNC) We will start with the fundamentals of SRv6 uSID and IOS XR-based configurations. Using NetDevOps principles, we'll show how NSO can accelerate deployment through automation. Finally, CNC will highlight software-defined provisioning, live visualization, and closed-loop network automation for advanced uSID use cases. A concise comparison of these approaches will help participants identify the most efficient and scalable method for their networks.

### IPv6:: It's Happening! - BRKIPV-2191
Speakers: Nathan Sherrard (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362738828001qY6P
IPv6 has had a long and winding history, with many organizations ignoring it. Yet in recent years, its use has exploded, and we are now at a point where everyone needs to take it seriously and get ready for an IPv6-only future. If you're looking at the steps toward enabling IPv6, and are wondering where and how to start, this is the session for you. We will look at recent adoption trends and government mandates that are forcing the issue, do a walkthrough on the protocol itself, and finally explore some best practices around addressing plans and transition technologies. This session is targeted for enterprise, commercial, and public sector customers who are only just beginning their IPv6 adoption. We will not be discussing issues relating to service provider and mobile network providers. No IPv6 knowledge is required to attend, though basic familiarity with IPv4 networking is assumed. If you attended the TECIPV Technical Seminars, you do not need to attend this session.

### ISE Deployment Improvements - Tips and Tricks - BRKSEC-2347
Speakers: Katherine McNamara (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362780294001qMEX
Are you preparing for your first ISE deployment? Have you inherited an ISE deployment? This session focuses on giving best practices when it comes to the preparation, deployment, maintenance, and optimization considerations a customer should think about to ensure a successful ISE deployment. Like any technology, its easy to over complicate it over time so my hope to educate you the tips and tricks that will make your ISE deployment easier to manage. We will examine how to avoid some of the common pitfalls that I commonly see people fall into with their ISE deployments and "cheats" to make it easier. The session will also include common design pitfalls to avoid when planning out how ISE will be deployed using real world examples and how to lower the administrative overhead of an ISE deployment. At the end of the session, attendees can expect to have a better understanding of how to prepare their environment and their staff for an ISE deployment. This session is targeted at Network and Security Engineers, who are tasked in deploying or managing ISE.

### Identity Based Attacks, a Red Team/Blue Team Experience - BRKSEC-2164
Speakers: JP Sanchez (Cisco), Ned Zaldivar (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362776038001qS9T
In this session, we will showcase the concept of Identity Based Attacks starting with a red teaming demo leveraging various tools, techniques and tactics in order to effectively demonstrate how Identity based attacks are some of the most prevalent attacks in today's Security Landscape. We will then move to showcase the blue team perspective and leverage tools in order to show alerts, policies, procedures and hands on cyber operational techniques which reduce or mitigate risk towards identity-based attacks. This session will showcase Cisco Duo, Identity Intelligence, XDR ITDR, and other Cisco Tools.

### Identity Intelligence Demystified - BRKSEC-2162
Speakers: Andy Winiarski (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362775817001qXPN
"Why hack in when I can log in?" 2 years ago, Cisco completed the acquisition of Oort, a leader in the Identity Security Posture Management (ISPM) and Identity Threat Detection and Response (ITDR) space. Why Oort? What is identity security all about? What benefits will your organization receive from this acquisition that is being integrated into all aspects of Cisco Security. What we see in the real world has shown us that organizations have more than one Identity Providers (IdP) like Okta, Micrsoft EntraID and Duo SSO, but the security team isn't getting a complete or holistic view across the different IdPs. Come join Duo TME, Oort alum, and CII expert Andy Winiarski as he talks all about identity security trends, threats, posture and Cisco Identity Intelligence itself. See how it works with Security Operations Centers, with Splunk, Cisco XDR, and even Duo IAM and most importantly how do you improve your Security Posture by getting a hold of the identity related threats.

### Identity is the heart and soul of Zero Trust - BRKSEC-2066
Speakers: Aaron Woland (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362772928001qQup
Zero Trust isn't a product - it's a fundamental security principle reshaping how organizations protect their digital assets. Solutions like Zero Trust [Network] Access, Cisco Secure Access, SASE, and SSE all reflect this philosophy, enabling secure access to applications and data from anywhere, at any time, with consistency and modernity. At the heart and soul of every effective Zero Trust strategy lies Identity. While many experts proclaim "Identity is the new perimeter," Zero Trust principles emphasize that Identity has always been - and remains - the true perimeter. Establishing and maintaining a secure, robust identity foundation is essential for safeguarding both human users and non-human entities such as Agentic AI, endpoints, workloads, and service accounts. Join Aaron Woland, Distinguished Engineer in Cisco's Duo Identity group, as he explores the critical role of Identity in Zero Trust solutions. Discover what makes an identity solution secure, how it applies to diverse identities, and see real-world examples from Cisco Secure Access (aka: ZTA), Duo Security-First Identity, Identity Intelligence, and Identity Services Engine offerings.

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362769038001qh1Y
This breakout session will explain Cisco's latest security reference architecture covering the most common solutions that customers are deploying. Demos of these covered solutions will be shown to highlight each outcome. The session will introduce the evolving threat landscape in AI Era introduced by AI Applications and autonomous agents , and the required solution capabilities and associated platforms to mitigate themAttendees should walk away from this session understanding how the Cisco Security Reference Architecture can help them or even identify any potential gaps in their existing environment, even if they have a mixed vendor environment today.

### Impact of AI Traffic in Transport Networks - BRKSPG-1180
Speakers: Virginia Teixeira (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362785544001qzku
Artificial Intelligence (AI) is set to revolutionize every industry and the way users interact with the digital world. It's still early phases of AI adoption, and still unclear what are the deployment models and AI technics that will become more prevalent. However, it's already apparent that AI traffic patterns are different from traditional internet traffic, and that the use of AI frameworks such as RAG (Retrieval-Augmented Generation), grounding, AI agents and AI distribution to the edge, will have an impact on transport network design considerations. This session includes an analysis of the AI traffic characteristics for a set of AI use cases, and their impact on WAN and DCI design requirements.

### Implement Data Center VXLAN Multi-Site Traffic Engineering - BRKDCN-2933
Speakers: Parth Patel (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362708007001q2BL
VXLAN EVPN uses Layer-3 IP fabric as an underlay within and between DC sites. The IP fabric provides ECMP load-balancing and resiliency. Between DC sites, the connectivity is limited and has more constraints - capacity, redundancy, and path diversity. There is a need to control traffic steering over topologies with specific objectives and constraints for better link utilization. The VXLAN Multi-Site Traffic Engineering solution enables traffic steering and load-balancing capabilities for traffic sent between sites across DCI links. This session's attendees will learn how Cisco Nexus 9000-based VXLAN Multi-Site deployments provide Traffic Engineering solutions to improve inter-DC link utilization, leverage disjoint paths for fault isolation and avoid fate-sharing, or select specific paths for Inter-DC traffic. Note: Basic knowledge of VXLAN EVPN will be helpful.

### Implementing Cisco Unified Branch: CVD-Driven Architecture, Automation and AI Ops - CTF-2003
Speakers: Arpitha Shetty (Cisco), Jay Chokshi (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362811057001qTSe
This session focuses on implementing Cisco Unified Branch using a platform approach that unifies routers, switches, and wireless into a single cloud-managed solution based on Cisco Validated Designs (CVDs). Attendees will get practical guidance to deploy scalable, secure, and automated branch networks quickly, standardize rollout across sites, and streamline ongoing operations.

### Implementing Zero Trust at Machine Speed: Government Comply to Connect Framework - BRKSEC-2064
Speakers: Scott Wofford (Cisco), Dominic Mendoza (State of Colorado), Chris Perkins (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362772485001qNrQ
Government agencies face a dual mandate: achieve strict Zero Trust compliance while defending against threats that operate at machine speed. Traditional, manual approaches to compliance are slow, costly, and fail to provide real-time security, leaving agencies exposed. How can you turn compliance from a static checklist into a dynamic, automated defense? This session provides a practical blueprint for implementing Zero Trust at machine speed, using the Government Comply to Connect (C2C) framework. Featuring a real-world implementation story from the State of Colorado's Office of Information Technology (OIT), you'll see how Cisco ISE, Duo, and Cisco Identity Intelligence converge to automate access control and enforce policy dynamically. You'll leave this session with a clear plan to: • Implement the Comply to Connect framework to meet government Zero Trust mandates • Automate identity and access control using an integrated Cisco and Splunk security stack • Learn from the challenges and successes of the State of Colorado OIT's implementation journey This session features a customer speaker from the State of Colorado OIT. Intermediate knowledge of network access control and Zero Trust principles is recommended.

Get a glimpse of the future of Splunk intelligent data fabric and see how you can drive better insights into networking and operational technology (OT) data via Splunk, without learning SPL.

Explore strategies for deploying secure, policy-based remote access that empowers both IT and OT teams while upholding safety and compliance standards. We will also cover practical approaches to segmentation that help limit the impact of security incidents and align with organizational security objectives.

### Industrial Vision Systems: AI-Ready Infrastructure Design for Real-Time Inspection - BRKIIOT-2746
Speakers: Hammad Naeem (Basler), Erika Franco (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780412371761001Hj9C
Machine vision, increasingly powered by AI at the edge, is rapidly expanding across modern manufacturing. As deployments scale, infrastructure design becomes critical - and not all vision workloads stress the network the same way. Some are metadata-driven and tolerant. Others stream high-resolution data and place the network directly in the inspection decision loop. This session translates real vision use cases into concrete infrastructure requirements. We'll cover bandwidth planning, latency and jitter considerations, PoE budgeting, and precise timing with PTP, using validated industrial architectures. Attendees will leave with practical guidance to design scalable, reliable infrastructure for AI-enabled inspection systems - without over-engineering or risking downtime.

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362793027001qPQU
AI is a board level priority for all customers, but many are unsure how to design infrastructure that is secure and capable of supporting demanding AI workloads. page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362782967001qIny

### Inside the War Room: Hunting Post-Exploit Beacons with EVE and Splunk - BRKOBS-2010
Speakers: Marc Mastrangelo (Cisco), Blake Anderson (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362753695001qpwU

Through interactive scenario analysis, EVE and Splunk will be leveraged to show clear tracking and event tracing for post-exploit activity. Participants will work through actual detection scenarios, understanding how modern C2 channels evade traditional security tools and how to build effective detection strategies for post-compromise activity.

### Inspired by F1: How Purpose Drives Peak Performance - PP-1206
Speakers: Laura Geyer (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780412378924001H35u
Performance rises when Purpose is clear. In the high-speed world of F1, every role - from engineers to pit crews - is laser-focused on how their expertise influences results. The professional responsible for preparing the driver's race gear knows that fabric, fit, and preparation affect comfort, focus, and reaction time. Nothing is random. Nothing is disconnected. That is Purpose in action, and it changes how work is done. In business, such clarity is rare. When performance settles at "good enough," people can't clearly see how their decisions shape customers, growth, and outcomes. Drawing inspiration from F1's race-winning culture, this session will challenge you to close the gap between activity and measurable results to adopt practical leadership shifts that increase ownership and accelerate execution immediately. Join us to discover how Purpose creates a winning formula, and how you can apply these lessons to drive higher performance in your organization. Key takeaways include: • Why clear Purpose drives measurable results • The leadership behaviors that activate or dilute agency • One immediate shift to make performance gains unavoidable

### Intent to Action: AI as the New CLI - BRKOPS-2384

### Introduction to Nexus Dashboard: Simplifying Management of Your Data Center - BRKDCN-1619
Speakers: Pavan Jaya (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362701849001qtZf
Cisco Nexus Dashboard (ND) is the unified platform that combines operations and automation designed to simplify, optimize, and modernize data center network management. ND provides a common operational platform for Cisco Nexus-powered data centers, spanning EVPN-VXLAN single- and multi-site fabrics, classic LAN architectures, IP fabric for media, storage networking deployments, and emerging AI/ML network environments. This session serves as an introduction to Nexus Dashboard with a primary focus on the fabric controller capabilities. In this session, we will briefly look at the Nexus Dashboard architecture, installation, and various deployment options. We will then move on to LAN fabric mode for VXLAN EVPN and classic LAN fabrics. The focus will be on best-practice, template-based provisioning capabilities with built-in configuration compliance and the myriad deployment scenarios supported within ND. We will also walk through Day 2 features such as compute visibility using Virtual Machine Manager (VMM) integration, the switch dashboard, and real-time topology search, as exposed through the unified Nexus Dashboard experience. No prior knowledge of the legacy NDFC product or ND is required.

### Introduction to SRv6 uSID Technology - BRKMPL-2203
Speakers: Jakub Horn (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362741936001q5b6
This session will provide an overview of the SRv6 uSID technology and use cases. The session will demonstrate the application of SRv6 uSID building blocks on real use cases like L3 VPN, Flexible Algorithm, and traffic engineering.

### Introduction to Segment Routing and SRv6 - BRKMPL-1207
Speakers: Leonir Hoxha (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362741057001qSxx
Multiprotocol Label Switching (MPLS) has been the backbone of scalable and efficient networks for decades. However, its reliance on maintaining per-flow state in the network core limits scalability and increases operational complexity. Segment Routing (SR) revolutionizes MPLS by encoding path information into segment identifiers (SIDs) directly within the packet headers, removing the need for complex signaling protocols like LDP or RSVP-TE. This introduction level session delves into how SR extends and enhances MPLS, providing simplified operations, improved scalability, and seamless integration with traffic engineering paradigms. Taking these innovations further, SRv6 integrates the principles of SR into IPv6, utilizing its abundant address space and native extensibility to enable advanced use cases such as network slicing, application-aware routing, and service chaining. Attendees will explore the underlying mechanics of SID encoding, segment routing policies, and SRv6 network programming. This session provides technical insights and the fundamental building blocks of SR and SRv6. Attendees new to SR and SRv6 technology are the target audience.

### Introduction to VXLAN: The Future Path of Your Data Center - BRKDCN-1621
Speakers: Richard Licon (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362702071001qQyD
Learn more about VXLAN and delve deeper into it. This session will cover what VXLAN is, how it works and the problems it solves. The need for flexibility in the data center opens the door to network overlays. Several standards have been proposed and implemented to enable virtualized networks to be layered over a physical network. The data center network needs to be flexible enough to support workloads that can move from one host to another, and for new services to be deployed rapidly. VXLAN is one such technology that provides massively scalable virtual network overlays on top of existing IP infrastructures. This session will include 101s and packet walks as we cover the insides of VXLAN. No prior knowledge of VXLAN is required. A basic understanding of unicast and multicast routing protcol is needed, along with an understanding of MP BGP.

### Is Hyperconverged Infrastructure (HCI) still relevant in the AI era? - BRKCOM-1011
Speakers: Sho Funatsu (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362693053001qm7E
This session will explore the evolving role of HCI amid the rapid growth of AI-driven applications and data demands. As AI workloads require scalable, flexible, and high-performance infrastructure, HCI continues to offer significant value by simplifying data center operations through integrated compute, storage, and networking resources managed via a unified software layer. This session will discuss how Cisco's collaboration with software vendors delivers a comprehensive HCI solution that supports hybrid multicloud environments, enabling seamless application mobility and operational simplicity. Attendees will learn how HCI addresses challenges such as edge computing growth, data explosion, and the need for faster time-to-market, while providing enterprise-grade security, disaster recovery, and proactive management through Cisco Intersight. The session will also highlight how HCI's modular and programmable architecture is well-suited to meet AI's dynamic infrastructure requirements, ensuring organizations can scale efficiently without increasing operational complexity. Join us to understand why HCI remains a critical foundation for modern AI-era IT strategies and how Cisco's innovations continue to raise the bar in hyperconverged solutions.

### Is VPN really dead and replaced by Zero Trust Network Access (ZTNA)? - BRKSEC-1015
Speakers: Gustavo Medina (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362767467001qBYy
Join us for an insightful 60-minute breakout session where we will explore the differences between traditional Virtual Private Networks (VPN) and Zero Trust Network Access (ZTNA) solutions. While some may claim that VPNs are obsolete, we believe that each technology has its strengths and weaknesses. In this session, we'll provide a comprehensive comparison of VPN and ZTNA and help you understand how each approach can be leveraged to achieve a secure architecture based on Zero Trust principles. We'll also highlight real-world use cases to help you make an informed decision about which solution is right for your organization. Don't miss out on this opportunity to gain new insights into the world of cybersecurity and learn how to integrate the latest security technologies into your infrastructure.

### Is Your Network Ready for AI Agents? - WOSPAR-1027
Speakers: Brandon Heller (Forward)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362853655001qdBn
AI agents are coming to network operations whether teams are ready or not. The question is not whether your network will support autonomous operations, but whether it has the verified foundation to make them safe. This session is a live demonstration of how a mathematically accurate digital twin lets you verify the exact impact of any change before a single command is committed, giving your team and your AI agents the ground truth they need before touching production. You'll leave this session with ideas to help you Close the gap between security policy intent and production reality with continuous verification before every change is deployed. Replace physical lab testing with always-on automated validation and redirect that capital toward higher-impact work. Establish the verified foundation that both your team and your AI agents must consult before any action reaches production.

### Journey to Agentic Operations Across Infrastructure Domains - BRKXAR-2049
Speakers: Arvind Durai (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362794391001qQXR
The shift to agentic operations marks a fundamental move away from reactive firefighting toward a resilient, AI-driven operating model - one that enables autonomous, self-healing outcomes at scale. This transformation unfolds in phases: beginning with intelligent agents that augment human workflows, then progressively expanding agent authority as trust is established across campus, data center, and WAN domains. Measuring success requires KPI frameworks that go beyond traditional incident counts. Key metrics should include zero-touch remediation rates, agent decision accuracy, and the reduction of human-in-the-loop bottlenecks. This presentation delivers a practical, phased roadmap for building core capabilities in observability and automation, with near-term wins that establish momentum for full agentic operations. Attendees will gain an overview of the journey from infrastructure automation to AI-driven issue triage using AI Canvas, explore capabilities across Catalyst Center, SD-WAN Controller, and Nexus Dashboard, and examine real-world customer case studies leveraging Splunk, Catalyst Center, Nexus Dashboard, and ThousandEyes - with a clear, scalable path toward AI Canvas.

### Keeping Up with Zero Trust - BRKSEC-2176
Speakers: Jerry Lin (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362776958001qnEN
Enforcing Zero Trust least privilege access has failed to delivered a simple user experience. Come see the updated Zero Trust requirements that will help you get Zero Trust back on track. This session will cover the top most commonly deployed use cases (i.e. ZTNA, SASE, Workload Security, Agentic AI) that spans from user to workload/data/application(north-south traffic) and workload/data/application to workload/data/application (east-west traffic). Agentic AI security will also be discussed because it is a rising threat for all. Demos of each use case showing the user access experience and the administrator's experience will be provided. Attendees will walk away understanding that the Zero Trust journey must include holistic access policies that span end-to-end.

### Knock, Knock. Who is There Behind your Firewall? - All About Identity on Cisco Secure Firewall - BRKSEC-2590
Speakers: Carol Trincia Dsouza (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362780959001qjxX
This session provides a walkthrough of identity on Cisco Secure Firewall - making sense of who's actually on your network. Ever looked at your firewall policy and wondered - sure, I know the IP address, but who's actually sitting behind it? That's exactly what this session is about. We'll walk through all the different ways you can bring user identity into your firewall policy, from the straightforward stuff like Passive Identity Agent, to more advanced approaches with TrustSec, SGTs, and Zero Trust Network Access. Whether you're just getting started with identity-aware policies or you're knee-deep in an ISE deployment, there's something here for you. The session covers a lot of ground - think of it as a wide map rather than a deep dive - but I'll make sure to flag the common pitfalls and gotchas that tend to catch people off guard. Things like what happens when a user's IP address changes, why machine authentication can interfere with your Passive Identity setup, and why an absolute timeout might be quietly causing you headaches. We'll also cover newer capabilities including dynamic attributes, Cisco Identity Intelligence integration, and how Universal ZTNA fits into the bigger picture alongside your traditional firewall policy.

### Know Your Employee: How Cisco Verifies its Global Workforce with Persona Identities - WOSPAR-1011
Speakers: Rebeca Teran (Cisco), Bobby Baek (Persona)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362850553001qpHI
Social engineering now drives a majority of identity-related breaches, often targeting help desk interactions and remote onboarding workflows where attackers can bypass MFA entirely. In this session, we'll show how organizations use Identity Verification to confirm an employee's real-world identity before sensitive actions are approved. Learn how teams add identity verification to high-risk workflows like help desk password resets, new user enrollment, and self-service account recovery. We'll also walk through how identity verification works behind the scenes, including government ID validation, selfie matching, liveness checks, and deepfake detection, to stop increasingly sophisticated impersonation attacks.

### Kubernetes (K8s) Infrastructure Connectivity for the Modern Data Center - BRKDCN-2963
Speakers: Camillo Rossi (Cisco), Domenico Dastoli (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362710042001qI9k
Kubernetes platforms serve as the cornerstone of cloud-native applications, with the process of bootstrapping a Kubernetes cluster now simplified to just a few commands. However, selecting and configuring an appropriate Container Network Interface (CNI) remains a complex yet critical task for establishing a scalable and reliable Kubernetes infrastructure. With a variety of CNI solutions available, determining which ones integrate most effectively with Cisco NX-OS and ACI is essential for making informed design choices. In this session, we will delve into the capabilities of Cilium and explore strategies for seamless integration with Cisco ACI and NX-OS based fabrics. We will discuss methods for implementing network segmentation, Service LoadBalancing and demonstrate how to enhance visibility across container and network environments. This exploration aims to empower attendees with the knowledge to optimize their Kubernetes networking configurations, ensuring robust and efficient infrastructure.

### Kubernetes in CML: Building Secure Cloud-Native Networks in Reproducible Lab Environments - BRKCLD-2019

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362682857001qzTb
Kubernetes networking can feel unpredictable - especially when you're trying to understand routing behavior, enforce zero-trust policies, or troubleshoot microservice traffic at scale. In this session, you'll learn how to build fully functional Kubernetes clusters inside Cisco Modeling Labs (CML), giving you a reproducible environment you can mirror in your own data centers for experimentation, validation, and training. We'll deploy Cilium as the CNI, use Hubble for eBPF-powered flow visibility, and integrate Envoy to inspect and control service-to-service communication. Together, we'll explore core networking concepts, trace real traffic flows, diagnose common misconfigurations, and apply identity-aware network policies that form the foundation of a zero-trust model. By grounding cloud-native networking in hands-on, observable practice, this session helps network engineers build the intuition needed to support secure, resilient Kubernetes deployments - whether on-prem, in the cloud, or across hybrid environments.

### Lab Up For Success With Cisco Modeling Labs - BRKCRT-2059

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362699837001qIZq
No matter what Cisco certification you're preparing for, you know you need to do more than just read the book to be successful. You need to practice.
