Cisco Modeling Labs (CML) is a tool that provides you the virtual gear you need to practice for several Cisco certification exams with almost limitless topology possibilities. What's more, you can "plug" packet sniffers in with just a few clicks, introduce link problems, and even connect to physical kit you do have. In this session, we will explore how you can use CML to setup your own home labs. We'll dive into features such as traffic generation, packet capture, and external connectivity. Example topologies will be shared for a few scenarios to kick-start your studying and set you up for success.

### Legacy Tech, Modern Threats: The Risks You Can't Ignore - PP-1202
Speakers: Jeremy Marcus (Cisco), Matt Fussa (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780412378000001HRwk
Outdated infrastructure can pose a critical security risk. Cisco research shows that nearly half (48%) of network assets worldwide are aging or obsolete, putting critical infrastructure in sectors like healthcare, energy, water, manufacturing, and finance at higher risk of exploitation by bad actors. But knowing when and how to replace aging technology can be a balancing act that has budget and environmental ramifications. Join Cisco sustainability, security, and technology policy leaders to learn how you can identify legacy equipment that presents a risk, assess whether aging technology should be replaced, and responsibly dispose of equipment and procure new gear that is built with sustainability and security in mind. Attendees will gain strategic insights to improve the security and resiliency of their infrastructure.

### Lessons Learned: Zero Trust Implementation for "Smart" Architectures - BRKXAR-2029
Speakers: Patrick Lloyd (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362791667001qFJw
The pursuit of micro segmentation is enough to keep most CISO's up at night. Knowing that devices with significant security vulnerabilities need to exist on the network further influences the need to segment the network, and protect critical assets. Utilizing the Zero Trust Model published in "Zero Trust Architecture" this session covers lessons learned in the planning, design, and implementation of 'smart' technologies, buildings, and cities. Use cases such as connecting HVAC systems, MRI machines, and manufacturing technology to the network are used as illustrative examples. Focus for this presentation is on easing complexity, maintaining security of the network, easing enforcement, and realizing ROI quicker. Methods developed from lessons learned in various verticals will be presented, the application of which is to ensure that devices on the network are properly eased into micro segmentation, while still maintaining successful operation. This session will cover what methods are used when security is of the utmost importance, and segmentation of both vertical and lateral threats is a requirement. Knowledge of ISE, SNA, and TrustSec recommended, but not required.

### Lessons from the SOC: Threats and Security Issues found using Endace's Always-On Packet Capture - WOSPAR-1022
Speakers: Michael Morris (Endace)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362852758001qQ4A
Endace is proud to be part of the Cisco SOC-in-a-Box Architecture that is leveraged at major security shows around the world in the past year, including: RSAC 2025 and 2026, Cisco Live US 2025, Black Hat US 2025, GovWare Singapore 2025, Cisco Live Melbourne 2025, and Cisco Live EMEA 2026. During these industry leading events, Endace's always-on packet capture has been a vital component of the SOC architecture and key part of successful incident investigations and threat hunting that uncovered many cybersecurity threats and issues. The goal of these events is to protect, educate, and innovate and we want to share some of the lessons we've learned along the way. This session will share: What world-class next-generation SOC architectures look like. A walk-through of some of the workflows and automations that have been developed and used at these events for more effective and efficient SOC operations Several interesting examples of security incidents we found at these cybersecurity shows Takeaways you can think about for your own SOC evolution.

### Let's Deploy Rugged Switches with Cisco Meraki Dashboard - BRKIIOT-1008

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780412369375001HXGc
Enterprises are expanding networks beyond traditional office spaces into outdoor and harsh environments, where rugged switching supports campus-edge connectivity and physical security for cameras, outdoor Wi-Fi, digital signage, access control, and Internet of Things (IoT) devices across parking lots, perimeters, and walkways. These deployments are often left partially unmanaged or operated with separate tools, increasing security exposure, operational complexity, and friction to scale. This session explains how Cisco is enabling customers to manage Rugged switches, Catalyst switches, and Meraki switches together through a common cloud management experience in the Cisco Meraki Dashboard. Participants will learn how to: Deploy, onboard, configure, and manage rugged switches at scale in the Cisco Meraki Dashboard Monitor health and performance from anywhere, including power over Ethernet (PoE) usage for better efficiency Strengthen security with consistent policies and centralized visibility into traffic and security events Troubleshoot faster with AI-ready insights using proactive monitoring and guided root-cause resolution The session includes demos showing real deployment and day-to-day operations for rugged switches in the Cisco Meraki Dashboard.

### Let's Deploy Unified Global Management (CCGM + Global Overview) for Catalyst Centers and Meraki Organizations - BRKOPS-2492

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362760115001qShC

In this session, you will learn how to: 1. Enable Meraki Global Overview for unified monitoring of Meraki and Catalyst environments 2. Automate bring up of CCGM on VMware ESXi or AWS 3. Monitor the health of networks, clients, devices, and SD-Access, including P1/P2/P3 issues, EOL/EOS, and Catalyst Center backup 4. ### Leverage Unique IOS XE Capabilities for Supercharging SRv6 with Crosswork Automation - BRKMSI-2002
Speakers: Kedar Karmarkar (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362744606001qSU9
Explore the unique capabilities of Segment Routing v6 (SRv6) combined with the Cisco 8000 Series Secure Router and the Cisco Crossworks Controller in this session. Attendees will gain a brief overview of SRv6 fundamentals, including how SRv6 leverages IPv6 for scalable, flexible, and programmable network architectures that simplify traffic engineering and path protection. The session will cover: • How unique capabilities of IOS XE enhance the simplicity of SRv6; add App-Awareness to traffic engineering capabilities using NBAR; support encrypting data traffic with PQC-certified IPsec and MACsec and support Multicast applications. • Use cases such as multi-tenancy with L3VPN over SRv6, App-Aware SR-TE Per-Destination/Per-Flow policies, create network slices for confidentiality of data traffic using Flexible Algorithm, connect and augment SD-WAN fabrics with SRv6 and SR-TE using the Cisco 8000 Series Secure Router platform. • The role of the Cisco Crossworks Controller in automating and orchestrating SRv6 networks, provide visualization and lifecycle management through SDN principles. Attendees MUST have prior knowledge of SRv6 fundamentals, L3 VPN, SR-TE policies, Flexible Algorithm, Multicast VPN to realize the value of this session.

### Leveraging Cisco Smart Switches for Best Practice Enterprise Campus Networks - BRKENS-2618
Speakers: Paul Broccardi (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362717737001qhzf
As enterprises modernize their campus environments, the demand for secure, automated, and resilient switching architectures has never been greater. This session examines how Cisco Smart Switches and IOS-XE enable best-practice designs for scalable, secure, and easily managed enterprise campus networks. Participants will gain an understanding of essential configuration strategies, including Network as Code (NaC) integration, spanning tree design, Power over Ethernet (PoE) management, uplink resiliency, and port-channeling, with practical examples from IDF, MDF, and WAN deployments. The session highlights proven CLI and NaC configuration templates, step-by-step troubleshooting guidance, and design patterns to maximize their investment in Cisco Smart Switches. Participants should have a foundational understanding of enterprise switching; familiarity with Cisco IOS-XE, Smart Switches, and basic scripting concepts is recommended. Live demonstrations and reference architectures will help you confidently design, deploy, and manage robust campus networks using Cisco best practices.

### Leveraging Tools to Successfully Migrate from UCM to Webex Calling Multi-Tenant - BRKCOL-2079
Speakers: Steven Fly (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362687322001qowC
Transitioning your on-premises UCM calling platform to Webex Calling can be a significant undertaking and sometimes may feel overwhelming. In this session we will use a 3-phase approach to divide the transition into manageable activities. Then following a 2-step transition and leveraging the PPDIO methodology, we will review the key transition activities and see how the Webex migration tools can assist to efficiently complete them. We will also highlight our migration partners, who have developed comprehensive tools to manage the complete migration process.

### Life of a Packet in Kubernetes: A Network Engineer's Guide - BRKCLD-3734
Speakers: Nico Vibert (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362683763001qJVv
Kubernetes networking can be difficult to reason about, especially for those used to traditional infrastructure. Traffic is routed through layers of abstraction such as services, overlays, policies and proxies, making visibility and control a challenge. This advanced session maps the full journey of a packet through a Kubernetes cluster, across nodes, and out via egress. Using Isovalent Cilium as the dataplane, we will unpack how traffic flows through technologies such as VXLAN and Geneve overlays, Gateway API, Egress Gateway, BGP routing, IPsec encryption, Direct Server Return (DSR), and multi-cluster networking with Cluster Mesh. We will also explore performance-focused features including BIG TCP and topology-aware traffic engineering. Expect live demos. Attendees will learn how to correlate packet behavior with Kubernetes constructs using Hubble, Cilium CLI, and Wireshark, gaining the insight needed to troubleshoot and optimize real-world networks. You'll leave this session with ideas to help you: trace Kubernetes traffic through encapsulation, routing, policy, and encryption layers understand the impact of topology-aware routing and load balancing decisions capture and analyze real traffic to resolve complex service connectivity issues This is an advanced session intended for network engineers, platform teams, and SREs with experience in IP networking and Kubernetes. Familiarity with routing, overlays, and load balancing is expected.

### Love-All: Meraki + Catalyst Wins Championship-Level Connectivity at "The Cincinnati Open" Tennis Tournament - CSSENT-2004
Speakers: Robert Nichols (The Cincinnati Open (Beemok))
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362806080001q3bV
Live sports venues face an impossible challenge: deliver flawless connectivity for massive crowds, deploy rapidly, and maintain it without an army of IT staff. The Cincinnati Open refused to compromise. As one of only nine ATP Masters 1000 events worldwide and the oldest professional tennis tournament in its original city, this premier venue's $260 million transformation demanded infrastructure matching the caliber of champions competing weeks before the US Open. Cisco's integrated solutions were instrumental in this digital transformation. A fully cloud-managed network combining Catalyst switching and Meraki wireless delivered unprecedented resiliency and simplified operations. Meraki Smart Cameras and ecosystem partners extended capabilities beyond connectivity, enhancing security, crowd management, and fan services. The result: championship-level infrastructure enabling mobile ticketing, cashless transactions, and seamless connectivity across 40+ acres - from practice courts to stadium seating. This session reveals how unified cloud management transformed fan experience while setting new standards for live sports venues.

### Low-Code Meets AI: Turn Cisco RADKit and n8n into Your Network's Smart Sidekick - BRKDEV-2150
Speakers: Alfonso Sandoval Rosas (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362713616001qUgy
If your network is already onboarded to Cisco RADKit, you now have the foundation for a fully AI-assisted operations platform without realizing it. Even if you are not using RADKit yet, this session is your chance to explore a platform that can streamline access to your network and unleash agentic AI operations by integrating with drag-and-drop workflows and just a tiny pinch of code. We will show how RADKit can become the backbone of an agentic AI system for network operations by leveraging the official RADKit MCP server along with the n8n low-code workflow platform. Through a series of live demos, you will see how to interact naturally with your network via multi-channel ChatOps or scheduled workflows to query device state, retrieve configurations, push safe network changes, generate reports, check compliance, and create remediation tickets. The workflows showcased are built with guardrails, human-in-the-loop control, defined agent personas, and an observability stack for full accountability. All source code will be made publicly available.

Cisco MDS switches contain many advanced diagnostic features to facilitate diagnosing and resolving these problems. Speakers: Nikolai Pitaev (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362721526001qYmM
Cisco Catalyst SD-WAN (formerly Viptela) builds a secure, unified connectivity over any transport and provides simplified operations with centralized management, policy control, and application visibility across the enterprise network. This introduction-level session will showcase how SD-WAN operations are made easier and more efficient with the introduction of new features and an enhanced user experience starting from 20.12 Release. They address customer challenges in the areas of onboarding, configuration, user interface, troubleshooting, visibility, and AI. Participants will become familiar with the new, intuitive, and consistent User Interface and will learn a fresh approach to configuration and guided workflows based on configuration groups, improved visibility, AI Assistant and new troubleshooting tools like Network Wide Path Insights (NWPI). Several live demonstrations will be shown.

### Making Private Mobile Networks Mobile: Adopting Non-Terrestrial Networking with Private 5G to Create a Next-Generation Infrastructure - BRKMSI-2211
Speakers: Derick Linegar (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362746886001qlUh
The accelerating demand for ubiquitous, secure, and high-performance connectivity is driving innovation beyond the reach of traditional terrestrial networks. Private 5G networks have emerged as a critical enabler for enterprises seeking robust wireless infrastructure; however, their potential remains constrained by physical and geographic limitations. This white paper explores how integrating Non-Terrestrial Networks (NTN), as standardized in 3GPP Release 17, with Cisco's Private 5G-as-a-Service (P5GaaS) offering can overcome these barriers. By leveraging satellite and airborne platforms, organizations can extend the reach of private mobile networks to remote, mobile, and underserved environments. We detail the architectural integration of Cisco P5GaaS with Cisco Identity Services Engine (ISE), enabling secure, policy-driven access and unified identity management across terrestrial and non-terrestrial domains. The paper examines the benefits of adopting 3GPP R17 NTN standards, such as seamless mobility, enhanced resilience, and global coverage, and outlines key deployment scenarios spanning industries from logistics and energy, to emergency response. Through practical use cases and technical insights, this session demonstrates how Cisco's holistic approach - blending P5GaaS, Cisco ISE, and NTN - can deliver a truly mobile, next-generation infrastructure, empowering organizations to unlock new operational efficiencies and business opportunities.

### Making the Internet a Better Place by Participating in the IETF - BRKGEN-1385

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362735530001qlSK

It can be daunting to think of developing and writing standards at first but know that this is a journey. Everyone is welcome at the IETF, this is free, and there is something for everyone to do and to learn. The presentation will be a unique and fun conversation between a veteran and a newcomer at the IETF to provide attendees with varied perspectives. Come to this session to learn about the IETF, the kind of work that happens there, and how you can get started with your journey at the IETF.

### Mastering Voice Issues Troubleshooting in Webex Calling with Local Gateway - TACCOL-2020

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780412381251001HiNP
When a critical business call suffers from poor voice quality or audio loss, the impact on productivity is immediate, and these types of issues in hybrid cloud deployments are notoriously difficult to isolate. Beyond operational disruption, such voice problems can lead to significant financial consequences, including lost revenue, and damage to customer satisfaction and brand reputation. In this technical deep dive, we strip away the complexity of Webex Calling media paths. Designed for engineers managing hybrid implementations, this session provides a systematic framework for identifying and resolving audio degradation, one-way audio, and no-way audio. We will guide you from the macro view of Control Hub Media Quality Analytics down to detailed endpoint Problem Report Tool (PRT) analysis, teaching you how to correlate cloud-side metrics with on-premises LGW debugs, decipher SDP media negotiations, and efficiently troubleshoot voice issues across all deployment. Join us to gain the diagnostic confidence to pinpoint the root cause of voice issues efficiently, ensuring your organization's voice services remain clear, reliable, and business-ready.

### Mean Time to Regret: When Faster Repairs Lead to Bigger Breaches - WOSPAR-2011
Speakers: Mitch Densley (Opengear)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362859270001qW8w
When outages hit, every minute counts but in the rush to shrink mean time to repair, many organizations accidentally create wide-open attack surfaces by putting remote access systems directly on the public internet. In this quick, high-impact 20-minute session, we'll look at a couple of major outages where remote access made the difference between hours and days of downtime and how insecure deployments could have turned these incidents into full-blown security failures. You'll get a fast tour of how attackers actually perform reconnaissance today, from open-source intelligence platforms to automated scanners and dark-web marketplaces that trade in exposed management interfaces. We'll also highlight real breaches where poorly secured remote access equipment served as the attacker's front door. Finally, we'll show how Opengear's design philosophy flips the script keeping management paths isolated, hardened, and invisible to internet-scale reconnaissance so teams can respond quickly without putting their infrastructure at risk. If you're responsible for reliability, networking, or security, this is 20 minutes you'll want to catch before an attacker catches you.

### Meet your 24/7 Agentic Workforce: Achieving Hands-Free Networking - PSOENT-1034

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362832511001q8sy

This isn't just another chatbot - it's a shift from managing infrastructure to commanding an autonomous, agentic workforce. In this session, discover how to use the Cisco AI Assistant and AI Canvas to orchestrate agents that sense, reason, and act on your behalf. Learn how to automate the "toil" of troubleshooting and configuration so you can stop being a first responder and start being an architect. Your agents have the night shift - it's time to reclaim your time.

### Meraki for Government: Making Compliance Easy in the Public Sector - BRKARC-2097
Speakers: Larry Woods (Cisco), Chris Hocker (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362674625001qIYY
Cisco Meraki has achieved FedRAMP Authorization to Operate (ATO) for use with Local, State and U.S. Federal Government entities and customers who work with the Public Sector and have FedRAMP compliance needs. This session will cover the Cisco Meraki FedRAMP offering (Meraki for Government), its capabilities, and the hardware options best suited to meeting public sector requirements. Furthermore, we will cover the architectures that best address Federal and public sector use cases, and what features/products we plan to bring in the future! Come join us to talk all things about Cisco Meraki in the Public Sector and how Cisco is making compliance easier for our partners and customers!

### Meta : Secure Workplace transformation with Catalyst 9K BGP EVPN VXLAN - BRKENS-2656
Speakers: Seshagiri Rao Kornepati (FACEBOOK INC), Carlos Zeledon (Meta), David Gutierrez (FACEBOOK INC), Rahul Kachalia (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362718368001qx08
Meta senior network architect managing hyper-scale network environment recounts the wide-range of operational challenges of large traditional L2 networks built around centralized policy enforcement. These legacy networks, while functional, forced rigid topologies and overlay dependencies that led to architectural complexity, limited scalability, security gaps, and fragile resiliency. Meta shares how their organization undertook a holistic transformation using industry-standard based BGP EVPN VXLAN technology on Cisco Catalyst 9000 series platforms to overcome these constraints with fully-distributed, fabric-based L2 flood-free networking while still retaining the required centralized policy controls. Attendees will learn how modern EVPN VXLAN architectures unlock unprecedented two-tier Hierarchical fabric structure to support scale, performance, Macro/Micro-segmentation, and operational simplicity, and why transitioning to fabric-driven designs is essential for building secure, future-proof networks at enterprise and hyper-scale levels.

### Migrate from Your Existing Network to VXLAN EVPN - BRKDCN-2951
Speakers: Yianni Thallas (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362709580001qCGE
Still running STP/vPC or FabricPath? This session will provide data center migration strategies from these legacy protocols to the Cisco VXLAN EVPN next-gen data centers, which help mitigate issues with these protocols and provide users with automation and visibility. The session will cover migrations of FHRPs in addition to coexistence of FHRP and Anycast Gateways, moving workloads, L4-L7 migration and migrating external layer 3 connectivity from a classic network to the new VXLAN EVPN network. By the end of this session, participants will leave with the knowledge and prerequisites in order to migrate to the Cisco next-generation data centers. Participants should have a basic understanding of VXLAN EVPN prior to joining this session.

### Migrating Cisco IP Phones from Unified Communications Manager to Webex Calling Multi-tenant - BRKCOL-2064
Speakers: Joe Martini (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362686440001qQg6
This session will provide an in-depth understanding of how to migrate Cisco 7800, 8800, and 9800 Series IP Phones from Cisco Unified Communications Manager (CUCM) or Webex Calling Dedicated Instance (DI) to Webex Calling Multitenant (MT). For the 7800 and 8800 Series phones this requires changing between Enterprise firmware and Multiplatform firmware. Enterprise firmware is used by CUCM and DI, while Multiplatform firmware is used by Webex Calling Multi-tenant. For the 9800 series phones, the call service mode must be changed to move to Webex Calling Multi-tenant. This phone migration session is for people who who already have existing phones being used with CUCM or DI. Attendees will learn about migration automation tools within Control Hub as well as the Cloud Upgrader (upgrade.cisco.com) from a TAC engineer and co-developer to maximize your success rate by avoiding common mistakes. Topics covered include: What Is a Phone Migration Phone Eligibility Migration Licensing Firmware Conversion

### Migrating Legacy Campus Network to BGP EVPN VXLAN Fabric - Migration Strategy, Best Practices and Real World Experience Use Cases - BRKENS-2839
Speakers: Anis Edavalath (Cisco), Goran Ristic (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362719732001qVhv
To meet the evolving needs of network in large enterprise environments, there is an increasing demand to migrate to the next generation open standard based BGP EVPN VXLAN architecture in campus network. If you are the one who is looking to migrate your legacy campus network to BGP EVPN VXLAN campus fabric, then this session is for you. This session will explore different migration approaches and strategies to upgrade legacy campus network to Catalyst 9000 based campus BGP EVPN VXLAN fabric. The session will cover detailed step by step migration process, including the planning, execution, and post-migration phases. Attendees will gain valuable insights regarding both wired and wireless migration to BGP EVPN campus fabric with best practices, common pitfalls, challenges encountered and solutions provided. We will also demonstrate how to leverage Ansible automation framework to efficiently migrate to BGP EVPN VXLAN fabric. The session will make use of a real customer use-case and first-hand deployment experience, lessons learnt, to highlight end-to-end migration strategy to transform legacy campus network seamlessly to Catalyst 9000 based BGP EVPN VXLAN fabric.

### Mission Critical: Communications for Disaster Response and Business Resiliency - PP-1209
Speakers: Eric Knudson (Cisco), Forrest Foster (Cisco Systems, Inc.)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780412379601001H7bR
The companies that are best able to respond during a crisis are those that plan before disaster strikes. A growing number of companies are building their own in-house emergency connectivity capabilities modeled on what frontline responders have long known to be true: when networks go down, missions fail. This session provides a primer on Business Continuity Planning and shares ways Cisco Crisis Response has helped partners across sectors -- from critical infrastructure to humanitarian organizations operating in active conflict zones. Participants will see how these different organizations have used Cisco technology to stay operational when it matters most. You'll leave this session with ideas to help you: • Assess your organization's current communications vulnerabilities and redundancy gaps • Build a scalable, in-house emergency communications capability using proven Cisco solutions • Learn from real implementations to accelerate your own resilience No technical background required. Developed for corporate responsibility, operations, and technology leaders.

### Mitigation of Adversarial Attacks on Generative AI and Agentic with Cisco's AI Defense - BRKSEC-2047
Speakers: Keith O'Brien (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362769801001qZp9
This session explores the evolving security landscape of generative AI, with a focus on threat models specific to large language models (LLMs) and agentic applications. We will examine key security frameworks, including the OWASP Top 10 for LLMs and MITRE ATLAS, to provide a structured understanding of potential risks. The talk will feature demonstrations of real-world threats derived from Cisco AI threat research, illustrating how adversaries can exploit generative models, AI supply chain and agents. Finally, we will introduce AI Defense and Cisco's agentic security framework and how it can be used to mitigate these threats and provide security visibility to enhance the security of LLM deployments.

### Model. Test. Deploy. Accelerating network deployments with Cisco Modeling Labs. - BRKOPS-2550
Speakers: Matt Leuschner (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362760334001q8jj
Cisco Modeling Labs (CML) provides the foundation to build a functional model of your production network. A platform where you can develop, test, and validate automation and network state before going to production. But how do you manage the differences between virtual and physical networks, like interface naming or module differences? In this session, attendees will go through a deployment workflow to model their network in CML, develop and test automation, and deploy to a production environment. Attendees will learn how to build the necessary Ansible playbooks, perform tests to validate that the changes have been applied, and confidently deploy those changes to hardware. Attendees will walk away ready to use CML as part of their network automation workflows, with confidence!

### Modern Authentication Explained to the Network Professional - BRKSEC-2144
Speakers: Aaron Woland (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362775163001qwzC
Identity Security is all the rage (again). However the identity attacks look quite a bit different than they used to. To understand the modern identity landscape, you have to start to understand these crazy concepts like "Single-Sign-On" (SSO), what on earth OIDC is, and how come "cookies" are so important. However, the more things change, the more they stay the same, too. AAA: Authentication, Authorization & Accounting does not mean 'RADIUS' or 'NAC'. Those of us in Network Security are usually familiar with 802.1X and authenticating to networks for wireless, wired, and even VPN. However, AAA is a fundamental and foundational security principle, not a product. Those concepts are universal and still apply when you hear the 'new' authentication protocol and technologies like: OAuth, SAML, OIDC, IAM, IdP, FIDO, Yubikeys, Passkeys, password-less, and many more. We will also dive into how this relates to Agentic AI, MCP Servers and other non-human identities. This session focuses on explaining modern web-based authentication protocols & teaching about them in a way that compares & contrasts them to network security authentication methods. In other words: come learn about the latest in identity attacks (like session hijacking, and MFA fatigue); the latest in authentication protocols and how to understand them with your networking background. If you know all about SAML & OAuth already, but don't understand 802.1X or EAP - this session is also for you! Aaron Woland, an expert leader from Cisco's Duo Identity Security business, will entertain you while educating you on this important and growing area of security.

### Modern Multi-Homing in VXLAN EVPN: Enabling vPC and ESI+ in Today's Data Centers - BRKDCN-2940
Speakers: Parth Patel (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362708226001qwAw
As data center fabrics evolve, network architects now have two powerful ways to deliver active-active multi-homing: classic vPC and the new EVPN ESI multihoming introduced in NX-OS 10.6.1. While both address the same problem, their design assumptions, control-plane behavior, and operational impacts differ significantly. This breakout session demystifies the two models - how they work, what changes with ESI+, and when customers should choose one over the other. Through real design examples, packet-flow comparisons, and deployment considerations, we will demonstrate how to select the right multi-homing approach based on your fabric's scale, resiliency requirements, and operational model. Note: This session will heavily focus on EVPN-based multi-homing. Attendees are expected to have prior knowledge of vPC-based VTEP deployments in VXLAN fabrics.

You'll leave this session understanding how AI is influencing the industry to avoid designing a system that will be obsolete by the time it is screwed into a rack. Whether you are a salty engineer or an aspiring architect, join us in this session and step into your next design meeting with more information and greater insight.

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362865040001qHNC

You'll see how combining deep network visibility with contextual intelligence helps teams move faster from detection to resolution, without the guesswork. You'll leave this session with a clear path to: Gain complete visibility across Cisco and multi-vendor networks without manual documentation Reduce time to resolution by turning alerts into actionable next steps Proactively manage network modernization using real-time network data Ditch the legacy network management systems and get ahead with Auvik. Come see for yourself how easy it is to switch

### Modernizing Brazil's Electric Grid: How ONS Unified ACI, SD-WAN and SD-Access to Power Mission-Critical Operations - CSSENT-2003
Speakers: Juliana Ferreira dos Santos (ONS), Leonardo Ferreira (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362805849001qFlf
The Operador Nacional do Sistema Eletrico (ONS), responsible for coordinating the entire Brazilian electric grid, is undertaking one of the largest digitalization programs in Latin America. To modernize its mission-critical WAN and data center architecture, ONS adopted a unified approach using Cisco ACI Multi-Site with Nexus Dashboard, Cisco SD-WAN, and full integration with its existing Cisco SD-Access deployment. This session walks through the end-to-end transformation - from the redesign of their OT and IT networks, to the adoption of ACI's policy-driven fabric, application-centric segmentation, multi-tenant capabilities, and hybrid-cloud integrations. We will detail how SD-WAN now provides real-time path optimization, multi-link resilience, and centralized operations across MPLS, Internet, and satellite links, while NDO delivers consistent intent, lifecycle automation, and unified visibility across sites. Attendees will learn how ONS improved availability, reduced operational complexity, strengthened Zero-Trust security, and created a scalable architecture capable of supporting future energy-sector demands.

### Modernizing UC Visibility at Scale: Insights from a Large Integrated Healthcare System - WOSPAR-2005
Speakers: Reginald Pearson (TeleMate, LLC.)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362857882001qo5V
Healthcare organizations rely on unified communications (UC) and contact center platforms to support critical operations, yet visibility is often fragmented across vendors, deployment models, and tools. In large-scale healthcare environments, this creates blind spots that slow troubleshooting, limit service assurance, and reduce operational insight. This session shows how holistic observability connects cloud, on-premises, and hybrid UC&C environments into a single, actionable view. The session includes real-world insights from a large, complex healthcare deployment, highlighting how tool consolidation and end-to-end visibility improve performance and operational outcomes. You'll leave this session with ideas to help you: Unify visibility across complex UC&C environments Reduce reliance on siloed tools Improve troubleshooting and service assurance at scale

### Modernizing at Scale: How Fiserv Streamlined Data Center and Campus Operations with Cisco Nexus Dashboard - CSSDCN-1008
Speakers: Karishma Gupta (Cisco), Amit Shah (Fiserv)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362805391001qFZ1
Fiserv, a global leader in financial services technology, set out to modernize and unify operations across their data center and campus environments. With rising application demands and strict uptime requirements, they needed a consistent, automated operational model. By deploying Cisco Nexus Dashboard and embracing Network as Code (NaC) across both domains, Fiserv moved from manual configuration to an API-driven, automated workflow. In the data center, NaC enabled standardized policy deployment, automated multi-site configuration, and faster, more reliable rollouts across multiple VXLAN fabrics. On the campus side, NaC delivered consistent policy expression and simplified change management for both VXLAN and traditional networks. The combination of Nexus Dashboard and NaC strengthened compliance, eliminated configuration drift, and streamlined operations across globally distributed sites. This session will share Fiserv's transformation journey, key architecture decisions, and the measurable operational and business outcomes achieved through Nexus Dashboard and Network as Code.

### Monitor your LLMs, AI Agents, and AI Infrastructure with Splunk AI Observability - BRKOBS-2000
Speakers: Joe deBlaquiere (Cisco), Bob Ni (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362752300001q0jo
As Large Language Models (LLMs) and AI agents become central to business operations, they introduce hallucinations and biased outputs that can damage customer trust. At the same time, modern infrastructure is growing more complex, requiring visibility into vector databases and other specialized AI components. This session explains how Splunk Observability for AI monitors the performance, quality, security, and cost of the AI stack. You'll learn how AI Agent Monitoring, integrated soon with Cisco AI Defense, ensures LLM and agentic applications remain reliable, safe, and effective in production, provides visibility into user and agent conversations, and generates automated quality evaluations such as bias, toxicity, and hallucinations. You'll leave the session understanding how to correlate AI application performance to infrastructure health, reliability, and availability. A basic understanding of AI frameworks and observability concepts is recommended.

### Multi-Cast with EVPN, Segment Routing, and Traffic Engineering - BRKMPL-1123
Speakers: Mankamana Mishra (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362740839001qH2B
Segment Routing (SR) is an industry-standard approach aimed at simplifying transport network, including traffic engineering and management across multiple domains. Ethernet VPN (EVPN) serves as a widely adopted overlay solution for Layer-2 extensions when L3 VPN serves as an overlay solution for IPv4 and IPV6 private services. This session offers a comprehensive exploration of multicast service delivery within EVPN and L3VPN networks. It delves into transport and services multicast delivery methods, covering both distributed protocols Multicast tree computation and controller-based approaches. Additionally, the session will focus on deploying multicast over the SRv6 transport network, providing a clear path forward for integrating multicast in SRv6 environments.

### Navigating the SNMP Free Journey: Unlock Real-Time Insights with Model Driven Telemetry - BRKOPS-2594
Speakers: Story DeWeese (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362760776001qJ6F
Are you ready to move beyond the limitations of SNMP and embrace a modern, scalable, and efficient network telemetry solution? Join us to explore Cisco's Model Driven Telemetry (MDT), a powerful alternative that leverages YANG data models and streaming telemetry to provide real-time, granular insights into your network infrastructure. This session will cover the fundamentals of MDT, including its architecture, data encoding, and transport mechanisms such as gRPC dial-out and gNMI dial-in. You will learn how MDT offers consistent, reliable, and secure telemetry data delivery, enabling proactive network monitoring and automation. We will also demonstrate practical deployment examples using Cisco IOS XE devices, Docker-based telemetry collectors, and visualization tools like Grafana. Whether you are just starting your journey to be SNMP-free or looking to deepen your understanding of model-driven telemetry, this session will equip you with the knowledge and tools to transform your network operations.

### Network Automation Simplified: Building MCP Servers - CISCOU-2320
Speakers: Gabi Zapodeanu (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362803112001qMJe
Managing network devices, client health, performance, and topology across multiple platforms is complex and challenging. In this presentation, we'll explore how Agentic AI and Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers can unify your entire network infrastructure into a single intelligent view. We will break down how to build MCP servers that provide operational data network-wide - from both on-premises and cloud platforms. Catalyst Center and Meraki APIs will be featured during the session, but the same concepts will apply for other network platforms. Key topics covered: MCP server architecture and client communication Designing API tools and AI-driven workflows AI network insights: clients and devices, health, topology, compliance and issues We'll use live demos and walk through sample code to demonstrate essential MCP components: client-to-server communication, tool schema design, and AI flows that orchestrate multiple tools. No prior MCP or LLM experience is required - just curiosity. You'll leave with practical examples, reusable code, and the confidence to start building your own MCP-powered automation.

### Networking for the AI era - From Connected Networks to Cognitive Network Systems - WOSPAR-2060
Speakers: Soumit Naha (Infosys), Chethan Krishna (Infosys)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362870523001qRTE
Enterprise networking is undergoing its most profound transformation since the birth of IP. What began as a discipline focused on connectivity, redundancy, and throughput is evolving into something fundamentally new: the cognitive network system where intelligence is built into the fabric of the network itself. In the AI era, the network is no longer a passive fabric that merely moves data - it is becoming an intelligent system that understands intent, observes experience, predicts outcomes, and acts in real time. This shift is driven by AI workloads with extreme performance demands, pervasive cloud and edge adoption, escalating cyber threats, and rising expectations for flawless digital experience. AI becomes the operating system of the enterprise network, where telemetry replaces guesswork, experience replaces uptime as the primary KPI, and autonomous agents prevent issues before users notice them. The destination is not just automation, but autonomy - networks that self configure, self heal, self secure, and continuously align with business outcomes.

### Networks Under Attack: The Path to Resilient Infrastructure - BRKSEC-2183
Speakers: Paul Giralt (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362777835001qYK5
Global threat actors are increasingly shifting their sights from endpoints to the network foundation, launching sophisticated attacks against the infrastructure itself. The reality is that many of these breaches are preventable, often stemming from insecure legacy configurations and operational gaps. In this session, we will examine how state-sponsored actors exploit these weaknesses to gain a foothold in critical networks - and how Cisco is answering the challenge. We will do a deep dive into Cisco's Resilient Infrastructure initiative, highlighting the proactive hardening changes being implemented across the entire product portfolio - spanning IOS XE, IOS XR, NX-OS, ASA/FTD, ISE, Catalyst Center, SD-WAN, Meraki, UCS, and more. You will learn how these changes are making platforms "secure by default," and explore new capabilities designed to detect and respond to threats. ### Operating AI Infrastructure at Scale: Building a Distributed Resilience Control Plane - WOSPAR-2013
Speakers: RAMTIN RAMPOUR (Opengear)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362859738001qoLG
GPU infrastructure is expanding rapidly across distributed sites, from NeoCloud providers to enterprise AI deployments. But while compute capacity grows, the management layer struggles to keep up. Operators face isolated management networks, inconsistent BMC implementations, fragmented telemetry, and slow provisioning, all compounded across dozens of sites with no consistent remote access pattern. This session explores how a distributed out-of-band control plane can close that gap. We'll show how a single secure gateway layer provides remote access to any resource on an isolated management network, whether that's a BMC for Redfish-based server management, a Cisco switch for Ansible-driven network automation, or a PDU for power management, without VPNs, jump boxes, or per-site firewall complexity. Using validated reference architectures and live demonstration, attendees will see how teams normalize BMC and Redfish management across vendors, automate network device configuration via Ansible through the same access layer, and drive rack bring-up from a centralized NOC. The result: faster time-to-revenue, consistent management across sites, and a resilient control plane that works when production networks don't.

### Overlay Multicast in VXLAN EVPN (Tenant Routed Multicast - TRM) - BRKDCN-3638
Speakers: John Weston (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362711829001qmUL
Data centers host servers that host various multicast applications such as collaboration, multimedia, video streaming, and conferencing. As video traffic is the highest bandwidth-consuming application in the network, it is imperative to implement architectures that most optimally forward traffic for multicast within the data center for east-west traffic and exiting/entering the data center for north-south traffic to/from external networks. This unique advanced-level session introduces Tenant Routed Multicast (TRM) routing in a VXLAN BGP EVPN fabric. The session will address the following questions: What problem does Tenant Routed Multicast address, and what are its benefits? What is an IPv4/IPv6 network architecture enabled for TRM? How do the control and data planes work in a network with TRM configured? What are the latest TRM innovations from Cisco? It is assumed the audience has a strong background in VXLAN BGP EVPN technology, MP-BGP, and fundamentals in IP multicast routing concepts such as PIM, and IGMP. You will leave this session with an introductory-level understanding of the TRM feature in VXLAN fabrics, applications, and architecture.

### PKI Automation: Adapting to Shorter Certificate Lifecycles for Enhanced Security - BRKSEC-2319
Speakers: Frank Michaud (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362780071001qyUD
The landscape of online security is rapidly evolving, driven by the critical need for enhanced crypto-agility and robust Public Key Infrastructure (PKI) management automation. This session addresses the industry-wide trend of shortening SSL/TLS certificate lifetimes, a pivotal change designed to boost security and encourage automated certificate operations. Participants will explore the significant implications of this shift for both public and private PKI environments. You'll leave this session with ideas to help you: Understand the imminent transition in public PKI, including the CABForum's move to 47-day certificate lifecycles and its industry impact. Implement best practices for managing private PKI with shorter certificate lifecycles, focusing on organic authorization and endpoint security. Develop effective automation strategies to navigate the complexities of frequent certificate revalidation at internet scale. This session is designed for network architects, security engineers, and IT professionals responsible for PKI infrastructure. A foundational understanding of public key cryptography and network security concepts is recommended.

### Plan, Provision, Validate: MCP-Enabled Network Automation - BRKDEV-2396
Speakers: Adrian Iliesiu (Cisco), Gabi Zapodeanu (Cisco), Pawan Singh (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362713836001qeHA
Network automation teams have already made strong progress with Ansible playbooks, Terraform plans, and Python SDK workflows running in production. Orchestrating these tools across multiple network controllers, and doing it consistently at scale, is still difficult. Each tool has its own interface, state model, and output format. Moving a change reliably from planning to provisioning to validation often requires deep knowledge of every step in the workflow, along with tiem consuming, manual verifications. Model Context Protocol (MCP) helps with this challenge by exposing existing automation as normalized tools that an AI agent can use end-to-end, without requiring you to rewrite what already works. In this session, we will walk through network management workflows using Ansible as the automation platform. We will introduce the core MCP concepts, including protocol fundamentals, MCP servers, tool design, and AI agents, and show how they connect to the Ansible workflows you already have. You will also see how AI and a natural language interface can help teams achieve consistent network management outcomes. Demos throughout the session will show how MCP transforms your existing IaC assets into a unified, intelligent workflow for planning, provisioning, and validation. No prior MCP or LLM experience is required, just curiosity. You will leave with practical examples, reusable code, and the confidence to start building your own MCP-powered automation.

Validation & Testing: Essential steps to ensure your design performs as expected in real-world environments. Prerequisites: A basic understanding of Wi-Fi and design principles.

Voice has emerged as one of the fastest-growing attack vectors, fueled by GenAI, deepfakes, and social engineering campaigns targeting enterprise environments. This session, designed for security, collaboration, and contact center professionals, explores the evolution of voice attacks and how Cisco UC, UCaaS, CC and CCaaS and Webex environments can be protected using two rapidly trending solutions: Call Reputation Intelligence Voice Firewall Attendees will learn: How AI is transforming voice-based attacks, and initial access threats How to apply real-time call reputation scoring within Webex Calling How to integrate a multi-layered Voice Firewall across the Cisco voice ecosystem Mutare's voice security solutions protect CUCM, CUBE, Cisco Unity Connection, Webex Cloud Contact Center and, Webex Calling.

### Protecting Your Gen AI Apps: A Beginner's Guide - BRKCLD-1006

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362681734001qTTv
With Large Language Models (LLMs), agents and Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers becoming a mainstay in our day to day vocabulary, it is important to understand the building blocks of AI applications you are building and/or consuming, the risks AI brings and how to secure against the same. This session will introduce basic components of LLM application and demystify some of the emerging trends and terminology in the space. We will then explore the evolving threat landscape of generative AI, with a focus on safety and security issues. We will examine key AI security frameworks, like OWASP Top 10 and MITRE ATLAS, to provide a structured understanding of potential risks. The talk will feature demonstrations of real-world threats, illustrating how adversaries can exploit generative models. Finally, we will introduce Cisco's AI Security capabilities and how these can be used to provide security visibility and mitigate AI threats and enhance security reducing risk across your AI application journey.

The session also breaks down which protocols to use for different visibility needs, where sensors should be placed, and how test intervals impact detection and troubleshooting. You'll learn: • When and why to use synthetic tests • How different protocols (ICMP, TCP, HTTP, etc.) expose different performance characteristics • Where and how to deploy sensors, from smart SFPs and hardware probes to software-based agents • How to set appropriate test intervals and packet rates based on use case and expected network behavior

### Quantum + AI: The Next Leap in Network Transformation and Operations - BRKNWT-2621
Speakers: Abhay Kaviya (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362748707001qwzY
Quantum computing and AI together are poised to create a fundamental shift in how networks are engineered, operated, and secured. This session explores how hybrid Quantum-AI models can solve deeply complex operational problems - traffic optimisation, fault prediction, spectrum scheduling, incident detection, and crypto management - that classical tools struggle with. Instead of replacing networks, quantum algorithms supercharge the control and analytics layers, while AI stabilises their probabilistic outputs into deterministic, production-grade decisions. For operators, this means faster convergence, predictive maintenance, fewer outages, improved SLA assurance, and a path toward self-optimising, self-securing networks. The talk translates quantum concepts into concrete operational workflows, showing exactly where Quantum + AI can plug into today's NOC, SOC, SDN, and automation architectures.

Real-world case studies and industry scenarios will demonstrate how organizations are navigating quantum readiness and capturing new opportunities. Interactive elements will enable deeper industry analysis and engagement. This session is designed for technology strategists, executives, and IT architects with a foundational understanding of cybersecurity and IT infrastructure.

### Quantum Safe Cryptography (and why you need it) - BRKSEC-2175
Speakers: Andrew Benhase (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362776736001quPC
This session will be an introduction for customers who need to learn about Quantum Resistance, Readiness, and ultimately a Quantum Safe network that protects their data in transit. We will show the customer what their path looks like for how to achieve a future implementation of Post Quantum algorithms for their existing IKEv2/IPSEC, and TLS 1.3 based operations, including what they can do today to meet readiness mandates.

### Quantum Secure Networks - BRKETI-2008
Speakers: Ashish Kundu (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362728813001qcoQ
Advances in Quantum computing, Quantum networks and error correction capabilities are taking the world closer and faster to Q-Day, when quantum computers can break classical cryptography such as RSA, and Diffie-Hellman. Such quantum threats to cryptography are based on the Shor's and Grover's quantum algorithms and their derivatives and are threatening the very foundations of security not only for networks but also for AI. In this talk, you will learn about the quantum threats, and how to protect your infrastructure from them in an end-to-end manner. We will explore how to make networks quantum safe and secure against these threats using post quantum cryptography (NIST PQC) and other techniques. We will present the systematic approach and framework at Cisco to address the quantum threats in a unified, holistic and adaptive manner. You will learn about the three components of this quantum resistance framework - (1) cryptography observability, (2) post-quantum resiliency, and (3) cryptography agility, and how they help an enterprise achieve quantum secure computing and networks. Join us to learn about how our cutting-edge solutions developed across various groups within Cisco help enterprises develop their cryptography supply chain (CBOM), cryptography dependency graphs, and prepare their networks to migrate to PQC, how to protect sensitive data from harvest-now-decrypt-later and how to govern quantum resistance in an adaptive manner.

### Quantum-Proofing the WAN: Implementing CNSA 2.0 on Cisco 8000 Series Secure Routers - CTF-2010
Speakers: Sumant Mali (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362812608001qer0
Quantum computing is redefining the cryptographic landscape. This session breaks down the mechanics of "Harvest Now, Decrypt Later" (HNDL) attacks and the urgency of securing long-term data. We will explore the strategic role of WAN infrastructure in mitigating these risks, detailing the implementation of CNSA 2.0-approved algorithms for encryption, authentication, and secure boot. You will gain technical insights into deploying Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC) across WAN architectures leveraging IPsec, MACsec, SSH and SD-WAN using the Cisco 8000 Series Secure Router. Join us to learn how to architect a resilient, future-ready network for the post-quantum era.

### Queue Intelligence in Action: Introducing the Queue Performance Index (QPI) - WOSPAR-1004
Speakers: Jason Forehand (ISI Analytics)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362848979001q2Uw
Customer expectations are rising, and operations teams can no longer rely on static reports or one-size-fits-all thresholds to manage call queue performance. What's needed is a more adaptive, intelligent way to understand when service levels are truly at risk. This session introduces the Queue Performance Index (QPI) - a dynamic queue health score that learns how each queue normally operates and surfaces issues only when they represent real customer impact. By analyzing patterns in missed calls, abandonment, and wait times, QPI helps teams quickly identify where attention is needed - without constant manual analysis. In addition, the session explores how Queue Analytics for Webex Calling Customer Assist is evolving into an AI-driven decision layer that automates trend detection and delivers clear, actionable insights. Participants will leave with practical ideas to: Move beyond static thresholds to adaptive, behavior-based queue performance monitoring Identify service risks earlier using AI-driven insights and anomaly detection Reduce manual investigation with automated trend analysis Improve operational response using prioritized, data-driven signals like QPI This session is designed for business leaders, operations managers, and collaboration teams seeking a clearer, more actionable understanding of queue performance. Real-world examples will illustrate how teams can proactively manage Customer Assist queues and prevent missed service opportunities.

Multiple practical use cases will be shown, covering day-to-day management, troubleshooting, and remediation.

As part of the enterprise customer journey towards digitization, there are multiple transitions happening. Firstly, applications are moving to cloud and secondly, proliferation of IoT endpoints which are getting connected to the network which cant host any endpoint security stack and running vulnerable SW versions. Security and risk management leaders are looking for more efficient, cost effective solutions for security orchestration and automation to try to keep up with unprecedented pace of changes within their business models, IT and OT Networks. All of these changes are putting tremendous stress on current and antiquated practices for security insertion. There are disparate models throughout the network in many cases with stateless ACL controls within the network and manual stitching of network security services with VLAN stitching to redirect network traffic through security services. In almost all cases, all traffic is treated the same and steered to security gateways for processing. This overprovisioning leads to bottlenecks. Any adds, moves or changes involve high OpEx of reconfiguration and testing of many devices throughout the network. In addition to the high cost of OpEx, there are implications of reduced time-to-value (TTV) for implementing new business services, which necessitates coordination between multiple operations groups (NetOps, SecOps, etc.). The efforts take weeks to months in some cases, usually involving planning, reviews, POCs, change management, and eventual manual provisioning. Not ideal and can result in human error. As part of this session, lets see how Security Service Insertion using Cisco Catalyst Center help to move away from physical topology-based security insertion, to intent-based security services.

### SD-WAN Troubleshooting from an Agentic AI perspective. - BRKTRS-1001
Speakers: Uma Mohanty (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362788057001qVvV
The evolution of modern networks has introduced unprecedented complexity, with network engineers now expected to manage not only connectivity but also automation and orchestration workflows. As a Network Engineer focusing on troubleshooting complex issues, require us to go through some extensive logs collected from the devices & controllers, which is tedious and at times takes a lot of cycles from our day to day work life. Our focus in this session will be on how we can make AI actively working towards a defined objective (log analysis) by interpreting context, applying reasoning, and potentially using tools to achieve that objective. This session will also presents an approach to building custom Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers using Cisco OpenAPI specifications, offering flexible and extensible solutions for contemporary network challenges. By integrating development workflows with tools like GitHub Copilot, network engineers can streamline code generation and automation tasks. Custom MCPs can play a critical role by enabling automated collection and analysis of network logs, as well as facilitating proactive responses to operational events. This framework empowers engineers to meet modern governance standards while simplifying the management of large-scale network environments.

### SR Flex-Algo - Simplifying Network Traffic Engineering with Flex-Algo - BRKMSI-2130
Speakers: Steven Blasiol (Cisco), Matt Breneisen (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362746653001q0WZ
The Problem: Legacy traffic engineering is a manual, state-heavy burden. Managing complex tunnels and RSVP-TE slows down operations, increases errors, and fails to meet the agility demands of modern networks. The Solution: Segment Routing Flex-Algo (SR-FA) strips away the complexity. By moving path intelligence directly into the IGP, SR-FA delivers a stateless, automated architecture that is easy to deploy and even easier to manage and demonstrate how to bridge the gap between theory and production using: App-Centric Routing: Prioritizing mission-critical workloads with low-latency paths. Secure Partitioning: Automated network slicing for multi-tenancy and compliance. Smart Automation: Real-world configuration snippets and workflows to replace manual tasks. You will leave this session with a practical blueprint to simplify your network and accelerate service delivery with flex-algo.

### SR-PM: Turning Segment Routing into a Measurable WAN - BRKMSI-2007
Speakers: Satya Narra (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362744827001qBtk
Service providers and large enterprises are under pressure to guarantee strict latency SLAs across Segment Routing-based WANs (SR-MPLS and SRv6), yet many networks still behave as "black boxes" with limited real-time insight into path delay. This session focuses on Segment Routing Performance Measurement (SR-PM), a built-in, standards-based toolkit to continuously measure latency along SR paths and policies without relying on external probes. Participants will learn how SR-PM uses STAMP-based probes, hardware and software timestamping, and flexible profiles to monitor one-way and two-way delay for IPv4/IPv6 endpoints, traffic-engineered paths, and VPN services. The session includes configuration examples, sample designs, and telemetry integration, and shows how to interpret histograms and time-series data when troubleshooting. You'll leave this session with ideas to help you: • Design Segment Routing WANs with deterministic, path-level latency visibility; • Deploy SR-PM delay measurement at scale for links, endpoints, and SR policies; • Use SR-PM telemetry to detect anomalies early and verify latency SLAs in production.

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362725686001qhsL

IOS XE software is now being enhanced with SRv6 alongside Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC) IPsec and MACsec, NAT, Firewall, and DPI capabilities for service rich Secure WAN deployments.

### Safeguarding the Internet's Backbone: Unleashing the Power of RPKI for Unbreakable IPv4 and IPv6 BGP Security - BRKENT-2179

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362724287001qciQ
BGP routes global internet traffic - but route hijacks by cybercriminals or nation-states can trigger chaos, from e-commerce blackouts to data theft. Real risks demand real defenses. RPKI is the cryptographic game-changer: it validates route origins, stops hijacks, and ensures trusted paths. This session provides the attendee: - Examples of Real World Incidents and how RPKI and other BGP capabilities could have thwarted them - Deployment strategies - Fully Functional Configuration Examples to begin your RPKI Journey

### Scalable Segmentation Orchestration with Asimily - WOSPAR-2051
Speakers: Shankar Somasundaram (www.asimily.com)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362868461001qFC5
Cisco ISE and pxGrid is deployed at many organizations. Yet many organizations' segmentation projects move slowly or stall. The gap between having ISE + pxGrid and successfully implementing segmentation comes down to four problems: insufficient device context beyond IP and MAC, no prioritization for which of your thousands of assets to segment first, lack of clarity on setting an effective segmentation strategy, and no ability to test a policy before it hits the network. This session introduces Asimily's Segmentation Orchestration approach that layers device intelligence, risk-based prioritization, automated policy recommendations - including Security Group ACLs (SGACLs) - on top of existing Cisco ISE infrastructure which is then augmented with the ability to simulate NAC policies. You'll leave this session with ideas to help you: Bring the Intelligence layer on top of Cisco ISE deployments to ensure segmentation projects can move forward Apply a progressive segmentation strategy that starts broad and adds granularity over time Use policy simulation and continuous validation to deploy segmentation policies with confidence

### Secure Firewall in ACI - BRKSEC-2992
Speakers: Fabien Gandola (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362782745001qLT1
In modern data centers, it is crucial to be able to insert, deploy, and operate next-generation firewalls (NGFWs) as easily as possible. This session won't make you a service insertion ACI expert nor a Cisco Secure Firewall expert in just 90 minutes. Instead, this session aims to explain and demonstrate how Cisco Firepower Threat Defense (FTD) can offer more than just any NGFW in ACI. We will first introduce very quickly the necessary key concepts on ACI to insert an NGFW, followed by the shortest Cisco Secure Firewall introduction ever. We will then cover the most common ways to insert NGFW nowadays, focusing mostly on service graphs leveraging L3 PBR. Finally, we will hit the key part of the session with few a key points that FTD brings to the table: The advantages of leveraging clustering, especially in multipod deployments The role of dynamic objects in FTD and how they can help accelerate enforcement, especially if leveraged by Cisco Secure Dynamic Attribute Connector (CSDAC) Integration with Cisco Secure Workload using FTD, for both policy and virtual patching The ability to automatically remediate with the remediation module in Firewall Management Center (FMC) The session is targeted at customers who already using FTD in ACI and wish to improve their integration, as well as those interested in implementing ACI and looking to learn about FTD insertion capabilities.

Our Managed XDR with built-in Managed Firewall gives them 24/7 coverage, threat detection, and response without requiring them to build the capability in-house.

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362722444001qBu7

### Secure by Design: How Nexus Dashboard Enables Segmented Operations Across Fabrics with role-based access control and multi-tenancy - BRKDCN-2638
Speakers: Varsha Dubey (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362704266001qHY3
As organizations consolidate data centers and share fabrics across multiple teams and environments, segmented operations become just as important as segmented traffic. In Nexus Dashboard, role-based access control (RBAC) and security domains are powerful but inherently broad - roles typically apply across all onboarded fabrics. Multi-tenancy complements RBAC by adding fine-grained isolation within a fabric: it abstracts tenants , VRFs and views so different RBAC roles only see and operate on "their" slice. Together, RBAC , security domains and multi-tenancy deliver a secure-by-design, least-privilege operating model across many fabrics. You'll leave this session with ideas to help you: Map your org structure (teams, tenants, environments, partner models) into a combination of RBAC roles, security domains and multi-tenant workspaces. Design segmented operations so RBAC secures who can do what, while multitenancy scopes where they can do it inside each fabric. Improve compliance, and delegate Day-2 tasks safely across multiple fabrics. You will see demo in Nexus Dashboard to build roles, tenants, and workspaces, then walk through customer-inspired workflows that show RBAC, security domains and multi-tenancy working together in practice.

### Secure the Network Edge against the DDoS Attacks! - BRKMSI-1001
Speakers: Raja Kolagatla (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362742824001qxEt
Latest DDoS trends indicate that L3-L4 attacks have increased, with 20% year on year in Q2 2024 and the average attack size of the L3-L4 attacks doubled since 2022. Traditional DDoS solutions can't scale with the above trends, making it prohibitively expensive for Service Providers to maintain large sized DDoS Scrubbers, in more distributed in tune with evolving Networks. Service Provider networks have also become the hotbed for the threat actors and also stand second in the targeted industry too. This serves as a wakeup call for the Service Providers, to fortify the core networks to become threat free. Cisco Secure DDoS Edge Protection is is an industry's first true on-box and CFO-Friendly solution, that stops DDoS attacks at the Service Provider Network Edge, with projected TCO savings up-to 83%, in comparison with traditional scrubber based solutions. Cisco Secure DDoS Edge Protection on routers detects and mitigates attacks autonomously in real time, optimizing the performance of low-latency applications. Goal of the session is to provide the high level architecture overview of the Cisco Secure Edge Protection solution, delve deeper into the use cases, showcase the TCO savings, provide customer success stories with this new innovative solution.

### Securing AI at Scale: Applying the ARMOR Framework to Cisco AI Factories - WOSPAR-2048
Speakers: Jillian Anderson (World Wide Technology)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362867766001qHrk
Enterprise AI adoption is accelerating rapidly, but many organizations are discovering that moving from pilots to production is being constrained less by compute availability and more by security, governance, and operational readiness. As AI workloads expand across hybrid environments, enterprises need a structured approach to secure, scale, and operate AI as a business?critical system - not an isolated experiment.? In this session, World Wide Technology (WWT) introduces how the ARMOR (AI Readiness Model for Operational Resilience) framework is applied to help organizations secure and operationalize Cisco AI Factories at scale. ARMOR addresses AI readiness across six domains: Governance, Risk & Compliance (GRC), Secure AI Operations, Model Protection, Secure Development Lifecycle (SDLC), Infrastructure Security, and Data Protection - providing a holistic, practical model for enterprise AI security and resilience.? You'll leave this session with ideas to help you:? - Assess AI readiness across the six ARMOR domains and identify gaps that prevent scaling AI securely? - Embed security, governance, and operational controls into AI platforms from day one - without slowing innovation? - Align AI infrastructure, operations, and risk management to support sustainable, hybrid AI deployments? This session is designed for enterprise architects, security leaders, infrastructure teams, and platform owners responsible for deploying or operating AI environments. Foundational understanding of enterprise infrastructure and security concepts is recommended, but no prior ARMOR experience is required.? WWT will draw on real?world partner implementations and examples to illustrate how ARMOR is used to move AI initiatives from experimentation to production, highlighting how a readiness?first approach enables secure, resilient, and scalable AI operations aligned to business outcomes.

### Securing AI at Scale: Operationalizing ARMOR and Cisco Frameworks for AI Resilience - ITLGEN-2023
Speakers: Istvan Berko (World Wide Technology), Amy Chang (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362818884001q8Si
Enterprise AI adoption is accelerating rapidly, but many organizations are discovering that moving from pilots to production is constrained less by compute availability and more by the absence of a comprehensive security and governance approach. In this session, World Wide Technology (WWT) introduces how the ARMOR (AI Readiness Model for Operational Resilience) framework is applied to help organizations secure and operationalize Cisco AI Factories at scale. ARMOR addresses AI readiness across six domains: Governance, Risk & Compliance (GRC), Secure AI Operations, Model Protection, Secure Development Lifecycle (SDLC), Infrastructure Security, and Data Protection - providing a holistic, practical model for enterprise AI security and resilience. WWT and Cisco will draw on real?world partner implementations and examples to illustrate how ARMOR and Cisco AI Security Frameworks are used to move AI initiatives to production, highlighting how a comprehensive readiness?first approach enables secure, resilient, and scalable AI operations aligned to business outcomes.You'll leave this session with ideas to help you: • Assess AI readiness across the six ARMOR domains and identify gaps that prevent scaling AI securely • Identify AI security threats using Cisco AI Security Framework • Embed security, governance, and operational controls into AI platforms from day one • Align AI infrastructure, operations, and risk management to support consistent, repeatable, and defensible all AI deployments This session is designed for IT and technology leaders (directors, VPs, CIOs, enterprise architects, security leaders, infrastructure teams) responsible for deploying or operating AI environments. Foundational understanding of enterprise infrastructure and security concepts is recommended.

### Securing Distributed Energy Operations: How CPFL Modernized Zero Trust with Cisco Secure Access - CTF-2001

The acceleration of cloud adoption and distributed operations has pushed utilities to confront unprecedented security challenges. For CPFL- one of Brazil's largest energy companies-these pressures were amplified by the need for a complete, efficient, and scalable secure-access framework capable of supporting 14,000 employees across power plants, field teams, substations, and corporate sites. Expanding attack surfaces, inconsistent user experiences and complexity underscored the urgency for a modern approach aligned with Zero Trust principles. This session will reveal how CPFL is transforming its cybersecurity foundation by evolving from a legacy Zscaler architecture to Cisco Secure Access. Beginning with Security Internet Access (SIA) and advancing toward Secure Private Access (SPA), CPFL is redefining how secure connectivity supports operational continuity at scale for a complete SSE strategy. This foundation also positions CPFL to address trust ambiguity and establish the security baseline required to advance into fraud-resilience frameworks. You'll leave this session with ideas to help you: See how this modernization improves performance and strengthens protection across cloud and on-premises environments Gain practical, end-to-end insights to deliver a complete SSE strategy. Streamline secure access to private and cloud applications Enhance Zero Trust outcomes through a real customer implementation with architecture insights and actionable lessons.
