### Segment Routing in a Nutshell - CISCOU-2348
Speakers: Peter Paluch (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362803564001qH2Z
Many of us have heard about Multi Protocol Label Switching (MPLS). For almost 30 years, it has provided the foundation for a myriad of services previously unfathomable in IP networking. Great technologies stay around, adapt, and evolve. So did MPLS! Its next generation is called Segment Routing. Keeping the well-known functionality from its MPLS predecessor and reviving the concept of source routing, Segment Routing brings some fascinating innovations and approaches to the packet forwarding process, pushing the boundaries yet again. This session aims to provide an introduction into the world of Segment Routing, highlight the main similarities and differences to MPLS, and offer an insight into two Segment Routing flavors: SR-MPLS and SR-IPv6.

### Signal to Enforcement: A Coordinated Security Walkthrough - WOSPAR-1040
Speakers: Correine Wiechec (Cisco), Rob Gresham (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780412388897001HV9X
Modern attacks rarely stay confined to a single domain, yet most security operations still respond through disconnected tools and fragmented workflows. In this Cisco Live walkthrough, Cisco and strategic ecosystem partners demonstrate how signals across identity, SaaS, device, and network environments can be transformed into coordinated enforcement actions through an integrated security architecture. Using a single attacker path as the narrative, attendees will follow how phishing activity, identity exposure, SaaS anomalies, unmanaged devices, and packet-level network evidence intersect across the enterprise. The session highlights how Cisco acts as a centralized decision layer, correlating telemetry from Red Sift, SpecterOps, AppOmni, Ordr, and Endace to drive coordinated enforcement through Duo and ISE. Rather than focusing on isolated alerts, this walkthrough demonstrates how organizations can reduce operational gaps between detection and response, improve investigative confidence, and operationalize cross-domain security enforcement at enterprise scale.

### Simple Leaf/Spine with a Touch of ToR - Network Designs for the Modern Data Center - BRKDCN-2967

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362710268001qkET
This session will step back and review the rationale behind classic 3-tier data center design and some of the deployment history in conjunction with virtual port channel (vPC) technology. We will review the challenges that have arisen with 3-tier data center designs and how those architectures are now evolving to overlay based fabric buildouts. We will outline how the data center requirements are transitioning to accommodate changing traffic patterns based on modern application development and how that's driving overlay fabric solutions. We will introduce VXLAN EVPN as the primary overlay fabric solution being deployed in the data center and how it's best suited to modern data center use cases. We will start with single fabric VXLAN EVPN designs and expand to multi-site data center deployments where we are engineering for high availability, fault tolerance, and optimal performance characteristics. Finally, we will examine the growingly important role network security and network operations play for customers as data center modernization efforts move forward

### Simplified and Resilient Campus Networks with EVPN Multihoming - BRKENS-2837

The independent and flexible system management and maintenance upgrade procedures, empower enterprise customers to safeguard their critical modern business communications in mission-critical environment.

### Simplifying Operations and Upgrades on Your Nexus Switch using Modular NXOS Software - BRKDCN-2639
Speakers: Samer Theodossy (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362704485001qLrq
This session will start with a discussion of the NX-OS overall system architecture and discuss upgrade procedures and best practices on Nexus switches for both the stand-alone and ACI product lines and what the best approaches are to take. Customers want ease of operations on their systems with Cisco software, and one of the biggest areas is upgrades and changing versions of software. Cisco's goal is to focus on best-in-class high availability. In-Service Software Upgrade (ISSU) is a major table-stake feature that requires coordination and patching. Customers want the ability to choose the software components based on deployment scenario and hardware they are using and there is a need to provide a more modular software to accommodate these goals and help bring lower memory footprint to Nexus switches. We will see how the Nexus architecture lends itself to the modular/optional design and will discuss how this helps with creating an NXOS software image that is more lean and provides a smaller memory footprint. In addition, this will lead to asynchronous delivery of features/fixes which can be deployed on the system. With the introduction of feature optionality, we will discuss how this provides the flexibility to remove unused RPM packages and how to install/uninstall an optional RPM package on the system.

### Simplifying Security Policy Visibility - WOSPAR-2063
Speakers: Tim Woods (FireMon)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362870972001qNW9
Speed and Visibility is Modern Security: Detect Faster, Respond Smarter, Reduce Risk As organizations expand across hybrid enterprise environments, maintaining strong cybersecurity becomes increasingly challenging. Rapid growth, distributed systems, and constant change often lead to fragmented network security policies, limited visibility, and rising operational complexity. These conditions create opportunities for misconfigurations, human error, and exploitable vulnerabilities. At the same time, the window to detect and respond to policy-related risks is rapidly shrinking. Threat actors are increasingly leveraging AI-driven techniques to identify and exploit security gaps faster than ever before. What once took days or weeks can now happen in minutes, compressing the required reaction time for defenders. In this environment, reliance on manual processes and multiple, disconnected management consoles is no longer sufficient. For business and technology leaders, it is critical to clearly understand how security policies are created, managed, and changed across the enterprise. Poor network security policy hygiene not only increases risk but also introduces unnecessary complexity, slowing innovation and complicating governance. This presentation explores how organizations can reduce risk by simplifying and standardizing network security policies across hybrid environments. It highlights the importance of establishing a centralized security policy control plane that provides a single, unified lens into all network security policies. With improved visibility, organizations can rapidly detect bad changes, enforce consistency, and respond to emerging threats at machine speeds. We will also examine how automation and continuous policy validation can strengthen policy hygiene, reduce manual errors, and ensure alignment with business intent. By prioritizing simplicity, visibility, and speed, organizations can move beyond reactive security practices and build a more resilient, adaptive defense posture.

### Simplifying Your SASE Implementation with Meraki SD-WAN and Cisco Secure Access to Deliver Zero Trust for the Network - BRKENT-2125
Speakers: Kenneth Pinto (Cisco), Ryan Shoemaker (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362723599001qNcl
Deploying a SASE architecture can be challenging, often due to the complexity of integrating SSE with SD-WAN. Cisco's Meraki SASE solution addresses this challenge by offering a simple-to-implement, highly effective, single-vendor platform that eliminates the need for administrators to manage disparate systems. Instead, they can focus on defining precise security and network policies. In this session, attendees will discover how Meraki SD-WAN seamlessly integrates into Cisco Secure Access through AutoVPN, enabling a unified and scalable approach to secure network connectivity. ### Solving the Segmentation Puzzle with Secure Workload - BRKSEC-2161
Speakers: Jorge Quintero (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362775599001qrx5
In a world of application workloads deployed anywhere, at any time, and with multi-cloud solutions, applying network security controls is no longer a trivial task. The policy control toolset just keeps growing, with multiple enforcement points in the network to protect our application workloads using different approaches such as the host firewalls, network firewalls and SDN controllers, or cloud-based in the form of security groups. With different teams managing each policy control, and usually working in organizational siloes, there is no wonder why it often leads to inconsistent islands of policy controls across the environment. Secure Workload has been solving this puzzle, by defining a common policy model across all of these enforcement points (host-based, network-based and cloud-based) harmonizing all policy controls into an effective Zero-Trust Segmentation policy. This session will navigate through the Network/NetSec team lenses on how you can leverage Secure Workload to define a common policy model for your applications using agent and agentless approaches to protect your application workloads regardless of their form factor (baremetal, VM or container) or location (on-prem or multi-cloud).

### Strategies and Services for a Quantum-Safe Security Transition - BRKCX-1007

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362700519001qar9
As quantum computing technology rapidly advances, it poses a significant threat to current security systems by potentially breaking widely used cryptographic algorithms. This session is designed for IT professionals and security operators with a foundational understanding of cryptographic principles. ### The AI Security Paradox: How Cisco and AWS Are Closing the Gap Between Innovation and Zero Trust - BRKPAR-2007

Generative AI and agentic AI are reshaping the enterprise at unprecedented speed. In this session we will show how the AWS and Cisco strategic partnership is enabling customers accelerate AI innovation without compromising security. You'll see how Zero Trust principles must evolve for generative AI and agentic workloads and walk through architectural patterns for securing the entire AI lifecycle.

### The Atomic Shift: Understanding Atomic Configuration Replace (ACR) for Modern Network Management - BRKDEV-1006
Speakers: Story DeWeese (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362713395001q8FD
Still relying on incremental configuration changes that leave your network vulnerable to drift and partial failures? Atomic Configuration Replace (ACR), built on NETCONF /YANG, eliminates these risks with an all-or-nothing approach - every deployment either succeeds completely or doesn't get applied at all. In this session, we'll describe how ACR works under the hood, walk through the end-to-end workflow - validation, rollback, and failure recovery - and demonstrate a real-world automation pipeline using Ansible and NetBox as the Source of Truth to deploy configurations atomically to Catalyst devices. Whether you're just starting with network programmability or scaling automation across your enterprise, this session gives you the knowledge to adopt ACR with confidence. Pair it with our companion hands-on workshop to put these concepts into practice.

### The Enterprise AI Flight Manual with Webex Contact Center - BRKCCT-1027

The 2-Minute Paradox: You can build a Webex AI Agent in 2 minutes. But can you trust it with 10,000 customer conversations when it's processing complex queries, handling digit inputs, and managing barge-in events in real-time? Most organisations discover the gap between "build time" and "run time" only after deployment - when agents hallucinate, escalations lose context, filler phrases disrupt flow, multi-agent transfers failing without context, and no one can explain routing decisions. This 90-minute session is your enterprise AI flight manual - a consulting-grade framework with best practices moving from AI Agent build through operations to measurable ROI. Leveraging Webex's latest innovations including 90% faster knowledge ingestion, advanced voice capabilities (barge-in, custom vocabulary, filler phrase control), AI-powered call summaries, custom data integration, and multi-agent orchestration, we'll navigate deployment across autonomous AI agents, AI-enhanced human agents, and proactive customer engagement and review with experience. You'll learn: Strategic use case selection, knowledge base management, live agent building with prompt engineering, voice configuration optimisation (language variables, special character handling), multi-agent transfer patterns with context preservation, the "1/10 Problem" mitigation, AI Quality Management frameworks,observability with enhanced transcripts and recording, human-agent synergy, ROI measurement, and governance. Take home: Ready-to-deploy reference implementations, Voice and Webex Connect flows with multi-agent patterns and voice configuration templates, best practice playbook for production, and Contact Center Reporting ROI dashboards for production-grade AI operations.

### The Enterprise Network as a Decision Platform Evolving Beyond Campus, WAN, and Edge in the Age of AI - WOSPAR-2047
Speakers: Morgan Spriggs (Computacenter)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362867534001qqtv
As enterprise networks evolve from manual operations toward greater automation and autonomy, their role is fundamentally changing. Networks are no longer just responsible for connectivity - they are increasingly expected to interpret context, enforce intent, and make decisions in real time. In this session, we explore how AI, identity?centric security, and cloud?native architectures are reshaping the enterprise network itself. Traditional constructs such as campus, WAN, and edge were designed for predictable traffic flows and human?driven control. In an AI?driven world, those assumptions no longer hold. We introduce a new architectural perspective: the enterprise network as a decision platform - one that integrates identity, policy, telemetry, and automation to deliver context?aware outcomes across users, devices, and applications. Join us to understand how organizations can evolve existing network investments into this new model, why human governance remains essential, and how the enterprise network is being redefined to support increasingly intelligent, adaptive systems.

### The Inner Workings of QoS for Wi-Fi 7 Networks - BRKEWN-2060

We will examine QoS deployment recommendations when using Cisco Wireless solutions. We will conclude by taking a look into the future of Wi-Fi8.

### The New Digital Substation: More Efficient, More Secure, and Ready for Demanding Modern Grid Applications - BRKIIOT-2015

The new Substation Automation solution architecture overhauls the substation network to drive out costs, improve security, and support demanding new smart grid applications, while reliably providing control and visibility of substation operations. In this session, we will review the updated network and security architecture, which is enhanced with new industrial routing (IR8300) and switching (IE9300) infrastructure, runs industrial cybersecurity with Cisco Cyber Vision, supports multiple WAN options including SD-WAN, and deploys software-defined network management with Cisco Catalyst Center (formerly DNA Center) and Catalyst SD-WAN Manager (formerly vManage) to reduce maintenance effort, increase uptime, and simply and consistently deploy the network. The solution has rich cybersecurity features, such as establishing an Electronic Security Perimeter for critical substation systems, deploying TrustSec-based micro-segmentation, and providing the visibility of substation devices and protocols with Cyber Vision.We will also explore typical DNP3 based architectures and look forward to what IEC61850 will bring to the substation environment.

### The New, QUIC Based Encrypted Protocol Stack Taking over the Internet and How to Deal with It - BRKSPM-2024
Speakers: Andreas Enotiadis (Cisco), Bart Van de Velde (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362786699001qfwe
Internet traffic is transitioning from TCP to QUIC very fast, QUIC already being 50% of total traffic and doubling every 18 months. QUIC, together with HTTP/3, DoH, and eSNI/ECH, obfuscates the traffic between applications and changes it's behaviour with applications now managing flow control and disintermediating the network. Application visibility is eliminated by this stack through TLS1.3 encryption, congestion management transitions to the application and the key assumptions SP networks are built on are rapidly being obsoleted. In this session, we will show how this protocol stack is constructed and evolving, its behaviour in terms of both visibility and flow control, and the impact it has on infrastructure elements. We will also discuss the techniques SPs can use to keep pace with it, enabling better traffic visibility, QoE monitoring and management, and efficient use of 5G spectrum and other scarce resources.

### The Power of Cisco SD-Access LISP Fabric: Simplified Deployment to Advanced Use Cases - BRKENS-2805

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362718819001qtjk

You'll leave with practical deployment strategies, proven best practices, and the confidence that your SD-Access network can deliver the flexible automation, scalable control, and unified identity-driven security your enterprise needs - today and tomorrow. Prerequisites: Prior SD-Access knowledge recommended. Review BRKENS-2810, BRKENS-2811, and BRKENS-2814 in the Cisco Live On-Demand Library. For foundational concepts, see BRKENS-1804 (Part 1: Fabric Deployment Fundamentals) - covering LAN automation, zero-touch onboarding, ISE integration, and policy-based segmentation. For deeper technical scenarios, refer to BRKENS-2804 from CLUS San Diego (Part 2: Advanced Scenarios), which covers the LISP Pub/Sub control plane, dynamic borders, LISP extranet deployments, and multi-Catalyst Center / ISE integrations.

### The Pyramid of Pain: Persuading Attackers to Take Their Ball and Go Home - PSOSEC-2347

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362841555001qlub

We'll map these capabilities to the Pyramid of Pain, showing how to systematically raise the cost of every move an adversary makes. The goal isn't just to stop attacks - it's to make your environment such a frustrating target that attackers take their ball and go home. ### The Quantum Threat Is Already Here. Is Your Network Ready? - WOSPAR-1025
Speakers: Eric Hay (Quantum XChange)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362853428001qfhQ
Nation-states are collecting your encrypted data today with the intent to decrypt it once quantum computers arrive. Cryptographically-relevant quantum computers (CRQCs) are expected as early as 2029, and the window to act is closing faster than most organizations realize. This session breaks down the quantum threat across three distinct time horizons: the risks your organization faces right now, the compounding danger of Harvest Now, Decrypt Later (HNDL), and what active quantum attacks look like once CRQCs arrive. You will leave this session with a clear understanding of: Why static private keys stored on endpoints are a critical vulnerability requiring immediate attention, with no quantum computer required to exploit them How HNDL puts your most sensitive data at risk today across financial services, healthcare, federal government, and commercial enterprise Why a single-algorithm defense will not hold up as the quantum threat evolves, and how a resilient architecture is needed to secure the network This session is designed for security, IT, and business leaders responsible for data protection strategy. No advanced cryptography background required

### The Voice Detective: Troubleshooting Wireless Call Drops and Quality Issues on Catalyst 9800 - TACENT-2040

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780412385388001HszV

### Their Playbook, Your Rules - Ruining Your Adversary's Day - BRKSEC-3026

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362783195001q59I

We start with a detailed threat analysis of an operation based on threat research and MITRE's ATT&CK to look into how the threat actor's actions and tools looks from the inside out and how they try to bypass today's security measurements to fly under the radar. With the knowledge collected in each step of the journey, we will use various methods to detect or mitigate the threat from within your environment. We will discuss the tactics used, the indicators found, and the impact on the victim in a recent threat. By the time the session closes, we'll all have the skills we need to better explain the adversary's behavior, the detections that uncover it, and the actions to mitigate it effecively. This session is targeted at security engineers and ambitious NetSec practitioners, or anyone who wants to learn how to tell better non-fiction cybersecurity stories.

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362785066001qzV2

It also explains how to monitor VOQ statistics and distinguish between credit-based drops and queue-limit exhaustion. Attendees will leave with a clear framework for troubleshooting data-plane loss in VOQ-based architectures with confidence.

By using the same tools and methods as our support experts, you will gain the skills to pinpoint performance bottlenecks and keep your network running at its best.

Additionally, we will collaboratively analyze examples from lab recreations to pinpoint the underlying issues. We will wrap up the session by discussing the Linux command "tcpdump" and how it can be useful in troubleshooting different connection issue within the FTDs. ### Troubleshooting the Session Initiation Protocol (SIP) - BRKCOL-2143
Speakers: Paul Giralt (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362688883001qpKq
The Session Initiation Protocol (SIP) is the de-facto standard for real-time collaboration session establishment. It is core to Cisco Webex Calling and the Cisco on-premises collaboration portfolio on products such as Unified Communications Manager (UCM) and the Cisco Unified Border Element (CUBE) as well as many 3rd party products. Having a solid understanding of the protocol is essential to understanding how to troubleshoot signaling flows across a variety of products and systems. This session will primarily focus on understanding of the protocol itself and will try to remain relatively product agnostic. The session will start with the basic format of SIP messages and how they are used to set up and tear down calls, then will dive deeper into more complex call flows. We will cover session establishment, requests and responses, media negotiation including SDP and ICE, session refresh, invoking supplementary services (hold, transfer, conference, etc...), and securing SIP with TLS and SRTP. We will also briefly cover tools that can be used to more easily parse through SIP logging information to make troubleshooting easier. After completing this session, you should have a firm understanding of how to troubleshoot a SIP call flow on any platform that supports SIP.

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362791893001qcY6

### Turning Failures into Recovery: Why AI Needs Edge Deployment and Console Access - WOSPAR-2020
Speakers: Mike Sale (ZPE Systems), Gabriela Markette (ZPE Systems)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362861382001qtwj
AI is becoming central to how infrastructure is monitored and managed, detecting anomalies, correlating events, and taking action. However, most AI systems depend on production networks being available. When devices become unreachable, visibility is lost and AI-driven operations become ineffective. This session focuses on two key areas: Closing the visibility gap: Participants will learn how traditional observability breaks down during outages, and how extending visibility to console and KVM interfaces, traditionally invisible to observability platforms yet accessible when network devices are down, provides the missing link enabling AI to detect, diagnose, and remediate during complete failures. Enabling resilient AI at the edge: Participants will understand why agentic AI should preferably operate on an isolated management layer, separate from production infrastructure, enabling continued detection, diagnosis, and action during failures. You will leave this session with ideas to help you understand: AI-driven operations are only as effective as the visibility they have; traditional observability breaks down when networks are unavailable Agentic AI deployed on an isolated edge management layer enables detection, diagnosis, and action even when primary infrastructure is down How to architect isolated management infrastructure enabling autonomous remediation at the source

Cisco are launching Ultra Secure Webex Meetings at Cisco Live US 2026 A new industry paradigm, where Zero Trust Encryption and Identity become the default setting for meetings and calls and not an optional feature This new security initiative introduces new industry unique features: 1) Webex standards based Zero Trust End to End Encryption (E2EE) support for all Webex clients (desktop, mobile, web browser (new)) and Cisco Video devices and IP Phones (new) - Now any Webex client or device can use E2EE 2) Webex standards based Zero Trust End to End Identity (E2EI) support - using customer provided user identity certificates for Identity Validation and Deepfake Prevention 3) Zero Trust Encryption and Identity for ALL Webex meetings - Meetings hosts can use strict E2EE (with feature limitations today), or select to enable E2EE and E2EEI in all standard meetings, if the participants support it, with all of their standard Webex Meeting features 4) Zero Trust Encryption and Identity for all Webex 1:1 calls 5) Zero Trust meetings with trusted on-premises services such as : Recording, AI summarization and SIP Phone joins

### Under the hood of IOS-XE EVPN/VXLAN on Catalyst 9000 - BRKENS-3840
Speakers: Ramchander Nadipally (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362721062001qErR
Ethernet VPN (EVPN) has become de-facto an industry standard for data center fabrics and L2/L3 VPN connectivity in service provider. EVPN functionality was also expanded into Campus networks, to address the drive of network operators toward simplicity and automation with a unified inter-domain connectivity technology. This session covers in detail packet path in the Fabric in case BUM, bridged and routed flows with verification of the Fabric states and deep explanation of the CLI outputs of the show commands for relevant components. Focus of the session is to build deep knowledge of EVPN "mechanics" on Catalyst 9000 and a clear understanding of the process of packet forwarding and related verification methods.

### Understanding AI Risks in the Enterprise - BRKSEC-1043
Speakers: Katherine McNamara (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362768807001qkYI
As artificial intelligence and machine learning become integral to modern enterprise infrastructure, the attack surface for these complex systems has expanded significantly. This session provides a exploration of the security challenges inherent in generative AI, moving beyond traditional cybersecurity paradigms to address vulnerabilities unique to ML-based architectures. Attendees will gain a foundational understanding of the four critical components - Model, Data, Application, and System - that form the backbone of today's AI-driven solutions and why securing these layers is essential for maintaining organizational integrity. We will dive into the evolving threat landscape, covering critical attack vectors such as direct and indirect prompt injection, AI jailbreaking, and the exploitation of integrated components like SQL databases and web interfaces. Through technical analysis, we will examine how attackers manipulate LLMs to exfiltrate sensitive data, bypass safety restrictions, and execute malicious code. The discussion will also highlight the risks associated with third-party model repositories, insecure function handling, and the dangers of "pickling" and steganography, providing a clear picture of how these vulnerabilities can lead to full system compromise.

### Understanding AI, Identifying Model Vulnerabilities, and Building Real-World Protections - CISCOU-1067
Speakers: Steve McNutt (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362799731001q0sg
The rapid growth of generative AI is driving innovation faster than security can keep pace, creating new risks tied directly to how modern model architectures operate. As AI becomes embedded in business processes, security teams must quickly understand and mitigate emerging AI-native threats that target models, data, and users. This session opens with an accessible explanation of how AI systems function, giving attendees the context needed to understand why models behave unpredictably. It then moves into the core mechanics of transformer architectures and shows how their design introduces attack paths that adversaries can exploit. Live demonstrations highlight prompt manipulation, representation drift, and other vulnerabilities that allow attackers to influence outputs and bypass safeguards. The final segment focuses on defense by mapping real threats to practical frameworks, including MITRE ATLAS and the OWASP GENAI project, and showing how Cisco AI Defense delivers operational controls to detect and reduce AI risk across the enterprise. The session is built for architects and security practitioners who understand core cybersecurity concepts and want a clear, demonstration-driven introduction to AI security without requiring deep mathematical expertise.

In addition, we will review a few examples of how similar UCM features can be configured in Webex Calling to accomplish the same goal.

### Understanding Wi-Fi for a Wireless First World - BRKEWN-2059
Speakers: Mark Krischer (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362732143001qKJ3
Would you like a better understanding of RF and how it impacts the design of wireless networks? Do you like knowing the "how" and the "why"? Wireless networks function within the electromagnetic spectrum, presenting unique challenges distinct from those of wired networks with their shielded cables. This session will demystify the core principles of RF networking at the physical (PHY) layer and bridges the gap to the 802.11 medium access (MAC) layer protocols, exploring contention mechanisms to coordinate access to a shared medium. We'll also delve into the latest 6 GHz wireless regulations and their impact on next-generation Wi-Fi 6E and Wi-Fi 7 technologies. Equip yourself with an understanding of the fundamentals of Wi-Fi to engineer robust wireless networks capable of supporting the most intensive real-time applications in a Wireless First World.

### Understanding Wireless Security and the Implications for Secure Wireless Network Design - BRKEWN-2104
Speakers: Mark Krischer (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362732816001qck2
This session explores secure wireless network design with a key focus on the latest 802.11 and Wi-Fi Alliance standards. Mobility brings unique challenges to network security, such as the need for strong authentication and support for secure fast roaming. Participants will learn how to address these requirements and will examine the changes brought by version 3 of the Wireless Protected Access standard and the implications for wireless deployments. Newer standards like Wi-Fi 6E and Wi-Fi 7 will also have an impact on security design, as will emerging threats from future quantum computers. We will also discuss the role of Catalyst Center and Meraki Dashboard, and how they expand on the wireless security standards to provide visibility with rogue access point detection and location, and advanced wireless intrusion detection and prevention. The intent is to provide a deeper understanding of wireless security capabilities from the perspective of the attacks they defend against.

### Understanding how Provision and Single Sign-On works for Collaboration - BRKCOL-2166

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362689321001qkME
We will review best practices for bridging identity between cloud and on-premises environments, and discuss when and how to leverage different protocols and mechanisms (SCIM, LDAP, REST API) for user provisioning. We will explore how our collaboration products implement single sign-on (SSO) and the available options for both cloud and on-premises products when using client applications such as Cisco Webex. We will discuss how protocols like OAuth, SAML, and OIDC can be used to deliver single sign-on. We will explain how the user's operating system interacts with the Webex App to deliver SSO, as well as the different authentication methods requested by the identity provider (IdP).

### Understanding the Quantum Threat and How to Protect Your Organization From It - BRKETI-1402
Speakers: Tim Szigeti (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362728360001qcst
This session cuts through media hype to deliver a clear view of the quantum threat to existing networks. To fully understand this threat, an overview of quantum computing is needed to provide key insights into security-concerning quantum algorithms, like Shor's and Grover's. Once we lay this foundation, we can definitively answer whether symmetric encryption systems (such as Advanced Encryption Standard [AES]) and asymmetric encryption systems (such as Public Key Infrastructure [PKI] are at risk - as well as explain why or why not. We will also discuss the latest advances in quantum computing to better estimate how close we are to a Cryptographically Relevant Quantum Computer (CRQC) and how many Years-to-Quantum (YTQ) - as it may be sooner than many think. Furthermore, we will also discuss the three key requirements to become quantum safe, as well as overview the various paths you can take to secure your communications and infrastructure from these threats, including Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC), Quantum Key Cryptography (QKD), Bring Your Own Key (BYOD), as well hybrid options. Finally, we will also introduce Cisco solutions and services to become quantum safe.

It will only focus on key new features along with the integration uses cases with the Collaboration AI POD.

### Unified API Operations: Mastering Cisco Multi-Domain Automation with Wingpy - CISCOU-1061
Speakers: Andreas Stenderup Baekdahl (Wingmen Solutions)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362798533001q9I5
As network platforms have evolved into controller-based architectures, interacting with the diverse REST APIs of Catalyst Center, SD-WAN, Firewall Management Center, Meraki and many others has become a core competency for the modern network engineer. Because each platform is optimized for its specific domain, engineers often face varying requirements for authentication, session management, pagination, and rate-limiting. These are complexities you normally handle manually in every script, creating a high entry barrier for those new to network automation. This session introduces Wingpy, an open-source Python abstraction layer designed to be "the Netmiko for REST APIs.", by providing a unified interface that manages the underlying "housekeeping" of multi-domain Cisco platforms Wingpy allows teams to script against APIs with simplified code that is easier to read, write and maintain.

### Unified CM Security Architecture: Technical Foundations for Deployment - BRKCOL-2226
Speakers: Scott Kiewert (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362690200001qmcI
Historically, securing Cisco Unified Communications Manager (Unified CM) deployments involved complex processes like Certificate Authority Proxy Function (CAPF) enrollment, managing client certificates (LSC), and explicit Certificate Trust Lists (CTL/ITL). While effective, this approach often led to significant administrative overhead, fragile trust models, and challenges in adapting to modern cloud-centric environments. This session dives into the evolution of Unified CM security, showcasing standards-based architecture designed for "always-on" security with reduced complexity. The session will also explore how the transition from traditional methods to a robust framework leveraging OAuth 2.0, Public Key Infrastructure (PKI) X.509 certificate chain validation, and streamlined certificate management is reshaping UC security. Key topics covered will include: • Client and Device Trust through PKI: Discover standard PKI X.509 certificate chain validation for trust establishment. Topics include managing certificate trust stores and trust lists (CTL/ITLs), incorporating enterprise CAs, Online Certificate Status Protocol (OCSP), and certificate onboarding with Enrollment over Secure Transport (EST) • Certificate Management and Encryption: Delve into certificate count reduction through multi-server (SAN) certificates and certificate reuse and TLS 1.3 support • API Protection with OAuth 2.0: Understand how OAuth has become the cornerstone for securing all client-facing APIs across soft clients and desk phones. Learn about authorization flows, access and refresh tokens, and the benefits of this optimized security modelSimplified • Device Onboarding and Provisioning: Explore phone onboarding methods, including Activation Code Onboarding (on-premises and Mobile Remote Access (MRA)) and Zero-Touch Onboarding (on-premises). Learn how these methods securely deliver OAuth tokens and trust anchors, enabling seamless mobility between on-premises and MRA Attendees will leave with an understanding of how to implement a secure Unified CM environment, significantly reducing administrative overheads and enhancing security.

You'll leave this session with ideas to help you detect problematic telemetry as well as exporters sending duplicate NetFlow data, which can lead to unnecessary consumption of Flows Per Second (FPS) licenses. The session is designed for network and security professionals with a working knowledge of SNA and NetFlow/IPFIX telemetry. Real-world examples and troubleshooting techniques will be demonstrated to enhance your operational efficiency and optimize license usage.

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362767691001qJ8E

### Using SRv6 to unify Wireless Networks and eBPF to extend SR to the Application and applying it to the Critical Communications and Defense spaces - BRKSPG-2046
Speakers: Andreas Enotiadis (Cisco), Bart Van de Velde (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362786013001qm7e
Wireless networks, especially in field conditions such as defence and critical communication scenarios, are by nature denied, degraded and intermittent. In this session, we will discuss how we leverage segment routing v6 as a common unifying layer across multiple wireless infrastructures such as 5G, WiFi, CURWB and satellite. We will show how SRv6 can unify the various disparate technologies into a common IP layer that enables application intent and constraints to be dynamically reflected in the network path. We will also show how we can leverage technologies such as eBPF and Cilium to extend the SRv6 network all the way to the application through APIs, and how this is applied to the defence and critical communication space. We will be joined by the CTO of BWI, the IT arm of the German Armed Forces who will explain how the requirements drive the architecture and enable real use cases in the field.

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362866170001q3Ow

### What is Cisco Doing in Quantum? An Introduction to Quantum Mechanics and Networking - BRKETI-1401
Speakers: Tim Szigeti (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362728141001q4kj
Cisco is making a major R&D investment in quantum networking, as future communication systems equipped with quantum networks will open new possibilities in several technology domains, including distributed quantum computing, secure communications, global clock synchronization, high-accuracy sensors, and more. This is an introductory session to the science and technologies that enable quantum networking. This session will begin with a brief introduction to quantum mechanics, with a focus on quantum bits (qubits) and some of their special properties, including superposition, entanglement, and no-cloning (Note: this discussion will include some mathematical concepts and proofs). This will be followed by an overview of quantum networking, including quantum teleportation and entanglement-swapping. Finally, we'll bring it all together to outline the building blocks, opportunities, and challenges of building quantum networks, as well as highlight what Cisco is already doing in this exciting space.

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362773382001qRu8

This session explores how these tools differ in scope, function, and outcome. While Cisco XDR Forensics provides acquisitions of endpoint activity tied directly to incidents, Orbital delivers deep, on-demand queries and scripts into system state using live telemetry. We will follow the same incident through both tools, revealing how each tells its own story and uncovers unique insights. Real-world use cases will highlight where each tool shines, how they complement each other, and when to leverage one versus the other. Whether you're responding to an incident or proactively hunting threats, understanding these tools capabilities will empower more informed, efficient decision-making.

### When 802.1X Meets SDWAN. Solving Authentication issues for wireless clients - TACENT-2034
Speakers: Daniel Saavedra Gonzalez (Cisco), Luis Angel Frias Ramirez (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780412384002001HGyZ
Wireless authentication failures in SD-WAN environments often stem from IP fragmentation during RADIUS 802.1X EAP-TLS handshakes. In this session, we'll pull back the curtain on Radius-based 802.1X authentication with EAP-TLS handshakes. From IP Fragmentation to MTU analysis, we'll explore the handshake process, explain why fragmentation breaks authentication flows and walk through multiple configuration approaches to resolve these issues. Learn the common pifalls that keep teams stuck in troubleshooting and gain actionable strategies to prevent authentication failures before they happen.

### When the overlay meets the underlay: How to integrate your Campus Fabric with a SRv6 WAN - BRKMSI-2008
Speakers: Nigel Gocan (Cisco), Tiago Lugarini Silva (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362745048001qN4y
Segment Routing over IPv6 (SRv6) is the foundation for scalable, deterministic WAN transport in service provider networks, while enterprises increasingly rely on policy-driven overlays to express application intent from Campus Fabrics. This session shows how overlay intent is translated into underlay SRv6 Flex-Algo transport slices using IOS-XR SRv6 cores and IOS-XE service edges to enable SLA-aware services at scale. Attendees will gain practical design patterns, automation workflows, and validation techniques to deliver deterministic SRv6 transport services into campus domains.

### Where does Cisco IT use AI to manage their networks? Everywhere! - BRKCOC-2033
Speakers: Chris Groves (Cisco), Manny Garcia (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362684210001qvcL
In this session, we'll explore how Cisco IT leverages Artificial Intelligence (AI) across the entire lifecycle of our global network infrastructure. From initial deployment in new buildings to daily operations and major network refreshes, AI is embedded into our processes, transforming how we design, manage, and optimize our networks. We'll demonstrate that for Cisco IT, the answer to "Where do you use AI to manage your networks?" is truly: Everywhere. Our approach to AI in network engineering and operations spans three key areas. First, we've integrated common AI use-cases, similar to those found in any leading tech company, to enhance our general operational efficiency. Second, as "Customer Zero," we actively deploy and provide feedback on cutting-edge AI features embedded directly within Cisco products. For instance, AI-driven Radio Resource Management (RRM) has eliminated the need for 90% of our wireless site surveys, significantly cutting costs, and AI-powered alerts in Catalyst Center accelerate issue resolution. Finally, we've developed bespoke, network-specific AI solutions, such as using AI to automatically parse service provider maintenance notices to generate change records, analyzing packet captures, or enriching incident data, dramatically reducing manual effort and improving response times as well as using AI to avoid incident tickets entirely. This talk will provide an overview of these diverse AI applications, showcasing concrete examples of business value delivered. Attendees will gain insights into how Cisco IT is not only adopting but also shaping the future of network management through our "Customer Zero" role, actively testing and influencing emerging AI capabilities like AI Canvas, Meraki AI Agent, IOS-XE AI Agent, Catalyst Center, Meraki Dashboard, Cisco Cloud Control and others. Join us to discover how AI is fundamentally redefining network operations and driving unparalleled efficiency within Cisco.

### Why AI Needs Better Data: Building AI-Optimized Architectures for Splunk - WOSPAR-2016
Speakers: Ray Krug (NETSCOUT)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362860440001qOez
As organizations accelerate AI adoption, many are discovering a critical challenge: traditional telemetry, based on MELT (metrics, events, logs, and traces) and sampled data, often lacks the fidelity and context required to deliver optimal AI outcomes. This session explores the data gap that can limit AI effectiveness in IT observability and cybersecurity, and how NETSCOUT's data platform addresses this challenge by transforming packet data at the source into compact, information-dense AI-ready Smart Data. Participants will learn how this architecture delivers ground-truth, session-aware data that enriches MELT and enhances platforms such as Splunk ITSI and Splunk Enterprise Security. By providing real-time, continuous, zero-sampling, context-rich insights, this approach reduces noise, improves correlation accuracy, accelerates threat detection with fewer false positives, and speeds root-cause analysis - capabilities that traditional data sources alone cannot achieve. You'll leave this session with ideas to help you: Understand why NETSCOUT packet-derived Smart Data is AI-ready and how it enhances MELT data to improve AI-driven observability and cybersecurity outcomes Review real-world use cases with Splunk ITSI that demonstrate the value of NETSCOUT Smart Data integrated with Splunk Build MCP-ready data pipelines to improve AI accuracy, automation, and operational efficiency Familiarity with observability and security operations is recommended.

### Why Don't You Trust Me? Taking the Pain Out of Certificates - BRKXAR-2047
Speakers: Paul Giralt (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362794165001qvRp
When trust chains break, the results can lead to widespread, catastrophic outages. Yet, despite their critical role, certificates remain one of the most misunderstood aspects of network infrastructure - a reality that is becoming riskier as more protocols and features rely on them. In this session, we will explore how certificates serve as the foundation of modern security. We will start by demystifying the mechanics of certificates: how they work, how they are validated, why they fail, and the key terminology you need to know. Next, we will dive into how these concepts are applied inside Cisco technologies, from Cisco's Trustworthy Technologies that ensure the integrity of hardware and software to protect against attacks, to authenticating and protecting sensitive data using various protocols throughout the Cisco product portfolio. Finally, we will explore upcoming industry changes that necessitate using available mechanisms to automate the provisioning and management of certificates, ensuring chains of trust are never broken and preventing catastrophic outages.

### Why Network Monitoring Still Can't Troubleshoot Problems - WOSPAR-2031
Speakers: Tim Titus (PathSolutions)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362863891001qYGL
Why do network problems still take hours to troubleshoot - even with modern monitoring tools? The answer isn't more dashboards or more alerts. It's that traditional monitoring was never designed to explain what's actually wrong. In this session, we'll break down why monitoring tools consistently fall short when it comes to troubleshooting, and why engineers are still forced to manually piece together root cause from incomplete data. You'll learn how network devices already expose the signals needed to identify failures, and why most tools fail to use them effectively. We'll also explore a new approach to network operations - one that focuses on understanding and explaining problems, not just detecting them - and how AI can be used to move from symptoms to root cause.

### Why Secure Meetings Go Wrong When Zero Trust Still Relies on People - WOSPAR-2003
Speakers: Barry Pascolutti (Mividas Video Solutions)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362857427001qB7A
Secure video meetings require more than a secure platform. Even in Zero Trust environments, security gaps still appear in practice. Not because the technology is lacking, but because critical trust decisions are left to people and manual processes. In this session, participants will see how, even with the strong security foundations of modern collaboration platforms, security incidents related to video meetings can still occur. Despite secure platforms, access can sometimes be broader than intended, meeting ownership is not consistently verified, and responsibility is fragmented across tools and teams. You will leave this session with an insight into how Mividas, drawing on long-standing experience supporting security-sensitive organisations, can enforce Zero Trust principals across the entire video meeting lifecycle, reducing the reliance on human judgement. If you are responsible for planning, hosting or managing video meetings, or you have responsibility for the underlying infrastructure, this session is intended for you.

### Why Won't My Customers Answer The Phone? Rebuilding Trust in Outbound Calling - WOSPAR-2021
Speakers: Lauren Brockman (Bandwidth)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362861610001qdBu
Customer trust in outbound calling is at an all-time low. Many complex factors contribute to this issue - from number mislabeling and identity authentication gaps, to the risks of call spoofing. A comprehensive approach to the solution is essential. In this session, you'll learn how to move beyond simple call branding and build a robust strategy to ensure your customers are receiving and answering your mission-critical communications. You'll learn: Key factors driving declining call answer rates, including number reputation, call authentication, and the risks of call spoofing The benefits of a layered strategy combining mislabel remediation, branded calling, and pre-call authentication How comprehensive solutions offered from a singular provider at the carrier level can simplify management and improve effectiveness

### Why Your Al Pilot Isn't Becoming a Product - And How to Fix It - BRKAI-2001
Speakers: Puneet Kamal (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362673289001qgcK
Your AI demo works perfectly. But when it moves toward production, critical questions emerge: where will it fail, how will it behave at scale, and what is the underlying model actually doing? The gap between a proof-of-concept and a production system is far larger than most teams expect - and many organizations only discover it after deployment. This session explores the hidden infrastructure required to make enterprise AI systems reliable, observable, and governable. We will examine the architectural layers that sit beneath successful AI deployments, including enterprise data integration patterns that safely connect models to operational systems, evaluation frameworks that expose real failure modes, and governance strategies that maintain control and auditability across multiple model providers. We will also discuss how to engineer AI systems for observable scalability - so teams can understand where performance degrades, identify bottlenecks, and trace model decisions from user input to output. Through real production examples, this session provides a practical framework for building enterprise AI systems that move beyond impressive demos to trustworthy, production-ready platforms.

### Why the Best Leaders Don't Need to Be Technical - BRKLTF-1006
Speakers: Somer Shively (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362740616001q8cl
So often in technical organizations, promotion to leadership comes with demonstration of strong technical understanding. But being a people-leader has nothing to do with your technical know-how, it has everything to do with your ability to be empathetic, drive outcomes over outputs, and coach others. The best teams have leaders focused on the team culture instead of just the team deliverables. The best leaders are rarely technical experts. They are people experts! But how can you lead when you don't have all the technical details? In this session, you'll learn strategies for how to build a strong team culture, discuss tangible examples of how to empower your people's growth, and how it all ties to together to drive outcomes that will ensure your team delivers the right technical outputs without you having to know all of the details.

### Wi-Fi 7 Deployment and Migration: What You Need to Know - BRKEWN-2025
Speakers: Anand Gurumurthy (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362729936001q1je
Wi-Fi standards continuously evolve to cater to the needs of the latest applications and use cases. The next generation of Wi-Fi, i.e Wi-Fi 7, has created a lot of buzz, and is set to enhance wireless connectivity with unprecedented speeds, ultra-low latency and very high efficiency. As this next-generation standard emerges, organizations must be prepared to leverage its full potential. This session will explore the key advancements of Wi-Fi 7, that includes 4K QAM, 320 MHz channel width, Multi-Link Operation, Preamble Puncturing, Multi Resource Units, and how these features will impact network performance, scalability and security. We'll also discuss the necessary infrastructure updates, security updates and strategies to ensure a smooth transition. Join us to discover how Wi-Fi 7 can elevate your network capabilities and learn about the steps you need to take to stay ahead.

### Win The SOC with Port53 and Cisco - WOSPAR-2040
Speakers: Omar Zarabi (Port53 Technologies Inc.)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362865949001qycJ
As SecOps enters the age of AI and autonomous agents, traditional SOC architectures are breaking down. This session explores how forward-thinking organizations are building a resilient security backbone by combining Cisco XDR with Splunk to unify telemetry, automate response, and drive real-time decisioning. We'll examine the rise of agentic threats - AI-driven attacks that move faster and adapt dynamically - and why legacy SIEM or siloed tools can't keep pace. Learn how a modern XDR + data platform approach enables scalable detection, investigation, and response, while future-proofing your SOC for an era where both attackers and defenders are powered by intelligent agents.

### Work Rewired: Refreshes, Robots and the Rise of AI - ITLGEN-2012
Speakers: Daniele Hayes (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362816679001qwBA
Imagine an organization where technology works for you - devices always perform at their best, IT support is just a quick chat away, and the right tools are at your fingertips. In this session, we will share inside stories of how Cisco IT Digital Workplace is transforming the way employees connect, collaborate, and get work done through AI-driven innovation. Understand how AI is impacting the employee laptop experience, IT support, and discover new ways to deliver a user focused experience through AI enabled robots and universal applications. Join us for a practical look at how we are boosting team productivity, simplifying IT operations, and creating a digital workplace built for tomorrow's challenges.

### Work Smarter, Engage Better with Polling and Enterprise Video - BRKCOL-1009
Speakers: Amy Ryan (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362685758001qEOm
Organizations are under increasing pressure to modernize the employee experience, all while AI is changing the way teams connect, collaborate, and get work done. Join us to explore how Cisco's AI-powered engagement tools are helping organizations work smarter through intelligent assistants, real-time insights, interactive experiences, and streamlined asynchronous video workflows. See live demos of innovations like AI-Generated Vidcast and AI-Generated Polls, and discover how these tools can drive inclusivity, reduce manual effort, boost engagement, and unlock productivity across your organization.

### Your AI Agents Don't Talk to My AI Agents - Let's Fix That! - BRKETI-1009
Speakers: Sabitha Krishnamurthy (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362727697001qJqy
Everyone is building AI agents - but almost no one is building the Internet they need to run on. To unlock the full potential of this new digital workforce, organizations are moving from single agents to networks of specialized agents. But fragmentation is becoming the biggest blocker: incompatible frameworks, closed runtimes, and no shared way for agents to discover, trust, and communicate across environments. In this session, Outshift by Cisco introduces the Internet of Agents and the role of AGNTCY.org, a Linux Foundation open-source project building the foundational infrastructure for agent-to-agent collaboration at scale. We'll cover the four core AGNTCY technologies - agent directory, agent identity, SLIM messaging, and observability - and demonstrate practical multi-agent use cases with demo and shared code.

### Your NetDevOps Toolbox: Automating Routing Changes from Templates to Pipelines - BRKENT-2242
Speakers: Francois Caen (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362724998001qMIv
Are you still making large-scale routing config changes by hand, copy-pasting templates from Notepad into eleventeen SSH terminals? Did you take your first steps with Python or Ansible, and then become overwhelmed by Jinja2 or NETCONF / YANG? You need a NetDevOps Toolbox! In this session, you will learn how to assemble open tools and standards to create your own Toolbox. Through live demos using VRF provisioning as our running example, you'll see routing changes deployed through each stage of a real automation pipeline. You'll leave with reference code and a clear roadmap for building automation that can deploy config changes safely at scale.

### Your Network is Smarter Than You Think: Mastering AgenticOps with AI Assistant - PSOENT-1035
Speakers: George Mokdessi (Cisco), Priya Ram (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362832739001qfkW
Managing a network shouldn't feel like a constant game of catch-up. Whether you're battling misconfigurations, application degradation, or performance bottlenecks, the manual grind is real. In this session, we'll explore how the AI Assistant is changing the game for network practitioners by turning reactive firefighting into proactive problem-solving. You'll see how agentic workflows automate repetitive tasks, while deep reasoning mode helps you pinpoint complex root causes in seconds. We'll show you how to cut through the noise, letting you focus on high-impact projects that move the needle. Join us to make your daily operations faster, smarter, and more efficient.

### Your Network, Now Conversational: Unlocking RADKit with the Official MCP Server - CISCOU-2385
Speakers: Alfonso Sandoval Rosas (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362803792001qfPA
What if you could talk to your RADKit-onboarded network instead of wrestling with CLI commands? This session introduces the official MCP server for Cisco RADKit, which enables AI assistants to interact directly with your infrastructure while unleashing the full capabilities of the RADKit SDK. By natively leveraging SDK features such as filtering, concurrency, and device grouping, the MCP server performs network reads and writes efficiently at scale, making the most of your RADKit deployment. The MCP server is also highly configurable and extensible. You will learn how to expand its behavior through customizable Python profiles, allowing you to implement additional logic, such as super strict guardrails, for example. In addition, customizable prompts allow you to shape how AI assistants reason about and interact with your infrastructure. Through live demos, we will showcase how to deploy and customize the MCP server, integrate it with chat platforms, and interact with a RADKit-enabled network conversationally. The result is a powerful and flexible foundation for safe, efficient, AI-driven network operations.

### Zero Day, Zero Trust Troubleshooting with Secure Access: End-to-End Visibility from Client to Private App - BRKSEC-2051
Speakers: Diego Barrantes Rivera (Cisco), Fuad Al Asouli (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362770280001qE66
Troubleshooting in Secure Access Zero Trust architecture demands full visibility across every access point, starting from the endpoint to the private application or Internet resource. This session focuses on Cisco Secure Access and provides an end-to-end, real-world troubleshooting journey that starts with the Secure Client Modules including ZTNA, SWG, Core VPN, passes through the Secure Access Cloud, and reaches the Private Application environment. Whether it's authentication failure, policy mismatch, tunnel issues, DNS resolution, or application reachability, this session shows how to diagnose and resolve issues across all layers of the Secure Access framework. Attendees will walk through real scenarios, analyze logs and telemetry, and use tools like Security Cloud Control, Client Debug, and application probes to pinpoint root causes and validate ZTNA policy behavior. If you're deploying or supporting Secure Access in production, this is the session that equips you with the tools, knowledge, and confidence to tackle Zero Day issues on Day 1 and beyond.

### Zero Trust Data Center Segmentation: VxLAN fabric with Group Policy Option (GPO) - WOSPAR-2027
Speakers: Scott Jester (SHI International), Daniel Vargas (SHI International)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362862956001qq9e
This session highlights a data center use case for NDFC?managed VXLAN fabric with Group Policy Option (GPO). This could replace traditional firewall?based segmentation not requiring stateful inspection but beyond VRFs. Leveraging GPO, we will discuss how it establishes policy-driven segmentation strategy for data center. We will share key design choices, challenges, and lessons to make the transition.

### Zero Trust Provision and AVoIP Accessories of Collaboration Devices - BRKCOL-2165
Speakers: Paulo Jorge Correia (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362689105001qsvK
As collaboration devices, such as IP phones, video endpoints, and AVoIP accessories, proliferate across enterprise networks, secure and efficient provisioning becomes paramount. We will explore key considerations for AVoIP accessories and the types of topologies supported by Cisco. In the era of cloud-first strategies and hybrid work, organizations must deploy these devices rapidly, while rigorously enforcing security to prevent unauthorized access and data breaches. This session delves into best practices for implementing Zero Trust principles when provisioning collaboration devices, emphasizing automated, policy-driven workflows that minimize administrative overhead. We will review practical integrations with network access control solutions like Cisco ISE and discuss real-world scenarios where Zero Trust architectures protect against evolving threats. Attendees will gain actionable insights on strengthening device security, streamlining deployment, and safeguarding their collaboration environments in today's dynamic workplace.

### Zero Trust for Agents - PSOSEC-2344
Speakers: Tsailing Merrem (Cisco), Prabhu Barathi (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362840881001q8vP
The "Agentic Era" is here, and while AI agents promise unprecedented productivity, they introduce a new, complex class of risk. As these agents move from experimental pilots to production, the critical question for security leaders is no longer just "who has access," but "what are these agents empowered to do?" Without consistent enforcement and fine-grained access control, an autonomous agent can inadvertently become the "ultimate insider," accessing restricted data or taking actions far beyond its intended scope. In this session, we explore why traditional security controls must evolve to address the unique challenges of the agentic workforce. We will demonstrate how we are expanding our existing Zero Trust access architecture to enforce strict access control, secure your data, and enable you to confidently operationalize an agentic workforce. Join us to discover how to bridge the trust gap between pilot and production by extending your current Zero Trust investments.

### Zero Trust for Tomorrow: Stopping AI & Identity Attacks - BRKSEC-2185
Speakers: Pascale Delaunay (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362778278001qfxe
Are you prepared for the next generation of threats? In 2026 and beyond, sophisticated AI-powered attacks will relentlessly target identity, exploiting the seams between your multicloud environments and distributed workforce. Fragmented point products can't keep pace. This session cuts through the noise to deliver a prescriptive, future-proof blueprint for defending against these evolving threats. Discover how Cisco's integrated platform - featuring Universal Zero Trust Access (UZTNA), Hybrid Mesh Firewall, and Security Operations (SOC) powered by Cisco XDR. Learn how to future-proof identity, secure the multicloud frontier and automate response beyond human scale to stop AI-driven attacks. Don't just react to 2026's threats - anticipate and neutralize them. Join me to learn how Cisco's unified approach helps you be better prepared and stay prepared!

### eBPF-Powered Runtime Security and Exposure Management in Splunk - BRKOBS-1007
Speakers: Colin Gibbens (Cisco), Natalia Ivanko (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1780362750953001qdbT
Discover how Splunk and Cisco Isovalent improve threat detection, incident investigation, and common vulnerabilities and exposures (CVE) mitigation workflows with richer data, deeper context, stronger correlation, and AI-driven insights with low friction and memory usage. This session showcases how to use kernel-level runtime and network security data in Splunk during threat hunting and incident response to protect critical cloud infrastructure against known and unknown vulnerabilities before they are exploited. You'll leave this session understanding how eBPF-based security observability with Splunk and Isovalent can strengthen your SecOps workflows and help protect your infrastructure from exploitation. Intermediate experience with security operations and cloud infrastructure is recommended.
